\item \textbf{Streaming:} \texttt{stream} flag with an \texttt{on\_\allowbreak{}token} callback and \texttt{stream\_\allowbreak{}idle\_\allowbreak{}timeout}; streaming is silently downgraded when no callback is wired; the agent opens a \texttt{StreamContext} per step. \texttt{sdk/\allowbreak{}llm/\allowbreak{}llm.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L505-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L512}, \texttt{L393-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L400}, \texttt{L1789-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1800}; \texttt{sdk/\allowbreak{}agent/\allowbreak{}agent.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L694-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L705}
\end{itemize}

\subsubsection*{Output}
\begin{itemize}
\item \textbf{Tool set:} \texttt{openhands-\allowbreak{}tools} (terminal, file\_editor, grep, glob, browser\_use, task\_tracker, delegate, planning\_file\_editor, apply\_patch) plus MCP and skill tools; each \texttt{ToolDefinition} pairs an action schema with a \texttt{ToolExecutor} and declares resources for locking. \texttt{tools/\allowbreak{}terminal/\allowbreak{}definition.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L185-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L199}; \texttt{sdk/\allowbreak{}tool/\allowbreak{}tool.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L283-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L328}, \texttt{L347-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L400}, \texttt{L529-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L545}
\item \textbf{Dispatch:} \texttt{\_\allowbreak{}handle\_\allowbreak{}tool\_\allowbreak{}calls} converts each tool call into an \texttt{ActionEvent} (thought and reasoning attached to the first), then \texttt{\_\allowbreak{}ActionBatch.\allowbreak{}prepare} truncates the batch at the first FinishAction, partitions hook-blocked actions and executes the rest. \texttt{sdk/\allowbreak{}agent/\allowbreak{}response\_\allowbreak{}dispatch.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L154-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L201}; \texttt{sdk/\allowbreak{}agent/\allowbreak{}agent.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L226-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L306}, \texttt{L628-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L656}
\item \textbf{Parallelism:} \texttt{ParallelToolExecutor} runs a batch sequentially by default (\texttt{tool\_\allowbreak{}concurrency\_\allowbreak{}limit} 1) or in a \texttt{ThreadPoolExecutor} with per-resource locks when raised. \texttt{sdk/\allowbreak{}agent/\allowbreak{}parallel\_\allowbreak{}executor.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L51-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L126}; \texttt{sdk/\allowbreak{}agent/\allowbreak{}base.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L314-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L322}; \texttt{sdk/\allowbreak{}agent/\allowbreak{}agent.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L473-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L475}
\item \textbf{Sandbox:} tools run in the Agent Server's workspace, which can be the local machine or an ephemeral Docker/Kubernetes workspace; Canvas picks the backend (local, Docker, VM, cloud). \texttt{software-\allowbreak{}agent-\allowbreak{}sdk/\allowbreak{}README.\allowbreak{}md:\allowbreak{}L37-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L38}; \texttt{README.\allowbreak{}md:\allowbreak{}L35-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L41}; \texttt{srv/\allowbreak{}docker\_\allowbreak{}runtime/\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}init\_\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1}; \texttt{srv/\allowbreak{}docker\_\allowbreak{}runtime/\allowbreak{}provisioning.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L20}
\item \textbf{Presentation:} events are persisted then published to subscribers (visualizer prepended for terminal rendering); Canvas renders them from the WebSocket with per-tool visualizers and a confirmation prompt. \texttt{local\_\allowbreak{}conversation.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L432-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L470}; \texttt{ui/\allowbreak{}components/\allowbreak{}features/\allowbreak{}chat/\allowbreak{}tool-\allowbreak{}visualizers/\allowbreak{}index.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L1-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L20}
\end{itemize}

\subsubsection*{Production concerns}
\begin{itemize}
\item \textbf{Persistence:} \texttt{ConversationState} keeps events in an \texttt{EventLog} on a \texttt{FileStore} (one JSON file per event under a lock with a length marker) and autosaves \texttt{base\_\allowbreak{}state.\allowbreak{}json}; a conversation resumes from \texttt{persistence\_\allowbreak{}dir} and can be forked (context plus control flow state). \texttt{sdk/\allowbreak{}conversation/\allowbreak{}event\_\allowbreak{}store.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L34-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L60}, \texttt{L188-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L244}; \texttt{sdk/\allowbreak{}conversation/\allowbreak{}state.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L233-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L234}, \texttt{L429-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L432}; \texttt{local\_\allowbreak{}conversation.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L353-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L374}, \texttt{L409-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L412}, \texttt{L804-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L830}
\item \textbf{Safety \& security:} security analyzers (model-reported \texttt{security\_\allowbreak{}risk}, defense-in-depth policy rails, Grayswan, ToolShield) feed confirmation policies (control flow); PreToolUse hooks block actions; \texttt{SecretRegistry} masks secrets in emitted events; sub-agent scope narrows tools; the Agent Server requires a session API key. \texttt{sdk/\allowbreak{}security/\allowbreak{}llm\_\allowbreak{}analyzer.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L10-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L28}; \texttt{sdk/\allowbreak{}security/\allowbreak{}analyzer.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L29-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L60}; \texttt{sdk/\allowbreak{}security/\allowbreak{}defense\_\allowbreak{}in\_\allowbreak{}depth/\allowbreak{}policy\_\allowbreak{}rails.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L40}; \texttt{sdk/\allowbreak{}subagent/\allowbreak{}scope.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L38-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L60}; \texttt{sdk/\allowbreak{}conversation/\allowbreak{}secret\_\allowbreak{}registry.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L24-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L75}; \texttt{srv/\allowbreak{}credential\_\allowbreak{}binding.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L30}
\item \textbf{Observability:} \texttt{Telemetry.\allowbreak{}on\_\allowbreak{}response} records latency, cost and tokens into \texttt{Metrics}, optionally logs each LLM call as JSON and annotates a span; Laminar \texttt{observe} spans wrap steps and tool executions; Canvas reports usage in a metrics modal. \texttt{sdk/\allowbreak{}llm/\allowbreak{}utils/\allowbreak{}telemetry.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L190-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L242}, \texttt{L401-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L470}; \texttt{sdk/\allowbreak{}observability/\allowbreak{}laminar.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L155-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L234}, \texttt{L235-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L260}; \texttt{sdk/\allowbreak{}agent/\allowbreak{}agent.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1527-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1537}
\item \textbf{Resilience:} retries and fallback on the model call; condensation on overflow; stuck nudge; iteration and budget caps; event-log resume; \texttt{ConversationRunError} carries the persistence dir; Canvas WebSocket reconnects. \texttt{local\_\allowbreak{}conversation.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L2064-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L2080}; \texttt{sdk/\allowbreak{}agent/\allowbreak{}agent.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L826-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L876}; \texttt{ui/\allowbreak{}hooks/\allowbreak{}use-\allowbreak{}websocket.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L118-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L145}
\item \textbf{Performance:} the view is maintained incrementally to avoid O(n) rebuilds per step; prompt caching; parallel tool execution; streaming; runtime metadata cached with a 1 h TTL. \texttt{sdk/\allowbreak{}agent/\allowbreak{}utils.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L592-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L597}; \texttt{sdk/\allowbreak{}llm/\allowbreak{}llm.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1780-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1786}; \texttt{sdk/\allowbreak{}agent/\allowbreak{}parallel\_\allowbreak{}executor.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L110-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L126}
\item \textbf{Evaluation:} SDK unit tests under \texttt{tests/\allowbreak{}sdk} (agent, conversation, llm, context, hooks) and behavior integration tests run by \texttt{run\_\allowbreak{}infer.\allowbreak{}py}; Canvas has vitest suites and Playwright mock-LLM and live end-to-end configs. \texttt{sdkt/\allowbreak{}integration/\allowbreak{}run\_\allowbreak{}infer.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L125-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L321}; \texttt{sdkt/\allowbreak{}sdk/\allowbreak{}agent/\allowbreak{}test\_\allowbreak{}agent\_\allowbreak{}context\_\allowbreak{}window\_\allowbreak{}condensation.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L30}; \texttt{playwright.\allowbreak{}mock-\allowbreak{}llm.\allowbreak{}config.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L88-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L100}
\end{itemize}

\subsubsection*{Hard to place}
\begin{itemize}
\item \textbf{The repo is a shell:} at this commit OpenHands/OpenHands holds no loop, model client or tools; AGENTS.md assigns those to software-agent-sdk, and most of Canvas is UI framework and transport (out of scope), yet Canvas keeps control-flow pieces (plan-mode routing, child-conversation launch, confirmation answers, router-at-start). The harness is split across three repos and the primitives cross repo boundaries. \texttt{AGENTS.\allowbreak{}md:\allowbreak{}L30-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L37}; \texttt{ui/\allowbreak{}api/\allowbreak{}conversation-\allowbreak{}service/\allowbreak{}agent-\allowbreak{}server-\allowbreak{}conversation-\allowbreak{}service.\allowbreak{}api.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L456-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L470}
\item \textbf{ACP agents:} \texttt{ACPAgent} drives a third-party agent (Claude Code, Codex, ...) through its own \texttt{step}; OpenHands' loop, persistence and confirmation then wrap another complete harness, which the multi-agent rule treats as an inner harness, although here it is a peer at depth 0 rather than a delegate. \texttt{sdk/\allowbreak{}agent/\allowbreak{}base.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1077-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1080}; \texttt{sdk/\allowbreak{}agent/\allowbreak{}acp\_\allowbreak{}agent.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1718-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1730}, \texttt{L3952-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L3960}
\item \textbf{Model-predicted risk:} \texttt{add\_\allowbreak{}security\_\allowbreak{}risk\_\allowbreak{}prediction=True} adds a \texttt{security\_\allowbreak{}risk} field to every tool schema, so the approval gate (control flow) is fed by the model's own output rather than by the harness; by rule it is output whose effect is control flow. \texttt{sdk/\allowbreak{}agent/\allowbreak{}agent.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L790-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L795}; \texttt{sdk/\allowbreak{}security/\allowbreak{}llm\_\allowbreak{}analyzer.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L20-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L28}
\item \textbf{Condenser as loop step:} when the condenser returns a \texttt{Condensation}, the step emits it and returns without a model call, so a context operation spends one loop iteration; the stop-hook path likewise fabricates a user message to keep the loop going. \texttt{sdk/\allowbreak{}agent/\allowbreak{}agent.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L748-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L756}; \texttt{local\_\allowbreak{}conversation.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1963-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1991}
\end{itemize}

\subsubsection*{Summary}
\begin{longtable}{@{}p{0.28\linewidth}p{0.66\linewidth}@{}}
\toprule \textbf{Primitive} & \textbf{Design decision} \\ \midrule \endhead
Input & Canvas posts user events to an Agent Server; SDK wraps them as MessageEvents; tool results are ObservationEvents \\
Context & Section-registry system prompt with AGENTS.md skills; event view condensed by LLM summary on token or size limit \\
Model interface & litellm chat or Responses API; 5 exponential retries; profile fallbacks; optional token streaming \\
Output & Typed ToolDefinitions with executors in a local or Docker workspace; batch truncated at Finish; optional parallelism \\
Control flow & Status-machine loop with iteration and budget caps, stuck detector, risk-based confirmation, hooks, threaded delegation \\
\bottomrule \end{longtable}


\subsection{Aider}

\textbf{Source:} https://github.com/Aider-AI/aider $\cdot$ \textbf{Version:} commit 5dc9490bb35f9729ef2c95d00a19ccd30c26339c (2026-05-22) $\cdot$ \textbf{Language:} Python
\textbf{Path conventions:} \texttt{a/\allowbreak{}} = \texttt{aider/\allowbreak{}}; \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}} = \texttt{aider/\allowbreak{}coders/\allowbreak{}}; \texttt{b/\allowbreak{}} = \texttt{benchmark/\allowbreak{}}; \texttt{t/\allowbreak{}} = \texttt{tests/\allowbreak{}basic/\allowbreak{}}. All paths relative to the repo root.
\textbf{What it is:} Aider is a terminal pair-programming tool by Paul Gauthier (Aider-AI) that edits files in a local git repository and commits each change. The model is reached through LiteLLM (\texttt{litellm.\allowbreak{}completion}) over HTTPS with streaming. There is no native tool calling on the main path: the model writes edits in a text "edit format" (SEARCH/REPLACE blocks, unified diffs, whole files) that the harness parses and applies, and a bounded "reflection" loop feeds parse, lint, and test failures back as the next user message.

\subsubsection*{Control flow}
\begin{itemize}
\item \textbf{Outer REPL loop (\texttt{Coder.\allowbreak{}run}):} \texttt{while True} reads a line, calls \texttt{run\_\allowbreak{}one}, and returns on EOF; \texttt{main} wraps it in a loop that catches \texttt{SwitchCoder} and rebuilds the coder when the user changes mode or model. \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}base\_\allowbreak{}coder.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L876-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L892}; \texttt{a/\allowbreak{}main.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1159-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1178}
\item \textbf{Reflection loop and stop rule (\texttt{run\_\allowbreak{}one}):} \texttt{while message:\allowbreak{}} sends one request; the loop goes again only when \texttt{reflected\_\allowbreak{}message} was set, and stops with a warning once \texttt{num\_\allowbreak{}reflections >= max\_\allowbreak{}reflections} (class default 3). \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}base\_\allowbreak{}coder.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L924-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L944}, \texttt{L101}
\item \textbf{Go-again triggers:} after a reply, \texttt{send\_\allowbreak{}message} sets \texttt{reflected\_\allowbreak{}message} on newly mentioned files, on an edit-format \texttt{ValueError} from \texttt{apply\_\allowbreak{}updates}, on lint errors (after \texttt{confirm\_\allowbreak{}ask}), and on test errors (after \texttt{confirm\_\allowbreak{}ask}), each returning early so \texttt{run\_\allowbreak{}one} re-sends. \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}base\_\allowbreak{}coder.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1561-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1566}, \texttt{L1585-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1623}, \texttt{L2296-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L2336}
\item \textbf{Approval gates the harness consumes:} \texttt{confirm\_\allowbreak{}ask} gates adding mentioned files, creating or editing files not yet in chat, running model-suggested shell commands (explicit yes required), adding command output, and attempting lint/test fixes; "don't ask again" and group "all/skip" answers are remembered per question. \texttt{a/\allowbreak{}io.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L807-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L860}; \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}base\_\allowbreak{}coder.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L2191-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L2242}, \texttt{L2450-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L2485}
\item \textbf{Architect delegation (depth 1):} \texttt{ArchitectCoder.\allowbreak{}reply\_\allowbreak{}completed} optionally asks "Edit the files?", builds a second \texttt{Coder} on \texttt{editor\_\allowbreak{}model} with the editor edit format and no repo map, runs the architect's reply as that coder's message, then folds cost and commit hashes back into the parent. \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}architect\_\allowbreak{}coder.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L11-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L48}; \texttt{a/\allowbreak{}models.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L625-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L645}
\item \textbf{Model routing keyed to the work:} the \texttt{weak\_\allowbreak{}model} writes commit messages and history summaries and the \texttt{editor\_\allowbreak{}model} performs the architect's edit step; both come from model settings, not from failures. \texttt{a/\allowbreak{}models.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L603-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L623}; \texttt{a/\allowbreak{}repo.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L342-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L364}; \texttt{a/\allowbreak{}history.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L7-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L13}
\end{itemize}

\subsubsection*{Input}
\begin{itemize}
\item \textbf{Terminal input:} \texttt{get\_\allowbreak{}input} shows files in chat and the mode in a prompt\_toolkit prompt; \texttt{preproc\_\allowbreak{}user\_\allowbreak{}input} dispatches \texttt{/\allowbreak{}commands}, scans the text for file mentions, and offers to scrape URLs into the message. \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}base\_\allowbreak{}coder.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L898-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L922}, \texttt{L964-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L984}; \texttt{a/\allowbreak{}io.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L523-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L545}
\item \textbf{Scripted input:} \texttt{-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}message} and \texttt{-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}message-\allowbreak{}file} call \texttt{coder.\allowbreak{}run(\allowbreak{}with\_\allowbreak{}message=.\allowbreak{}.\allowbreak{}.\allowbreak{})}, which sends one message and returns the reply text. \texttt{a/\allowbreak{}main.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1126-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1140}; \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}base\_\allowbreak{}coder.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L876-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L881}
\item \textbf{File-watcher input ("AI comments"):} \texttt{FileWatcher.\allowbreak{}process\_\allowbreak{}changes} turns \texttt{AI!} or \texttt{AI?} comments found in changed files into a request, adding the files to chat and including tree-sitter context around each comment. \texttt{a/\allowbreak{}watch.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L181-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L283}
\item \textbf{Results re-enter as text:} approved shell-command output, \texttt{/\allowbreak{}run} and \texttt{/\allowbreak{}test} output, lint and test errors, and the edit-format failure report are either appended as \texttt{user}/\texttt{assistant} message pairs or become the next \texttt{reflected\_\allowbreak{}message}. \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}base\_\allowbreak{}coder.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1609-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1623}; \texttt{a/\allowbreak{}commands.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1013-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1060}; \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}editblock\_\allowbreak{}coder.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L79-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L124}
\end{itemize}

\subsubsection*{Context}
\begin{itemize}
\item \textbf{System prompt and chunk order:} \texttt{fmt\_\allowbreak{}system\_\allowbreak{}prompt} fills the edit-format prompt with fence choice, platform and shell info, reply language, and lazy/overeager reminders; \texttt{format\_\allowbreak{}chat\_\allowbreak{}chunks} assembles ordered chunks: system, examples, read-only files, repo map, summarized history, chat files, current messages, and a reminder placed as system or appended to the last user message if it fits. \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}base\_\allowbreak{}coder.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1174-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1224}, \texttt{L1226-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1331}; \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}chat\_\allowbreak{}chunks.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L16-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L26}
\item \textbf{Repo map:} \texttt{RepoMap} extracts definitions and references with tree-sitter (cached by mtime in a \texttt{diskcache} SQLite directory), ranks files with NetworkX PageRank personalized toward chat files and mentioned identifiers, and binary-searches the tag count so the rendered tree fits \texttt{map\_\allowbreak{}tokens} (default 1024, times 8 when no files are in chat). \texttt{a/\allowbreak{}repomap.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L103-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L168}, \texttt{L217-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L265}, \texttt{L470-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L529}, \texttt{L629-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L709}; \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}base\_\allowbreak{}coder.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L709-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L748}
\item \textbf{History compaction:} \texttt{ChatSummary.\allowbreak{}summarize\_\allowbreak{}real} keeps a tail under half the budget, summarizes the head with the weak model, and recurses to depth 3; \texttt{summarize\_\allowbreak{}start} runs it in a background thread whenever \texttt{done\_\allowbreak{}messages} exceeds \texttt{max\_\allowbreak{}chat\_\allowbreak{}history\_\allowbreak{}tokens}, and \texttt{format\_\allowbreak{}chat\_\allowbreak{}chunks} joins the thread before use. \texttt{a/\allowbreak{}history.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L33-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L130}; \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}base\_\allowbreak{}coder.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1002-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1046}
\item \textbf{Window check:} \texttt{check\_\allowbreak{}tokens} compares the estimated prompt to \texttt{max\_\allowbreak{}input\_\allowbreak{}tokens} and asks before sending; a \texttt{ContextWindowExceededError} sets \texttt{exhausted} and prints a report instead of retrying. \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}base\_\allowbreak{}coder.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1396-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1417}, \texttt{L1536-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1546}
\item \textbf{Prompt caching:} with \texttt{-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}cache-\allowbreak{}prompts}, \texttt{add\_\allowbreak{}cache\_\allowbreak{}control\_\allowbreak{}headers} marks the system/examples, repo-map, and chat-files chunks \texttt{cache\_\allowbreak{}control:\allowbreak{} ephemeral}, and \texttt{warm\_\allowbreak{}cache} re-sends the cacheable prefix with \texttt{max\_\allowbreak{}tokens=1} every \textasciitilde{}5 minutes to keep it warm. \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}chat\_\allowbreak{}chunks.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L28-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L56}; \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}base\_\allowbreak{}coder.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1333-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1394}
\end{itemize}

\subsubsection*{Model interface}
\begin{itemize}
\item \textbf{Client:} \texttt{Model.\allowbreak{}send\_\allowbreak{}completion} builds the request (temperature, an optional single forced \texttt{tools} function, \texttt{extra\_\allowbreak{}params}, ollama \texttt{num\_\allowbreak{}ctx}, a 600 s timeout) and calls \texttt{litellm.\allowbreak{}completion}; litellm is imported lazily with \texttt{drop\_\allowbreak{}params = True}. \texttt{a/\allowbreak{}models.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L985-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1037}, \texttt{L28}; \texttt{a/\allowbreak{}llm.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L21-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L45}
\item \textbf{Streaming:} \texttt{show\_\allowbreak{}send\_\allowbreak{}output\_\allowbreak{}stream} iterates chunks, accumulates content, reasoning, and function-call deltas, and raises \texttt{FinishReasonLength} when \texttt{finish\_\allowbreak{}reason == "length"}. \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}base\_\allowbreak{}coder.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1900-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1975}
\item \textbf{Retries and prefill continuation:} \texttt{send\_\allowbreak{}message} loops with exponential backoff from 0.125 s until \texttt{RETRY\_\allowbreak{}TIMEOUT = 60} for litellm exceptions whose table entry says \texttt{retry=True}; on \texttt{FinishReasonLength} it appends the partial reply as an assistant prefill and re-sends when the model supports it. \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}base\_\allowbreak{}coder.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1449-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1513}; \texttt{a/\allowbreak{}exceptions.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L13-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L57}; \texttt{a/\allowbreak{}models.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L26}
\item \textbf{Summarizer and commit-message fallback:} \texttt{ChatSummary} and \texttt{get\_\allowbreak{}commit\_\allowbreak{}message} try \texttt{[weak\_\allowbreak{}model, main\_\allowbreak{}model]} in order through \texttt{simple\_\allowbreak{}send\_\allowbreak{}with\_\allowbreak{}retries}, which has its own backoff loop. \texttt{a/\allowbreak{}history.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L98-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L130}; \texttt{a/\allowbreak{}models.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1039-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1084}; \texttt{a/\allowbreak{}repo.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L342-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L364}
\end{itemize}

\subsubsection*{Output}
\begin{itemize}
\item \textbf{Edit formats as the tool set:} each \texttt{Coder} subclass pairs a prompt with a parser and applier: \texttt{EditBlockCoder.\allowbreak{}get\_\allowbreak{}edits} parses SEARCH/REPLACE blocks and fenced shell blocks, \texttt{UnifiedDiffCoder} parses hunks, \texttt{WholeFileCoder} whole files, \texttt{PatchCoder} the apply-patch format. \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}editblock\_\allowbreak{}coder.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L15-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L41}, \texttt{L439-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L470}; \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}udiff\_\allowbreak{}coder.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L49-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L94}; \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}init\_\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L34}
\item \textbf{Fuzzy application and failure report:} \texttt{replace\_\allowbreak{}most\_\allowbreak{}similar\_\allowbreak{}chunk} tries exact, whitespace-insensitive, and \texttt{.\allowbreak{}.\allowbreak{}.\allowbreak{}} elision matches; failed blocks produce a \texttt{ValueError} with the block, "did you mean" lines, and instructions to resend only the failed blocks. \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}editblock\_\allowbreak{}coder.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L157-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L189}, \texttt{L79-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L124}
\item \textbf{Apply pipeline:} \texttt{apply\_\allowbreak{}updates} runs parse, dry run, \texttt{prepare\_\allowbreak{}to\_\allowbreak{}edit} (per-file permission and dirty commit), and apply; edited files are auto-committed with a generated commit message and, if enabled, linted and tested. \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}base\_\allowbreak{}coder.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L2269-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L2336}, \texttt{L2375-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L2395}, \texttt{L1681-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1696}; \texttt{a/\allowbreak{}repo.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L131-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L140}
\item \textbf{Shell execution:} model-suggested commands run after approval through \texttt{run\_\allowbreak{}cmd} (pexpect interactive spawn on a POSIX TTY, otherwise \texttt{subprocess.\allowbreak{}Popen}) in the repo root with no sandbox; output is added to the chat only after a second confirmation. \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}base\_\allowbreak{}coder.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L2434-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L2485}; \texttt{a/\allowbreak{}run\_\allowbreak{}cmd.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L11-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L24}, \texttt{L42-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L62}, \texttt{L89-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L121}
\item \textbf{Rendering:} streamed markdown is drawn live through \texttt{MarkdownStream}, reasoning tags are reformatted, and \texttt{/\allowbreak{}diff} shows the auto-commit diff. \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}base\_\allowbreak{}coder.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1977-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1992}; \texttt{a/\allowbreak{}io.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1014-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1042}
\end{itemize}

\subsubsection*{Production concerns}
\begin{itemize}
\item \textbf{Persistence:} chat history is appended to \texttt{.\allowbreak{}aider.\allowbreak{}chat.\allowbreak{}history.\allowbreak{}md} and reloaded with \texttt{-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}restore-\allowbreak{}chat-\allowbreak{}history} (context); git commits are the durable artifact and \texttt{/\allowbreak{}undo} reverts aider's own commits (output); repo-map tags live in \texttt{.\allowbreak{}aider.\allowbreak{}tags.\allowbreak{}cache.\allowbreak{}v4} (context). \texttt{a/\allowbreak{}io.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1117-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1137}; \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}base\_\allowbreak{}coder.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L519-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L523}; \texttt{a/\allowbreak{}commands.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L553-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L575}; \texttt{a/\allowbreak{}repomap.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L35-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L43}
\item \textbf{Safety \& security:} no sandbox; safety is the \texttt{confirm\_\allowbreak{}ask} gates (control flow), gitignore and aiderignore checks before editing, and \texttt{-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}dry-\allowbreak{}run} (output). \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}base\_\allowbreak{}coder.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L2191-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L2242}, \texttt{L2450-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L2460}; \texttt{a/\allowbreak{}repo.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L523-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L566}
\item \textbf{Observability:} per-message token and cost report from \texttt{completion.\allowbreak{}usage} including cache read/write tokens; \texttt{-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}llm-\allowbreak{}history-\allowbreak{}file} logs full prompts and responses; opt-in analytics send \texttt{message\_\allowbreak{}send} and \texttt{exit} events with redacted model names to PostHog or Mixpanel. \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}base\_\allowbreak{}coder.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1994-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L2126}; \texttt{a/\allowbreak{}io.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L754-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L766}; \texttt{a/\allowbreak{}analytics.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L213-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L253}
\item \textbf{Resilience:} backoff retries on transient provider errors (model interface), prefill continuation on output-length cutoff (model interface), the \texttt{exhausted} report on context overflow (context), and Ctrl-C handling that records the interruption in the transcript. \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}base\_\allowbreak{}coder.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1456-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1513}, \texttt{L1575-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1583}, \texttt{L1628-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1680}
\item \textbf{Performance:} streaming, prompt-cache headers plus keepalive pings, background summarization thread, repo-map memoization keyed by chat files and mentions, and lazy litellm import. \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}base\_\allowbreak{}coder.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1340-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1394}; \texttt{a/\allowbreak{}repomap.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L576-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L627}; \texttt{a/\allowbreak{}llm.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L21-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L45}
\item \textbf{Evaluation:} \texttt{benchmark/\allowbreak{}benchmark.\allowbreak{}py} runs Exercism and SWE-bench tasks in Docker per model and edit format; unit tests cover the coder loop, edit-block parsing, and the repo map. \texttt{b/\allowbreak{}benchmark.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L679-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L700}; \texttt{t/\allowbreak{}test\_\allowbreak{}editblock.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L30}; \texttt{t/\allowbreak{}test\_\allowbreak{}repomap.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L30}; \texttt{t/\allowbreak{}test\_\allowbreak{}coder.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L30}
\end{itemize}

\subsubsection*{Hard to place}
\begin{itemize}
\item \textbf{Edit formats:} one class per format carries both the prompt (what the model is shown) and the parser/applier (what is done with the reply); by function the prompt text is context and \texttt{get\_\allowbreak{}edits}/\texttt{apply\_\allowbreak{}edits} are output, so the rules split a single component. \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}editblock\_\allowbreak{}coder.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L15-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L41}; \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}editblock\_\allowbreak{}prompts.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L8-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L28}
\item \textbf{File mentions in the reply:} a model mention of a file name has the effect of a tool call (add file to context) without any tool; the rules resolve it as a context effect behind a control-flow approval gate, but the capture is text scanning, not a tool-call format. \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}base\_\allowbreak{}coder.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1714-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1782}, \texttt{L1561-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1566}
\item \textbf{Cache warming:} a background thread issues real model requests (\texttt{max\_\allowbreak{}tokens=1}) whose response is discarded; it is a model-interface call made purely as a performance measure for context, with no input, output, or control-flow role. \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}base\_\allowbreak{}coder.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1340-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1394}
\end{itemize}

\subsubsection*{Summary}
\begin{longtable}{@{}p{0.28\linewidth}p{0.66\linewidth}@{}}
\toprule \textbf{Primitive} & \textbf{Design decision} \\ \midrule \endhead
Input & Terminal REPL, scripted message, or AI! file comments; results re-enter as plain text message pairs \\
Context & Ordered chunks: system, examples, PageRank repo map, files, weak-model summarized history; optional cache headers \\
Model interface & LiteLLM completion with streaming, exponential backoff to 60 s, assistant-prefill continuation on length cutoff \\
Output & Text edit formats parsed and fuzzily applied to files, auto-commit, lint/test, approved unsandboxed shell commands \\
Control flow & Reflection loop up to 3 re-sends on edit/lint/test failure; confirm gates; architect-to-editor two-coder handoff \\
\bottomrule \end{longtable}


\subsection{Cline}

\textbf{Source:} https://github.com/cline/cline $\cdot$ \textbf{Version:} commit 23791b9323aadd91d7dbaea8a8fa262cd2f7ad0b (2026-10-15) $\cdot$ \textbf{Language:} TypeScript (Bun/Node)
\textbf{Path conventions:} \texttt{ag/\allowbreak{}} = \texttt{sdk/\allowbreak{}packages/\allowbreak{}agents/\allowbreak{}src/\allowbreak{}}; \texttt{co/\allowbreak{}} = \texttt{sdk/\allowbreak{}packages/\allowbreak{}core/\allowbreak{}src/\allowbreak{}}; \texttt{ll/\allowbreak{}} = \texttt{sdk/\allowbreak{}packages/\allowbreak{}llms/\allowbreak{}src/\allowbreak{}}; \texttt{sh/\allowbreak{}} = \texttt{sdk/\allowbreak{}packages/\allowbreak{}shared/\allowbreak{}src/\allowbreak{}}; \texttt{cli/\allowbreak{}} = \texttt{apps/\allowbreak{}cli/\allowbreak{}src/\allowbreak{}}. All paths relative to the repo root.
\textbf{What it is:} Cline is an open-source coding agent (Cline Bot Inc.) shipped as a VS Code extension, a CLI, and an SDK. At this commit the harness is a layered monorepo: \texttt{@cline/\allowbreak{}agents} holds the stateless loop (\texttt{AgentRuntime}), \texttt{@cline/\allowbreak{}core} adds sessions, persistence, compaction, hooks, the tool set, and sub-agents, and \texttt{@cline/\allowbreak{}llms} reaches providers through the Vercel AI SDK (\texttt{streamText}) behind a gateway registry. Tool calls use the providers' native tool-calling wire format.

\subsubsection*{Control flow}
\begin{itemize}
\item \textbf{Iteration loop and stop rule:} \texttt{execute} loops \texttt{while iteration < maxIterations}, issues one model request per iteration, executes the reply's tool calls, and returns \texttt{completed} when a turn has no tool calls or a \texttt{completesRun} tool (such as \texttt{submit\_\allowbreak{}and\_\allowbreak{}exit}) succeeded; exceeding \texttt{maxIterations} throws and the run fails. \texttt{ag/\allowbreak{}agent-\allowbreak{}runtime.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L836-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L838}, \texttt{L949-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L972}, \texttt{L1005-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1027}, \texttt{L2383-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L2403}
\item \textbf{Completion reminders:} when a required completion tool exists and a turn has no tool calls, a reminder user message is appended and the loop continues instead of stopping. \texttt{ag/\allowbreak{}agent-\allowbreak{}runtime.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L744-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L786}, \texttt{L955-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L963}
\item \textbf{Budgets and safety stops:} \texttt{MistakeTracker} counts consecutive mistakes (default 6) and either injects guidance or stops; \texttt{LoopDetectionTracker} (CLI default: soft at 3, hard at 5 identical calls) appends a recovery notice or escalates to the mistake limit; any hook returning \texttt{\{stop\}} raises \texttt{ControlledStopError}. \texttt{co/\allowbreak{}runtime/\allowbreak{}safety/\allowbreak{}mistake-\allowbreak{}tracker.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L80-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L164}; \texttt{co/\allowbreak{}runtime/\allowbreak{}safety/\allowbreak{}loop-\allowbreak{}detection.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L66-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L124}; \texttt{co/\allowbreak{}runtime/\allowbreak{}orchestration/\allowbreak{}session-\allowbreak{}runtime-\allowbreak{}orchestrator.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L471-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L510}, \texttt{L1465-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1498}; \texttt{ag/\allowbreak{}agent-\allowbreak{}runtime.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L2777-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L2788}; \texttt{cli/\allowbreak{}runtime/\allowbreak{}defaults.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L6-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L9}
\item \textbf{Approval gate:} \texttt{prepareToolExecution} resolves a per-tool policy and, when \texttt{autoApprove === false}, awaits \texttt{requestToolApproval}; the CLI answers with a terminal \texttt{[y/\allowbreak{}N]} prompt or a file-based desktop IPC polled for up to 5 minutes, and a rejection becomes an error tool result. \texttt{ag/\allowbreak{}agent-\allowbreak{}runtime.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L2477-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L2541}; \texttt{cli/\allowbreak{}utils/\allowbreak{}approval.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L68-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L113}; \texttt{co/\allowbreak{}runtime/\allowbreak{}tools/\allowbreak{}tool-\allowbreak{}approval.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L29-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L102}
\item \textbf{Sub-agents (depth 1 per spawn, inner harness is a \texttt{SessionRuntime}):} the \texttt{spawn\_\allowbreak{}agent} tool builds a delegated \texttt{SessionRuntime} with its own tools, \texttt{maxIterations}, and parent id, awaits \texttt{run(\allowbreak{}task)}, and returns text, iteration count, and usage as the tool result; \texttt{AgentTeam} composes worker/reviewer teams. \texttt{co/\allowbreak{}extensions/\allowbreak{}tools/\allowbreak{}team/\allowbreak{}spawn-\allowbreak{}agent-\allowbreak{}tool.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L117-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L204}; \texttt{co/\allowbreak{}extensions/\allowbreak{}tools/\allowbreak{}team/\allowbreak{}delegated-\allowbreak{}agent.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L149-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L158}; \texttt{co/\allowbreak{}extensions/\allowbreak{}tools/\allowbreak{}team/\allowbreak{}multi-\allowbreak{}agent.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L239-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L245}, \texttt{L535-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L587}
\item \textbf{Plan/Act modes as a bundle:} plan mode swaps the system prompt (context), installs a \texttt{beforeTool} command guard that rejects file-editing shell commands (output filter), and exposes \texttt{switch\_\allowbreak{}to\_\allowbreak{}act\_\allowbreak{}mode} so the model can change mode (control flow). \texttt{sh/\allowbreak{}prompt/\allowbreak{}cline.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L22-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L58}; \texttt{co/\allowbreak{}runtime/\allowbreak{}orchestration/\allowbreak{}runtime-\allowbreak{}builder.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L546-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L566}; \texttt{co/\allowbreak{}extensions/\allowbreak{}tools/\allowbreak{}command-\allowbreak{}guard.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L1-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L23}
\end{itemize}

\subsubsection*{Input}
\begin{itemize}
\item \textbf{Host entry:} \texttt{LocalRuntimeHost.\allowbreak{}runTurn} takes a prompt with optional images and file paths, records \texttt{session.\allowbreak{}input\_\allowbreak{}sent}, and either runs the turn or enqueues it (\texttt{queue} or \texttt{steer} delivery) when a run is active. \texttt{co/\allowbreak{}runtime/\allowbreak{}host/\allowbreak{}local-\allowbreak{}runtime-\allowbreak{}host.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L1142-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1224}; \texttt{co/\allowbreak{}runtime/\allowbreak{}orchestration/\allowbreak{}user-\allowbreak{}input-\allowbreak{}builder.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L10-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L37}
\item \textbf{Mid-turn steering:} \texttt{steer} prompts are consumed at every request boundary by \texttt{consumePendingUserMessage}, which appends them as a user message before the request; \texttt{queue} prompts drain after the turn finishes. \texttt{ag/\allowbreak{}agent-\allowbreak{}runtime.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L1687-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1695}, \texttt{L2302-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L2321}; \texttt{co/\allowbreak{}runtime/\allowbreak{}host/\allowbreak{}local-\allowbreak{}runtime-\allowbreak{}host.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L861-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L868}; \texttt{co/\allowbreak{}runtime/\allowbreak{}turn-\allowbreak{}queue/\allowbreak{}pending-\allowbreak{}prompt-\allowbreak{}service.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L54-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L120}
\item \textbf{Tool results re-enter:} each executed call becomes a \texttt{tool} message holding a \texttt{tool-\allowbreak{}result} part (\texttt{isError} on failure or rejection) pushed onto \texttt{state.\allowbreak{}messages}. \texttt{ag/\allowbreak{}agent-\allowbreak{}runtime.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L2628-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L2647}, \texttt{L974-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L982}
\item \textbf{Model-consumed answers:} the \texttt{ask\_\allowbreak{}question} tool returns the person's chosen option as a tool result (input by rule), distinct from the harness-consumed approval. \texttt{co/\allowbreak{}extensions/\allowbreak{}tools/\allowbreak{}definitions.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L818-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L837}; \texttt{cli/\allowbreak{}utils/\allowbreak{}approval.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L119-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L125}
\end{itemize}

\subsubsection*{Context}
\begin{itemize}
\item \textbf{System prompt:} \texttt{buildClineSystemPrompt} fills the ACT or YOLO template (platform, date, IDE, cwd, workspace metadata, rules) and appends mode instructions; the orchestrator merges extension rules and passes the result to the runtime. \texttt{sh/\allowbreak{}prompt/\allowbreak{}cline.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L152-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L213}; \texttt{sh/\allowbreak{}prompt/\allowbreak{}system/\allowbreak{}act.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L1-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L36}; \texttt{co/\allowbreak{}runtime/\allowbreak{}orchestration/\allowbreak{}session-\allowbreak{}runtime-\allowbreak{}orchestrator.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L139-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L152}, \texttt{L990-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1027}
\item \textbf{Request assembly:} \texttt{prepareModelRequest} clones the transcript, lists tool definitions, appends pending steer input, runs \texttt{prepareTurn} (compaction) and then \texttt{beforeModel} hooks that may replace messages, tools, or options; the orchestrator's \texttt{beforeModel} runs message builders that convert to the provider's message format. \texttt{ag/\allowbreak{}agent-\allowbreak{}runtime.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L1656-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1736}; \texttt{co/\allowbreak{}runtime/\allowbreak{}orchestration/\allowbreak{}session-\allowbreak{}runtime-\allowbreak{}orchestrator.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L1152-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1192}
\item \textbf{Hook context injection:} \texttt{beforeRun}, \texttt{beforeTool}, and \texttt{afterTool} hooks may return \texttt{appendContext}, which is buffered and flushed as one user message after the tool results. \texttt{ag/\allowbreak{}agent-\allowbreak{}runtime.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L1099-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1133}, \texttt{L2457-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L2466}, \texttt{L2606-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L2614}
\item \textbf{Compaction trigger and strategies:} \texttt{createContextCompactionPrepareTurn} estimates request tokens, scales the budget by observed provider under-counting (capped at 4x), compacts when the estimate reaches 90\% of the effective input limit, and targets 70\%; \texttt{agentic} compaction calls a summarizer model over messages before a cut index that preserves 20k recent tokens, \texttt{basic} compaction drops and truncates by projection policy. \texttt{co/\allowbreak{}extensions/\allowbreak{}context/\allowbreak{}compaction.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L267-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L420}; \texttt{co/\allowbreak{}extensions/\allowbreak{}context/\allowbreak{}compaction-\allowbreak{}shared.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L15-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L37}; \texttt{co/\allowbreak{}extensions/\allowbreak{}context/\allowbreak{}agentic-\allowbreak{}compaction.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L116-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L175}; \texttt{co/\allowbreak{}extensions/\allowbreak{}context/\allowbreak{}budget-\allowbreak{}projection/\allowbreak{}project.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L479-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L560}
\item \textbf{Oversized tool results:} tools marked \texttt{resultPolicy:\allowbreak{} "cache-\allowbreak{}oversized"} have results longer than \texttt{maxCachedResultChars} stored in a tool-result cache and replaced by a preview in the transcript. \texttt{co/\allowbreak{}runtime/\allowbreak{}orchestration/\allowbreak{}session-\allowbreak{}runtime-\allowbreak{}orchestrator.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L1160-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1180}, \texttt{L1001-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1005}
\end{itemize}

\subsubsection*{Model interface}
\begin{itemize}
\item \textbf{Gateway and providers:} \texttt{DefaultGateway.\allowbreak{}stream} resolves provider and model from a registry, validates model-side tools, derives \texttt{maxTokens}, and calls the provider's \texttt{stream}; built-in providers wrap AI SDK modules (Anthropic via \texttt{@ai-\allowbreak{}sdk/\allowbreak{}anthropic}). \texttt{ll/\allowbreak{}providers/\allowbreak{}gateway.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L273-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L408}; \texttt{ll/\allowbreak{}providers/\allowbreak{}vendors/\allowbreak{}anthropic.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L15-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L70}
\item \textbf{Streaming and tool-call wire format:} \texttt{streamText} runs with \texttt{maxRetries:\allowbreak{} 5}, \texttt{experimental\_\allowbreak{}repairToolCall}, and native tools; the runtime consumes \texttt{text-\allowbreak{}delta}, \texttt{reasoning-\allowbreak{}delta}, \texttt{tool-\allowbreak{}call-\allowbreak{}delta}, \texttt{usage}, and \texttt{finish} events and classifies a finish error (\texttt{classifyProviderError}, \texttt{isRetryableProviderError}). \texttt{ll/\allowbreak{}providers/\allowbreak{}ai-\allowbreak{}sdk.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L1086-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1097}, \texttt{L2436-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L2446}; \texttt{ag/\allowbreak{}agent-\allowbreak{}runtime.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L1808-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1830}, \texttt{L1877-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1895}, \texttt{L1988-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L2008}
\item \textbf{Turn-level retries:} the runtime re-issues a turn that failed with a retryable provider error up to 3 times with 1 s to 15 s exponential backoff, only when nothing was streamed; an AI SDK middleware retries empty streams up to 3 times. \texttt{ag/\allowbreak{}agent-\allowbreak{}runtime.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L72-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L91}, \texttt{L1202-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1322}; \texttt{ll/\allowbreak{}providers/\allowbreak{}middleware/\allowbreak{}retry-\allowbreak{}empty-\allowbreak{}response.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L97-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L101}, \texttt{L273-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L340}
\item \textbf{Prompt caching:} Anthropic-compatible routing attaches \texttt{cache\_\allowbreak{}control} according to per-provider prompt-cache routes. \texttt{ll/\allowbreak{}providers/\allowbreak{}routing/\allowbreak{}anthropic-\allowbreak{}compatible.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L51-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L91}, \texttt{L199-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L227}
\end{itemize}

\subsubsection*{Output}
\begin{itemize}
\item \textbf{Tool set:} default tools are \texttt{read\_\allowbreak{}files}, \texttt{search\_\allowbreak{}codebase}, \texttt{run\_\allowbreak{}commands}, \texttt{fetch\_\allowbreak{}web\_\allowbreak{}content}, \texttt{editor} (or \texttt{apply\_\allowbreak{}patch}), \texttt{skills}, \texttt{ask\_\allowbreak{}question}, and optionally \texttt{submit\_\allowbreak{}and\_\allowbreak{}exit}; MCP server tools, plugin tools, and team tools are appended by the runtime builder. \texttt{co/\allowbreak{}extensions/\allowbreak{}tools/\allowbreak{}definitions.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L915-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L981}; \texttt{co/\allowbreak{}runtime/\allowbreak{}orchestration/\allowbreak{}runtime-\allowbreak{}builder.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L568-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L598}
\item \textbf{Executor and concurrency:} \texttt{executeToolCalls} prepares every call, then runs adjacent \texttt{parallel}-mode calls under \texttt{Promise.\allowbreak{}all} and \texttt{sequential} ones one at a time; each tool carries \texttt{timeoutMs} (default 30 s), \texttt{retryable}, and \texttt{maxRetries} (default 3). \texttt{ag/\allowbreak{}agent-\allowbreak{}runtime.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L2343-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L2381}; \texttt{sh/\allowbreak{}tools/\allowbreak{}create.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L108-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L138}
\item \textbf{Shell execution:} \texttt{run\_\allowbreak{}commands} runs its commands concurrently through \texttt{createShellExecutor}, which spawns the default shell (detached on POSIX) with a rolling output collector capped at \texttt{maxOutputChars} and a per-command timeout; there is no OS sandbox for commands, while plugins run in a \texttt{SubprocessSandbox} or \texttt{WorkerSandbox}. \texttt{co/\allowbreak{}extensions/\allowbreak{}tools/\allowbreak{}definitions.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L180-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L263}; \texttt{co/\allowbreak{}extensions/\allowbreak{}tools/\allowbreak{}executors/\allowbreak{}bash.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L717-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L760}, \texttt{L1133-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1182}; \texttt{co/\allowbreak{}runtime/\allowbreak{}tools/\allowbreak{}subprocess-\allowbreak{}sandbox.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L126-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L160}; \texttt{co/\allowbreak{}runtime/\allowbreak{}tools/\allowbreak{}worker-\allowbreak{}sandbox.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L91-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L130}
\item \textbf{Result presentation:} \texttt{tool-\allowbreak{}started}, \texttt{tool-\allowbreak{}updated} (streaming command output), \texttt{tool-\allowbreak{}finished}, and \texttt{assistant-\allowbreak{}text-\allowbreak{}delta} events are emitted to hosts (CLI TUI, VS Code webview) through the session event bridge. \texttt{ag/\allowbreak{}agent-\allowbreak{}runtime.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L2543-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L2556}, \texttt{L2578-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L2590}, \texttt{L2692-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L2760}
\item \textbf{Checkpoints:} hook-based checkpoints snapshot the working tree with a stash-style commit plus an untracked-files parent, kept under private git refs per session for restore from the UI. \texttt{co/\allowbreak{}hooks/\allowbreak{}checkpoint-\allowbreak{}hooks.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L249-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L260}, \texttt{L349-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L360}, \texttt{L437-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L450}, \texttt{L497-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L540}
\end{itemize}

\subsubsection*{Production concerns}
\begin{itemize}
\item \textbf{Persistence:} session rows live in a SQLite \texttt{sessions.\allowbreak{}db} (provider, model, cwd, status, \texttt{messages\_\allowbreak{}path}); messages are persisted through \texttt{persistSessionMessages} on runtime events and reloaded from \texttt{messages\_\allowbreak{}path} on resume (context); compaction state is saved to a sidecar. \texttt{co/\allowbreak{}services/\allowbreak{}storage/\allowbreak{}sqlite-\allowbreak{}session-\allowbreak{}store.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L25-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L90}; \texttt{co/\allowbreak{}runtime/\allowbreak{}host/\allowbreak{}local-\allowbreak{}runtime-\allowbreak{}host.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L352-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L366}, \texttt{L754-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L770}, \texttt{L1620-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1627}; \texttt{co/\allowbreak{}runtime/\allowbreak{}host/\allowbreak{}history.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L418-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L447}
\item \textbf{Safety \& security:} per-tool approval policies with an approval callback (control flow), the plan-mode command blacklist and hook \texttt{skip}/\texttt{stop} controls (output filters), provider-executed tool calls skipped locally, and command timeouts. \texttt{ag/\allowbreak{}agent-\allowbreak{}runtime.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L2420-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L2500}; \texttt{co/\allowbreak{}extensions/\allowbreak{}tools/\allowbreak{}command-\allowbreak{}guard.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L1-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L23}; \texttt{cli/\allowbreak{}runtime/\allowbreak{}tool-\allowbreak{}policies.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L30-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L86}
\item \textbf{Observability:} \texttt{CORE\_\allowbreak{}TELEMETRY\_\allowbreak{}EVENTS} (session, \texttt{task.\allowbreak{}tokens}, \texttt{task.\allowbreak{}tool\_\allowbreak{}used}, \texttt{task.\allowbreak{}provider\_\allowbreak{}api\_\allowbreak{}error}, \texttt{task.\allowbreak{}mistake\_\allowbreak{}limit\_\allowbreak{}reached}) through a \texttt{TelemetryService} with OpenTelemetry and Langfuse adapters; the runtime logs request diagnostics and emits \texttt{status-\allowbreak{}notice} events for retries and compaction. \texttt{co/\allowbreak{}services/\allowbreak{}telemetry/\allowbreak{}core-\allowbreak{}events.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L43-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L82}; \texttt{co/\allowbreak{}services/\allowbreak{}telemetry/\allowbreak{}TelemetryService.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L11-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L40}; \texttt{ll/\allowbreak{}services/\allowbreak{}langfuse-\allowbreak{}telemetry.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L1-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L40}; \texttt{ag/\allowbreak{}agent-\allowbreak{}runtime.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L1721-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1733}
\item \textbf{Resilience:} provider-error turn retry, one-shot overflow recovery (compact and retry), max-tokens recovery by compaction then up to 3 concision nudges, a single continuation after an unknown finish, AI SDK request retries honoring retry-after, and abort propagation. \texttt{ag/\allowbreak{}agent-\allowbreak{}runtime.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L1163-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1200}, \texttt{L1363-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1448}, \texttt{L1481-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1596}; \texttt{ll/\allowbreak{}providers/\allowbreak{}ai-\allowbreak{}sdk.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L1086-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1097}
\item \textbf{Performance:} streamed deltas, parallel tool groups, prompt-cache routing, oversized-result caching, a char-based token estimator corrected by provider feedback, and idle-timeout sandboxes. \texttt{ag/\allowbreak{}agent-\allowbreak{}runtime.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L2362-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L2380}; \texttt{co/\allowbreak{}extensions/\allowbreak{}context/\allowbreak{}compaction.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L335-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L372}; \texttt{co/\allowbreak{}runtime/\allowbreak{}tools/\allowbreak{}subprocess-\allowbreak{}sandbox.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L148-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L160}
\item \textbf{Evaluation:} 290 Bun unit-test files across agents, core, and llms (the runtime alone has a 4,920-line test), provider VCR fixtures, CLI end-to-end tests, and \texttt{evals/\allowbreak{}} with smoke tests and \texttt{cline-\allowbreak{}bench} run under Harbor in Docker or Daytona. \texttt{ag/\allowbreak{}agent-\allowbreak{}runtime.\allowbreak{}test.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L1-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L40}; \texttt{ll/\allowbreak{}tests/\allowbreak{}provider-\allowbreak{}vcr/\allowbreak{}README.\allowbreak{}md:\allowbreak{}L1-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L31}; \texttt{cli/\allowbreak{}cli.\allowbreak{}e2e.\allowbreak{}test.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L1-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L40}; \texttt{evals/\allowbreak{}e2e/\allowbreak{}run-\allowbreak{}cline-\allowbreak{}bench.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L1-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L40}
\end{itemize}

\subsubsection*{Hard to place}
\begin{itemize}
\item \textbf{Provider-executed tools:} Anthropic \texttt{web\_\allowbreak{}search} runs inside the provider; the harness sees it only as \texttt{tool-\allowbreak{}call-\allowbreak{}delta} events with \texttt{execution} metadata, records it as model tool activity, and skips local execution; the tool runs on the model side, so the output primitive is partly outside the harness, and the rules do not cover it. \texttt{ag/\allowbreak{}agent-\allowbreak{}runtime.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L1877-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1895}, \texttt{L2422-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L2432}; \texttt{ll/\allowbreak{}providers/\allowbreak{}vendors/\allowbreak{}anthropic.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L29-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L47}
\item \textbf{Agentic compaction:} a context component (\texttt{prepareTurn}) itself issues a model request to the summarizer; by function the trigger and cut are context and the summarizer call is model interface, but the call is sequenced from inside context assembly, not by the loop. \texttt{co/\allowbreak{}extensions/\allowbreak{}context/\allowbreak{}agentic-\allowbreak{}compaction.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L116-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L175}; \texttt{co/\allowbreak{}extensions/\allowbreak{}context/\allowbreak{}compaction-\allowbreak{}shared.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L678-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L720}
\item \textbf{Mistake and loop trackers:} one component both appends guidance as a user message (context) and decides to abort the run (control flow); the rules split it by effect. \texttt{co/\allowbreak{}runtime/\allowbreak{}orchestration/\allowbreak{}session-\allowbreak{}runtime-\allowbreak{}orchestrator.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L1465-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1498}; \texttt{co/\allowbreak{}runtime/\allowbreak{}safety/\allowbreak{}mistake-\allowbreak{}tracker.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L128-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L160}
\item \textbf{Subprocess hook files:} user hook scripts are bundles that can cancel a run (control flow), modify context, and observe events (observability); each decomposes under the rules. \texttt{co/\allowbreak{}hooks/\allowbreak{}subprocess.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L133-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L180}; \texttt{co/\allowbreak{}hooks/\allowbreak{}hook-\allowbreak{}file-\allowbreak{}hooks.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L760-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L770}
\end{itemize}

\subsubsection*{Summary}
\begin{longtable}{@{}p{0.28\linewidth}p{0.66\linewidth}@{}}
\toprule \textbf{Primitive} & \textbf{Design decision} \\ \midrule \endhead
Input & Host runTurn with text, images, files; steer queue consumed per request; tool results as tool messages \\
Context & Templated system prompt plus rules, cloned transcript, hook context, estimator-driven compaction at 90\% via summarizer \\
Model interface & Gateway over Vercel AI SDK streamText, native tools, 5 SDK retries plus 3 turn retries with backoff \\
Output & Native tool calls in parallel groups with timeouts; shell spawn, editor, MCP; git-ref checkpoints \\
Control flow & Loop until no tool calls or completion tool; maxIterations, mistake and loop trackers, approval gates, sub-agents \\
\bottomrule \end{longtable}


\subsection{Gemini CLI}

\textbf{Source:} https://github.com/google-gemini/gemini-cli $\cdot$ \textbf{Version:} commit b25669d84e1a404e30c176d779e732e25dafcec0 (2026-10-16) $\cdot$ \textbf{Language:} TypeScript (Node)
\textbf{Path conventions:} \texttt{core/\allowbreak{}} = \texttt{packages/\allowbreak{}core/\allowbreak{}src/\allowbreak{}}; \texttt{cli/\allowbreak{}} = \texttt{packages/\allowbreak{}cli/\allowbreak{}src/\allowbreak{}}. All paths relative to the repo root.
\textbf{What it is:} Gemini CLI is Google's open-source terminal coding agent. The \texttt{core} package holds the agent loop (\texttt{GeminiClient}), chat history (\texttt{GeminiChat}), the tool scheduler, policy engine, hooks, sandboxing and sub-agents; the \texttt{cli} package is an Ink/React terminal UI that drives the loop and runs tool calls. The model is reached through the \texttt{@google/\allowbreak{}genai} SDK (API key, Vertex AI, or a gateway) or through the Code Assist OAuth backend, always as a streaming \texttt{generateContentStream} call.

\subsubsection*{Control flow}
\begin{itemize}
\item \textbf{Turn loop and budgets:} \texttt{sendMessageStream} runs one model call through \texttt{processTurn} and recurses with \texttt{boundedTurns -\allowbreak{} 1} for continuations; recursion is capped by \texttt{MAX\_\allowbreak{}TURNS = 100} and the session by \texttt{maxSessionTurns}, and a pre-flight overflow check or a \texttt{MaxSessionTurns} event ends the run. \texttt{core/\allowbreak{}core/\allowbreak{}client.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L79}, \texttt{L644-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L663}, \texttt{L727-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L744}, \texttt{L940-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1000}
\item \textbf{Tool re-entry loop lives outside the client:} \texttt{LegacyAgentProtocol} collects \texttt{ToolCallRequest} events, calls \texttt{scheduler.\allowbreak{}schedule}, and re-sends the completed calls' \texttt{responseParts} as the next request, stopping when a response has no tool calls, on a \texttt{STOP\_\allowbreak{}EXECUTION} tool error, or on a fatal tool error; the interactive CLI does the same in \texttt{handleCompletedTools} -> \texttt{submitQuery(\allowbreak{}.\allowbreak{}.\allowbreak{}.\allowbreak{}, \{isContinuation:\allowbreak{} true\})}. \texttt{core/\allowbreak{}agent/\allowbreak{}legacy-\allowbreak{}agent-\allowbreak{}session.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L199-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L215}, \texttt{L260-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L343}; \texttt{cli/\allowbreak{}ui/\allowbreak{}hooks/\allowbreak{}useGeminiStream.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L2109-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L2130}, \texttt{L2314-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L2326}
\item \textbf{Stop rule:} when a turn ends with no pending tool calls, \texttt{checkNextSpeaker} asks a utility model whether the \texttt{model} should speak next and, if so, the harness sends "Please continue."; \texttt{LoopDetectionService} watches repeated tool calls (5), repeated content chunks (10) and an LLM check after 30 turns, injecting a corrective message on the first detection and aborting on the second. \texttt{core/\allowbreak{}core/\allowbreak{}client.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L777-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L795}, \texttt{L840-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L857}, \texttt{L905-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L937}, \texttt{L1302-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1328}; \texttt{core/\allowbreak{}utils/\allowbreak{}nextSpeakerChecker.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L14-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L43}; \texttt{core/\allowbreak{}services/\allowbreak{}loopDetectionService.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L29-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L65}, \texttt{L185-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L215}, \texttt{L260-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L300}
\item \textbf{Approval gates and hooks:} the scheduler's \texttt{\_\allowbreak{}processToolCall} runs the BeforeTool hook, then \texttt{checkPolicy} -> \texttt{PolicyEngine.\allowbreak{}check} (TOML rules plus approval mode DEFAULT/AUTO\_EDIT/YOLO/PLAN) yielding ALLOW/DENY/ASK\_USER, escalates taint-risk invocations to ASK\_USER, and resolves ASK\_USER through \texttt{resolveConfirmation} on the message bus, cancelling the whole batch on a user Cancel; BeforeAgent hooks can stop or block a run, and AfterAgent hooks can stop it or force one more turn. \texttt{core/\allowbreak{}scheduler/\allowbreak{}scheduler.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L640-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L770}; \texttt{core/\allowbreak{}scheduler/\allowbreak{}policy.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L53-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L75}; \texttt{core/\allowbreak{}policy/\allowbreak{}policy-\allowbreak{}engine.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L301-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L304}, \texttt{L350-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L357}; \texttt{core/\allowbreak{}core/\allowbreak{}client.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L956-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L988}, \texttt{L1004-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1058}; \texttt{core/\allowbreak{}hooks/\allowbreak{}types.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L43-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L54}
\item \textbf{Model routing and sub-agents:} per prompt a \texttt{ModelRouterService} composite (Fallback, Override, ApprovalMode, classifier strategies, Default) picks the model, which then sticks for the sequence; \texttt{AgentTool} runs a \texttt{LocalAgentExecutor} whose \texttt{runInternal} loops \texttt{executeTurn} until \texttt{complete\_\allowbreak{}task}, \texttt{maxTurns} or a \texttt{maxTimeMinutes} deadline, and returns the result as a tool result (built-in agents: codebase investigator, CLI help, generalist). \texttt{core/\allowbreak{}core/\allowbreak{}client.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L797-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L826}; \texttt{core/\allowbreak{}routing/\allowbreak{}modelRouterService.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L39-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L75}; \texttt{core/\allowbreak{}agents/\allowbreak{}agent-\allowbreak{}tool.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L217-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L243}; \texttt{core/\allowbreak{}agents/\allowbreak{}local-\allowbreak{}executor.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L582-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L598}, \texttt{L685-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L718}; \texttt{core/\allowbreak{}agents/\allowbreak{}registry.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L286-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L288}
\end{itemize}

\subsubsection*{Input}
\begin{itemize}
\item \textbf{User message:} the CLI's \texttt{submitQuery} hands the typed parts to \texttt{geminiClient.\allowbreak{}sendMessageStream}; \texttt{@path} references are expanded by \texttt{handleAtCommand} through the \texttt{read\_\allowbreak{}many\_\allowbreak{}files} tool before submission. \texttt{cli/\allowbreak{}ui/\allowbreak{}hooks/\allowbreak{}useGeminiStream.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L1910-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1916}; \texttt{cli/\allowbreak{}ui/\allowbreak{}hooks/\allowbreak{}atCommandProcessor.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L690-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L699}
\item \textbf{Tool results:} completed calls' \texttt{functionResponse} parts become the next user content; a queued user steering hint is prepended to that content; if the model was switched by a quota error the responses are only recorded to history. \texttt{cli/\allowbreak{}ui/\allowbreak{}hooks/\allowbreak{}useGeminiStream.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L2280-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L2326}; \texttt{core/\allowbreak{}agent/\allowbreak{}legacy-\allowbreak{}agent-\allowbreak{}session.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L278-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L308}, \texttt{L341-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L342}
\item \textbf{Answers the model asked for:} \texttt{AskUserTool} receives the user's answers from the confirmation payload and returns them as JSON \texttt{llmContent}. \texttt{core/\allowbreak{}tools/\allowbreak{}ask-\allowbreak{}user.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L171-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L179}, \texttt{L187-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L248}
\item \textbf{IDE context:} open files and cursor state from the IDE companion are appended as a user part when no tool call is pending. \texttt{core/\allowbreak{}core/\allowbreak{}client.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L459-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L470}, \texttt{L755-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L771}
\end{itemize}

\subsubsection*{Context}
\begin{itemize}
\item \textbf{System prompt:} \texttt{PromptProvider.\allowbreak{}getCoreSystemPrompt} composes snippet sections (mandates, workflows, sandbox, git, user memory, skills, plan-mode tool list) chosen by model generation, or loads a \texttt{GEMINI\_\allowbreak{}SYSTEM\_\allowbreak{}MD} override file with substitutions. \texttt{core/\allowbreak{}prompts/\allowbreak{}promptProvider.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L47-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L135}; \texttt{core/\allowbreak{}prompts/\allowbreak{}snippets.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L136-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L336}
\item \textbf{Project and user memory:} \texttt{GEMINI.\allowbreak{}md} files are read from the global \texttt{\textasciitilde{}/\allowbreak{}.\allowbreak{}gemini} dir, upward from the cwd to the project root, and from extensions, then concatenated into the user-memory section. \texttt{core/\allowbreak{}tools/\allowbreak{}memoryTool.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L11}; \texttt{core/\allowbreak{}utils/\allowbreak{}memoryDiscovery.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L209}, \texttt{L295}, \texttt{L317}, \texttt{L342}, \texttt{L463-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L471}
\item \textbf{History shape:} \texttt{GeminiChat} keeps a comprehensive history and derives a curated one (invalid model turns dropped) that is what gets sent. \texttt{core/\allowbreak{}core/\allowbreak{}geminiChat.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L220-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L255}, \texttt{L1270-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1278}
\item \textbf{Compaction:} before each call \texttt{tryCompressChat} runs when the last prompt token count reaches 50\% of the model's limit; it truncates to budget, splits keeping the last 30\%, summarizes the rest with a utility model into a \texttt{<state\_\allowbreak{}snapshot>}, and falls back to truncation only after an inflated result; an optional \texttt{ContextManager} pipeline (masking, distillation, rolling summary processors) replaces this when enabled. \texttt{core/\allowbreak{}context/\allowbreak{}chatCompressionService.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L45}, \texttt{L269}, \texttt{L467-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L560}, \texttt{L595-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L614}; \texttt{core/\allowbreak{}core/\allowbreak{}client.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L670-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L725}, \texttt{L1226-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1283}; \texttt{core/\allowbreak{}context/\allowbreak{}contextManager.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L90-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L122}
\item \textbf{Tool output masking:} \texttt{tryMaskToolOutputs} replaces bulky older tool outputs once protected recent output exceeds 50k tokens, and the request is token-estimated against the window before sending. \texttt{core/\allowbreak{}context/\allowbreak{}toolOutputMaskingService.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L25-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L28}; \texttt{core/\allowbreak{}core/\allowbreak{}client.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L727-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L744}, \texttt{L1288-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1297}
\end{itemize}

\subsubsection*{Model interface}
\begin{itemize}
\item \textbf{Client:} \texttt{createContentGenerator} builds a \texttt{GoogleGenAI} SDK client (API key, Vertex, gateway, proxy agent) or the Code Assist OAuth generator, wrapped in \texttt{ModelMappingContentGenerator} and \texttt{LoggingContentGenerator}, optionally \texttt{RecordingContentGenerator}. \texttt{core/\allowbreak{}core/\allowbreak{}contentGenerator.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L210-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L239}, \texttt{L293-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L310}, \texttt{L385-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L427}
\item \textbf{Streaming and retries:} \texttt{generateContentStream} is wrapped in \texttt{retryWithBackoff} (default 10 attempts, 5s to 30s backoff on 429/5xx); mid-stream invalid-content or network errors are retried up to 4 attempts with exponential backoff and a \texttt{RETRY} event tells the UI to discard partial output. \texttt{core/\allowbreak{}core/\allowbreak{}geminiChat.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L105-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L109}, \texttt{L664-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L700}, \texttt{L739-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L794}, \texttt{L1180-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1230}; \texttt{core/\allowbreak{}utils/\allowbreak{}retry.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L20}, \texttt{L42-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L46}, \texttt{L205}
\item \textbf{Fallback model:} a persistent 429 calls \texttt{handleFallback}, which selects the next available model from the policy chain and consults a user handler whose intent (\texttt{retry\_\allowbreak{}always}, \texttt{retry\_\allowbreak{}once}, \texttt{stop}, ...) decides whether to switch. \texttt{core/\allowbreak{}fallback/\allowbreak{}handler.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L26-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L80}, \texttt{L157-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L196}
\item \textbf{Tool-call format:} native Gemini \texttt{functionCall} parts; \texttt{Turn.\allowbreak{}run} turns each into a \texttt{ToolCallRequest} event and yields \texttt{Finished} with the finish reason and usage. \texttt{core/\allowbreak{}core/\allowbreak{}turn.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L270-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L300}, \texttt{L380-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L414}, \texttt{L508-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L521}
\end{itemize}

\subsubsection*{Output}
\begin{itemize}
\item \textbf{Tool set:} core tools registered in \texttt{Config} (ls, read\_file, grep/ripgrep, glob, edit, write\_file, shell, web\_fetch, web\_search, memory, ask\_user, activate\_skill, agent tools) plus MCP tools discovered into the \texttt{ToolRegistry}. \texttt{core/\allowbreak{}config/\allowbreak{}config.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L4014-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L4060}; \texttt{core/\allowbreak{}tools/\allowbreak{}tool-\allowbreak{}registry.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L287-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L302}, \texttt{L370}, \texttt{L663}; \texttt{core/\allowbreak{}tools/\allowbreak{}mcp-\allowbreak{}tool.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L501}
\item \textbf{Executor and parallelism:} the scheduler dequeues contiguous parallelizable calls as one batch, validates them concurrently, and executes all scheduled calls with \texttt{Promise.\allowbreak{}all}, streaming live output into state. \texttt{core/\allowbreak{}scheduler/\allowbreak{}scheduler.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L519-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L566}, \texttt{L777-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L821}
\item \textbf{Shell execution:} commands run under \texttt{node-\allowbreak{}pty} with a 16MB buffer, an inactivity timeout, and optional backgrounding. \texttt{core/\allowbreak{}services/\allowbreak{}shellExecutionService.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L50}, \texttt{L454-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L470}; \texttt{core/\allowbreak{}tools/\allowbreak{}shell.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L554-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L660}
\item \textbf{Sandbox:} \texttt{createSandboxManager} picks macOS \texttt{sandbox-\allowbreak{}exec} (seatbelt profiles), Linux bubblewrap with seccomp, or a Windows manager; the CLI can also run itself inside a docker/podman container. \texttt{core/\allowbreak{}services/\allowbreak{}sandboxManagerFactory.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L22-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L36}; \texttt{core/\allowbreak{}sandbox/\allowbreak{}macos/\allowbreak{}MacOsSandboxManager.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L178}; \texttt{core/\allowbreak{}sandbox/\allowbreak{}linux/\allowbreak{}LinuxSandboxManager.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L303-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L318}; \texttt{cli/\allowbreak{}utils/\allowbreak{}sandbox.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L121}, \texttt{L402-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L403}
\item \textbf{Result presentation and truncation:} shell output above 40,000 chars is saved to the project temp dir and replaced by a truncated excerpt; stream events are rendered incrementally by \texttt{processGeminiStreamEvents}. \texttt{core/\allowbreak{}scheduler/\allowbreak{}tool-\allowbreak{}executor.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L202-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L232}; \texttt{core/\allowbreak{}config/\allowbreak{}config.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L483}; \texttt{cli/\allowbreak{}ui/\allowbreak{}hooks/\allowbreak{}useGeminiStream.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L1917-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1920}
\end{itemize}

\subsubsection*{Production concerns}
\begin{itemize}
\item \textbf{Persistence:} context/input record: \texttt{ChatRecordingService} writes each session as a JSONL file under the project temp \texttt{chats/\allowbreak{}} dir (sub-agents nested under the parent id) for resume. \texttt{core/\allowbreak{}services/\allowbreak{}chatRecordingService.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L779-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L823}
\item \textbf{Safety \& security:} control flow and output: TOML policy engine with approval modes and non-interactive deny default, taint-risk escalation to ASK\_USER, OS sandboxes, hook shell commands with a 60s timeout. \texttt{core/\allowbreak{}policy/\allowbreak{}policy-\allowbreak{}engine.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L301-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L304}; \texttt{core/\allowbreak{}scheduler/\allowbreak{}scheduler.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L687-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L696}; \texttt{core/\allowbreak{}hooks/\allowbreak{}hookRunner.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L252-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L262}
\item \textbf{Observability:} OpenTelemetry logs, metrics and traces exported over OTLP gRPC/HTTP plus Clearcut; \texttt{logApiResponse} records duration, status and token usage including cached tokens. \texttt{core/\allowbreak{}telemetry/\allowbreak{}sdk.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L15-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L21}; \texttt{core/\allowbreak{}telemetry/\allowbreak{}loggers.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L307-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L330}; \texttt{core/\allowbreak{}telemetry/\allowbreak{}types.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L691}
\item \textbf{Resilience:} model interface and context: connection and mid-stream retries, model fallback on persistent 429, pre-flight overflow detection and compaction, loop recovery. \texttt{core/\allowbreak{}core/\allowbreak{}geminiChat.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L739-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L794}; \texttt{core/\allowbreak{}core/\allowbreak{}client.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L727-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L744}, \texttt{L1302-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1328}
\item \textbf{Performance:} output and context: parallel tool batches, streaming, compaction at 50\% of the window, tool-output masking, model routing to cheaper models by classifier. \texttt{core/\allowbreak{}scheduler/\allowbreak{}scheduler.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L519-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L566}; \texttt{core/\allowbreak{}routing/\allowbreak{}strategies/\allowbreak{}classifierStrategy.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L31-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L32}
\item \textbf{Evaluation:} \texttt{evals/\allowbreak{}} behavioral evals (38 \texttt{.\allowbreak{}eval.\allowbreak{}ts}) check what the model chooses to do; \texttt{integration-\allowbreak{}tests/\allowbreak{}} (48 \texttt{.\allowbreak{}test.\allowbreak{}ts}) replay recorded API responses through \texttt{FakeContentGenerator}. \texttt{evals/\allowbreak{}README.\allowbreak{}md:\allowbreak{}L1-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L20}; \texttt{core/\allowbreak{}core/\allowbreak{}contentGenerator.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L216-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L228}
\end{itemize}

\subsubsection*{Hard to place}
\begin{itemize}
