\item \textbf{Model calls that steer the loop:} \texttt{checkNextSpeaker}, the LLM loop check and the classifier router are model calls whose only effect is a go-again or routing decision; by the routing rule they are control flow even though each is itself a request to the model interface. \texttt{core/\allowbreak{}utils/\allowbreak{}nextSpeakerChecker.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L43-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L80}; \texttt{core/\allowbreak{}services/\allowbreak{}loopDetectionService.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L260-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L300}; \texttt{core/\allowbreak{}routing/\allowbreak{}strategies/\allowbreak{}classifierStrategy.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L132}, \texttt{L171}
\item \textbf{Hooks as bundles:} BeforeAgent \texttt{additionalContext} is context, BeforeTool arg modification is output, AfterAgent stop/continue is control flow, and BeforeModel/AfterModel hooks edit the request inside the chat layer; the hook runner spawning a shell process fits no primitive. \texttt{core/\allowbreak{}core/\allowbreak{}client.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L978-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L988}; \texttt{core/\allowbreak{}scheduler/\allowbreak{}scheduler.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L670-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L674}; \texttt{core/\allowbreak{}core/\allowbreak{}geminiChat.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L1173-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1176}; \texttt{core/\allowbreak{}hooks/\allowbreak{}hookRunner.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L7}
\item \textbf{Turn loop split across packages:} the client only ever issues one model call per \texttt{processTurn}; the model/tool alternation is owned by the caller (CLI hook or \texttt{LegacyAgentProtocol}), so the "loop" is control flow assembled from two files. \texttt{core/\allowbreak{}core/\allowbreak{}client.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L644-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L663}; \texttt{core/\allowbreak{}agent/\allowbreak{}legacy-\allowbreak{}agent-\allowbreak{}session.\allowbreak{}ts:\allowbreak{}L260-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L343}
\end{itemize}

\subsubsection*{Summary}
\begin{longtable}{@{}p{0.28\linewidth}p{0.66\linewidth}@{}}
\toprule \textbf{Primitive} & \textbf{Design decision} \\ \midrule \endhead
Input & CLI/ACP parts with @file expansion; tool results and AskUser answers re-enter as functionResponse parts \\
Context & Snippet-composed system prompt, hierarchical GEMINI.md memory, curated history, 50\% threshold snapshot compression, tool masking \\
Model interface & @google/genai or Code Assist streaming, 10-attempt backoff, mid-stream retries, policy-chain model fallback \\
Output & Registry of core and MCP tools, parallel batches, node-pty shell, seatbelt/bwrap/container sandboxes, 40k truncation \\
Control flow & Caller-driven tool loop, 100-turn cap, next-speaker and loop checks, policy/confirmation gate, hooks, classifier routing, sub-agents \\
\bottomrule \end{longtable}


\subsection{Goose (Block)}

\textbf{Source:} https://github.com/block/goose $\cdot$ \textbf{Version:} commit 5eca486214c86945ac6de2603ee27f51083822fb (2026-10-16) $\cdot$ \textbf{Language:} Rust
\textbf{Path conventions:} \texttt{g/\allowbreak{}} = \texttt{crates/\allowbreak{}goose/\allowbreak{}src/\allowbreak{}}; \texttt{sm/\allowbreak{}} = \texttt{crates/\allowbreak{}goose/\allowbreak{}src/\allowbreak{}agents/\allowbreak{}state\_\allowbreak{}machine/\allowbreak{}}; \texttt{ga/\allowbreak{}} = \texttt{crates/\allowbreak{}goose-\allowbreak{}agent/\allowbreak{}src/\allowbreak{}}; \texttt{gp/\allowbreak{}} = \texttt{crates/\allowbreak{}goose-\allowbreak{}providers/\allowbreak{}src/\allowbreak{}}; \texttt{gpt/\allowbreak{}} = \texttt{crates/\allowbreak{}goose-\allowbreak{}provider-\allowbreak{}types/\allowbreak{}src/\allowbreak{}}; \texttt{cli/\allowbreak{}} = \texttt{crates/\allowbreak{}goose-\allowbreak{}cli/\allowbreak{}src/\allowbreak{}}. All paths relative to the repo root.
\textbf{What it is:} Goose is an open-source general-purpose agent started by Block and now attributed to the Agentic AI Foundation, with a CLI, a desktop app and a server sharing one Rust core. Each user message runs an ordered state machine of "operations" over a persisted SQLite conversation, with a single inference step at the end of the order. The model is reached through a \texttt{Provider} trait with many HTTP implementations (Anthropic, OpenAI, Google, Bedrock, Ollama, ...) and some ACP/CLI-wrapper providers that run their own tool loop.

\subsubsection*{Control flow}
\begin{itemize}
\item \textbf{State machine loop:} \texttt{build\_\allowbreak{}state\_\allowbreak{}machine} orders operations (slash command, tool approval, skills, recipe, tool execution, remaining tool calls, subagent, steer, bang shell, compaction, tool-pair compaction, doctor, project, retry, empty response, stop hook, exit on error, max turns) and appends the \texttt{Inference} step; \texttt{StateMachine:\allowbreak{}:\allowbreak{}step} runs the first applicable operation and returns \texttt{Continuing}, or \texttt{Yielded} when none applies, and \texttt{run} repeats load-step-apply until a yield or cancel. \texttt{g/\allowbreak{}agents/\allowbreak{}state\_\allowbreak{}machine\_\allowbreak{}builder.\allowbreak{}rs:\allowbreak{}L43-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L136}; \texttt{ga/\allowbreak{}machine.\allowbreak{}rs:\allowbreak{}L121-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L190}, \texttt{L211-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L226}
\item \textbf{Go-again rule:} inference applies only when the projected conversation ends with a provider turn (\texttt{should\_\allowbreak{}infer}) and there is no trailing error; after tool results are appended the next step infers again, and a plain assistant reply leaves nothing applicable, so the turn ends. \texttt{ga/\allowbreak{}inference.\allowbreak{}rs:\allowbreak{}L277-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L286}, \texttt{L377-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L403}
\item \textbf{Budgets:} \texttt{MaxTurnsOperation} ends the turn with a fixed message once assistant turns since kickoff reach \texttt{max\_\allowbreak{}turns} (session config, \texttt{GOOSE\_\allowbreak{}MAX\_\allowbreak{}TURNS}, default 1000) and advertises a \texttt{<turn-\allowbreak{}budget>} part after half is used; \texttt{ExitOnErrorOperation} yields on a trailing error. \texttt{sm/\allowbreak{}ops\_\allowbreak{}maxturns.\allowbreak{}rs:\allowbreak{}L14-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L67}; \texttt{g/\allowbreak{}agents/\allowbreak{}agent.\allowbreak{}rs:\allowbreak{}L52}, \texttt{L565-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L569}; \texttt{sm/\allowbreak{}ops\_\allowbreak{}exit\_\allowbreak{}on\_\allowbreak{}error.\allowbreak{}rs:\allowbreak{}L21-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L32}
\item \textbf{Approval gate:} \texttt{ToolApprovalOperation} (skipped in \texttt{Chat} mode) runs tool inspectors, marks denied requests non-executable and emits \texttt{ActionRequired} confirmation messages; \texttt{stream\_\allowbreak{}state\_\allowbreak{}machine\_\allowbreak{}turn} waits for all confirmation answers, then re-runs the machine; modes are \texttt{Auto}, \texttt{Approve}, \texttt{SmartApprove}, \texttt{Chat}. \texttt{sm/\allowbreak{}ops\_\allowbreak{}tool\_\allowbreak{}approval.\allowbreak{}rs:\allowbreak{}L43-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L148}; \texttt{g/\allowbreak{}agents/\allowbreak{}agent.\allowbreak{}rs:\allowbreak{}L793-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L837}; \texttt{gpt/\allowbreak{}goose\_\allowbreak{}mode.\allowbreak{}rs:\allowbreak{}L22-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L32}
\item \textbf{Stop hook, steering, sub-agents:} \texttt{StopHookOperation} lets a plugin deny ending the turn up to \texttt{stop\_\allowbreak{}hook\_\allowbreak{}block\_\allowbreak{}cap} times; \texttt{SteerOperation} drains queued user messages between model and tool turns; \texttt{SubagentOperation} with \texttt{ForegroundSubagentRunner} runs a delegated \texttt{SubAgent} session (own recipe, max\_turns and provider) through \texttt{stream\_\allowbreak{}subagent\_\allowbreak{}turn} and delivers its outcome back as a message. \texttt{sm/\allowbreak{}ops\_\allowbreak{}stop\_\allowbreak{}hook.\allowbreak{}rs:\allowbreak{}L80-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L119}; \texttt{sm/\allowbreak{}ops\_\allowbreak{}steer.\allowbreak{}rs:\allowbreak{}L60-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L97}; \texttt{g/\allowbreak{}agents/\allowbreak{}subagent\_\allowbreak{}handler.\allowbreak{}rs:\allowbreak{}L35-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L82}, \texttt{L102-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L130}; \texttt{g/\allowbreak{}agents/\allowbreak{}platform\_\allowbreak{}extensions/\allowbreak{}summon.\allowbreak{}rs:\allowbreak{}L396-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L421}, \texttt{L460-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L470}
\end{itemize}

\subsubsection*{Input}
\begin{itemize}
\item \textbf{User message:} \texttt{Agent:\allowbreak{}:\allowbreak{}reply} -> \texttt{reply\_\allowbreak{}impl} -> \texttt{reply\_\allowbreak{}with\_\allowbreak{}state\_\allowbreak{}machine\_\allowbreak{}inner}, which persists the message with \texttt{session\_\allowbreak{}manager.\allowbreak{}add\_\allowbreak{}message} before the machine runs; the CLI calls \texttt{agent.\allowbreak{}reply} per prompt. \texttt{g/\allowbreak{}agents/\allowbreak{}agent.\allowbreak{}rs:\allowbreak{}L953-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1035}, \texttt{L637-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L660}; \texttt{cli/\allowbreak{}session/\allowbreak{}mod.\allowbreak{}rs:\allowbreak{}L1333-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1340}
\item \textbf{Mid-turn messages:} \texttt{Agent:\allowbreak{}:\allowbreak{}steer} pushes into a per-session queue that \texttt{SteerOperation} consumes; a message carrying an \texttt{ElicitationResponse} completes a pending MCP elicitation instead of starting a turn. \texttt{g/\allowbreak{}agents/\allowbreak{}agent.\allowbreak{}rs:\allowbreak{}L293-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L302}, \texttt{L996-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1031}
\item \textbf{Tool results:} executed, declined and unparseable requests are recorded into one user message of tool responses, emitted and persisted as an \texttt{AppendMessage} effect. \texttt{sm/\allowbreak{}ops\_\allowbreak{}toolcalling.\allowbreak{}rs:\allowbreak{}L1044-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1100}; \texttt{sm/\allowbreak{}session.\allowbreak{}rs:\allowbreak{}L52-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L56}
\end{itemize}

\subsubsection*{Context}
\begin{itemize}
\item \textbf{System prompt:} \texttt{PromptManager:\allowbreak{}:\allowbreak{}build\_\allowbreak{}system\_\allowbreak{}prompt} renders \texttt{system.\allowbreak{}md} (extension list and instructions, mode, date) or a session \texttt{system\_\allowbreak{}prompt\_\allowbreak{}override}, then appends hints and per-operation \texttt{prompt\_\allowbreak{}parts}; inference tools are gathered from every operation. \texttt{g/\allowbreak{}agents/\allowbreak{}prompt\_\allowbreak{}manager.\allowbreak{}rs:\allowbreak{}L114-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L160}, \texttt{L201-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L213}; \texttt{g/\allowbreak{}prompts/\allowbreak{}system.\allowbreak{}md:\allowbreak{}L1-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L30}; \texttt{sm/\allowbreak{}inference\_\allowbreak{}preparation.\allowbreak{}rs:\allowbreak{}L40-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L90}; \texttt{ga/\allowbreak{}machine.\allowbreak{}rs:\allowbreak{}L143-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L155}
\item \textbf{Project files:} \texttt{.\allowbreak{}goosehints} and \texttt{AGENTS.\allowbreak{}md} (configurable \texttt{CONTEXT\_\allowbreak{}FILE\_\allowbreak{}NAMES}) are loaded from the global config dir and the working directory. \texttt{g/\allowbreak{}hints/\allowbreak{}load\_\allowbreak{}hints.\allowbreak{}rs:\allowbreak{}L10-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L21}, \texttt{L232-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L252}
\item \textbf{Per-turn context event:} a \texttt{<turn-\allowbreak{}context>} user-role message with working dir, context limit and operation \texttt{moim\_\allowbreak{}parts} (such as the turn budget) is appended to the request when it changed. \texttt{g/\allowbreak{}agents/\allowbreak{}moim.\allowbreak{}rs:\allowbreak{}L92-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L122}; \texttt{sm/\allowbreak{}ops\_\allowbreak{}maxturns.\allowbreak{}rs:\allowbreak{}L20-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L28}
\item \textbf{Compaction:} \texttt{CompactionOperation} fires when tokens/context\_limit exceeds the threshold (default 0.8, lowered so compaction starts by 225k tokens) or reactively after a \texttt{ContextLengthExceeded} error (at most 2), summarizes with the provider via \texttt{compact\_\allowbreak{}messages} and replaces the conversation; \texttt{ToolPairCompactionOperation} summarizes old tool pairs past a cutoff; providers that manage their own context skip both. \texttt{sm/\allowbreak{}ops\_\allowbreak{}compaction.\allowbreak{}rs:\allowbreak{}L24}, \texttt{L192-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L294}; \texttt{g/\allowbreak{}context\_\allowbreak{}mgmt/\allowbreak{}mod.\allowbreak{}rs:\allowbreak{}L25-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L42}, \texttt{L87}; \texttt{crates/\allowbreak{}goose-\allowbreak{}context-\allowbreak{}management/\allowbreak{}src/\allowbreak{}lib.\allowbreak{}rs:\allowbreak{}L32}; \texttt{sm/\allowbreak{}ops\_\allowbreak{}tool\_\allowbreak{}pair\_\allowbreak{}compaction.\allowbreak{}rs:\allowbreak{}L65-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L83}
\item \textbf{Request projection and caching:} before sending, only agent-visible messages are kept, the conversation is repaired and consecutive messages merged; the Anthropic format sets \texttt{cache\_\allowbreak{}control:\allowbreak{} ephemeral} on the request. \texttt{ga/\allowbreak{}inference.\allowbreak{}rs:\allowbreak{}L434-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L439}; \texttt{g/\allowbreak{}agents/\allowbreak{}reply\_\allowbreak{}parts.\allowbreak{}rs:\allowbreak{}L293-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L306}; \texttt{gpt/\allowbreak{}formats/\allowbreak{}anthropic.\allowbreak{}rs:\allowbreak{}L126-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L134}
\end{itemize}

\subsubsection*{Model interface}
\begin{itemize}
\item \textbf{Provider trait:} \texttt{stream} is the one required method, \texttt{complete} collects the stream, and \texttt{retry\_\allowbreak{}config} is overridable; concrete providers are built from \texttt{ProviderDef:\allowbreak{}:\allowbreak{}from\_\allowbreak{}env}. \texttt{gpt/\allowbreak{}base.\allowbreak{}rs:\allowbreak{}L520-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L565}; \texttt{g/\allowbreak{}providers/\allowbreak{}base.\allowbreak{}rs:\allowbreak{}L31-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L68}
\item \textbf{HTTP client and streaming:} the Anthropic provider POSTs \texttt{v1/\allowbreak{}messages} through \texttt{with\_\allowbreak{}retry}, decodes SSE lines into a message stream, and retries once with thinking history stripped if the API rejects replayed thinking blocks; default timeouts are 600s per request and 30s connect. \texttt{gp/\allowbreak{}anthropic.\allowbreak{}rs:\allowbreak{}L184-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L262}, \texttt{L434-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L449}; \texttt{gp/\allowbreak{}api\_\allowbreak{}client.\allowbreak{}rs:\allowbreak{}L19-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L20}
\item \textbf{Retry policy:} \texttt{RetryConfig} defaults to 3 retries with 1s initial delay, x2 backoff, 30s cap, retrying rate-limit, server and network errors and honoring a server \texttt{retry\_\allowbreak{}delay}. \texttt{gpt/\allowbreak{}retry.\allowbreak{}rs:\allowbreak{}L14-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L17}, \texttt{L107-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L157}
\item \textbf{Stream consumption:} \texttt{InferenceRunner:\allowbreak{}:\allowbreak{}infer} calls \texttt{provider.\allowbreak{}stream}, records usage per chunk, tags assistant chunks with provider/model metadata, drops duplicate tool requests, and retries an empty response up to 3 times; provider errors become error messages in the conversation; no backup model was found. \texttt{ga/\allowbreak{}inference.\allowbreak{}rs:\allowbreak{}L77}, \texttt{L443-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L520}; \texttt{g/\allowbreak{}agents/\allowbreak{}reply\_\allowbreak{}parts.\allowbreak{}rs:\allowbreak{}L282-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L352}
\end{itemize}

\subsubsection*{Output}
\begin{itemize}
\item \textbf{Tool set:} every tool is an MCP extension reached through an \texttt{ExtensionLease}: builtin platform extensions (developer shell/editor, summon with \texttt{delegate}, code execution, skills) and external stdio/HTTP MCP servers. \texttt{sm/\allowbreak{}ops\_\allowbreak{}toolcalling.\allowbreak{}rs:\allowbreak{}L841-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L848}; \texttt{g/\allowbreak{}agents/\allowbreak{}extension\_\allowbreak{}manager/\allowbreak{}builtin.\allowbreak{}rs:\allowbreak{}L12-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L26}; \texttt{g/\allowbreak{}agents/\allowbreak{}platform\_\allowbreak{}extensions/\allowbreak{}developer/\allowbreak{}shell.\allowbreak{}rs:\allowbreak{}L229-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L245}
\item \textbf{Executor and parallelism:} \texttt{ToolExecutionOperation:\allowbreak{}:\allowbreak{}run} dispatches each executable request (pre-tool hooks, \texttt{lease.\allowbreak{}call}, post-tool hooks) and merges the result streams with \texttt{select\_\allowbreak{}all}, so calls from one response run concurrently and finish in any order. \texttt{sm/\allowbreak{}ops\_\allowbreak{}toolcalling.\allowbreak{}rs:\allowbreak{}L397-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L470}, \texttt{L910-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L944}, \texttt{L1010-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1100}
\item \textbf{Sandbox:} a session may carry a Docker container id; builtin extensions are then started with \texttt{docker exec} inside it, otherwise tools run as host processes. \texttt{g/\allowbreak{}agents/\allowbreak{}container.\allowbreak{}rs:\allowbreak{}L3-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L12}; \texttt{g/\allowbreak{}agents/\allowbreak{}extension\_\allowbreak{}manager/\allowbreak{}builtin.\allowbreak{}rs:\allowbreak{}L19-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L26}; \texttt{sm/\allowbreak{}ops\_\allowbreak{}toolcalling.\allowbreak{}rs:\allowbreak{}L443}
\item \textbf{Result size:} shell output is capped at 2000 lines / 50,000 bytes with the full output saved to a file, and any tool text over 200,000 chars (\texttt{GOOSE\_\allowbreak{}MAX\_\allowbreak{}TOOL\_\allowbreak{}RESPONSE\_\allowbreak{}SIZE}) is written to a temp file and replaced by a reference. \texttt{g/\allowbreak{}agents/\allowbreak{}platform\_\allowbreak{}extensions/\allowbreak{}developer/\allowbreak{}shell.\allowbreak{}rs:\allowbreak{}L159-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L164}; \texttt{g/\allowbreak{}agents/\allowbreak{}large\_\allowbreak{}response\_\allowbreak{}handler.\allowbreak{}rs:\allowbreak{}L5-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L30}
\item \textbf{Presentation:} events flow through an mpsc channel to the caller's stream; the CLI renders them through a streaming markdown buffer with MCP spinners. \texttt{g/\allowbreak{}agents/\allowbreak{}agent.\allowbreak{}rs:\allowbreak{}L862-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L920}; \texttt{cli/\allowbreak{}session/\allowbreak{}mod.\allowbreak{}rs:\allowbreak{}L1342-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1344}
\end{itemize}

\subsubsection*{Production concerns}
\begin{itemize}
\item \textbf{Persistence:} context record: sessions and messages live in a SQLite \texttt{sessions.\allowbreak{}db} (WAL) under the data dir; every machine effect is applied through \texttt{SessionManager:\allowbreak{}:\allowbreak{}apply\_\allowbreak{}effects} and the machine reloads the session each step, so a turn can be resumed after a crash. \texttt{g/\allowbreak{}session/\allowbreak{}session\_\allowbreak{}manager.\allowbreak{}rs:\allowbreak{}L34}, \texttt{L1066-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1080}; \texttt{sm/\allowbreak{}session.\allowbreak{}rs:\allowbreak{}L33-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L79}; \texttt{g/\allowbreak{}agents/\allowbreak{}agent.\allowbreak{}rs:\allowbreak{}L712}
\item \textbf{Safety \& security:} control flow and output: Goose modes, a persisted tool permission store (\texttt{tool\_\allowbreak{}permissions.\allowbreak{}json}), an LLM permission judge for read-only classification, a prompt-injection scanner (threshold 0.8) that forces approval, and shell hooks with a 30s default timeout. \texttt{g/\allowbreak{}permission/\allowbreak{}permission\_\allowbreak{}store.\allowbreak{}rs:\allowbreak{}L45-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L90}; \texttt{g/\allowbreak{}permission/\allowbreak{}permission\_\allowbreak{}judge.\allowbreak{}rs:\allowbreak{}L145-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L158}; \texttt{g/\allowbreak{}security/\allowbreak{}scanner.\allowbreak{}rs:\allowbreak{}L126-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L130}; \texttt{g/\allowbreak{}security/\allowbreak{}security\_\allowbreak{}inspector.\allowbreak{}rs:\allowbreak{}L29-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L53}; \texttt{g/\allowbreak{}hooks/\allowbreak{}mod.\allowbreak{}rs:\allowbreak{}L43}, \texttt{L1031-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1040}
\item \textbf{Observability:} tracing spans carry \texttt{gen\_\allowbreak{}ai.\allowbreak{}*} attributes (usage, tool arguments, finish reasons), exported via OTLP, a Langfuse layer, and PostHog product telemetry. \texttt{g/\allowbreak{}agents/\allowbreak{}gen\_\allowbreak{}ai\_\allowbreak{}telemetry.\allowbreak{}rs:\allowbreak{}L52-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L70}; \texttt{g/\allowbreak{}otel/\allowbreak{}otlp.\allowbreak{}rs:\allowbreak{}L75-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L143}; \texttt{g/\allowbreak{}tracing/\allowbreak{}langfuse\_\allowbreak{}layer.\allowbreak{}rs:\allowbreak{}L155-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L157}; \texttt{g/\allowbreak{}posthog.\allowbreak{}rs:\allowbreak{}L17-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L50}
\item \textbf{Resilience:} model interface, context and control flow: provider retries, reactive compaction on context overflow, empty-response retries, recipe \texttt{RetryOperation} with success checks and an on-failure command, cancellation tokens checked between steps. \texttt{sm/\allowbreak{}ops\_\allowbreak{}retry.\allowbreak{}rs:\allowbreak{}L227-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L266}; \texttt{sm/\allowbreak{}ops\_\allowbreak{}compaction.\allowbreak{}rs:\allowbreak{}L203-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L218}; \texttt{ga/\allowbreak{}machine.\allowbreak{}rs:\allowbreak{}L159-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L171}
\item \textbf{Performance:} output and context: concurrent tool streams, streamed provider responses, prompt-cache headers, tool-pair summarization, session naming done by a background task. \texttt{g/\allowbreak{}agents/\allowbreak{}agent.\allowbreak{}rs:\allowbreak{}L662-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L682}; \texttt{gpt/\allowbreak{}formats/\allowbreak{}anthropic.\allowbreak{}rs:\allowbreak{}L126-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L134}
\item \textbf{Evaluation:} \texttt{evals/\allowbreak{}harbor} runs terminal-bench-2 style benchmarks across models and builds; \texttt{goose-\allowbreak{}self-\allowbreak{}test.\allowbreak{}yaml} is a self-testing recipe; the state machine has its own test module. \texttt{evals/\allowbreak{}harbor/\allowbreak{}README.\allowbreak{}md:\allowbreak{}L1-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L14}; \texttt{goose-\allowbreak{}self-\allowbreak{}test.\allowbreak{}yaml:\allowbreak{}L1-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L12}; \texttt{sm/\allowbreak{}mod.\allowbreak{}rs:\allowbreak{}L36-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L37}
\end{itemize}

\subsubsection*{Hard to place}
\begin{itemize}
\item \textbf{Toolshim:} for models without native tool calling, the text reply is sent to a separate interpreter model that extracts tool calls, and the system prompt is rewritten to ask for JSON; this is a second model call that produces the wire format, so it sits in the model interface with a context side effect, not a routing decision. \texttt{g/\allowbreak{}providers/\allowbreak{}toolshim.\allowbreak{}rs:\allowbreak{}L1-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L12}; \texttt{g/\allowbreak{}agents/\allowbreak{}reply\_\allowbreak{}parts.\allowbreak{}rs:\allowbreak{}L248-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L257}
\item \textbf{Permission judge:} an LLM classifies pending tool calls as read-only to auto-approve them; the decision is a harness-consumed approval (control flow) made by a model call. \texttt{g/\allowbreak{}permission/\allowbreak{}permission\_\allowbreak{}judge.\allowbreak{}rs:\allowbreak{}L145-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L158}
\item \textbf{Operations as bundles:} every control-flow operation also contributes \texttt{prompt\_\allowbreak{}parts}, \texttt{moim\_\allowbreak{}parts} and \texttt{inference\_\allowbreak{}tools} to the request, so one object mixes control flow, context and the tool set and must be decomposed per method. \texttt{ga/\allowbreak{}machine.\allowbreak{}rs:\allowbreak{}L143-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L155}; \texttt{ga/\allowbreak{}operation.\allowbreak{}rs:\allowbreak{}L89-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L139}
\item \textbf{Providers that run their own loop:} \texttt{ProviderDef:\allowbreak{}:\allowbreak{}RUNS\_\allowbreak{}OWN\_\allowbreak{}TOOL\_\allowbreak{}LOOP} hands the session's MCP servers to an external agent (ACP/CLI wrappers), and \texttt{manages\_\allowbreak{}own\_\allowbreak{}context} disables compaction; output and control flow then live outside this harness, so it degrades to input plus a thin model interface. \texttt{g/\allowbreak{}providers/\allowbreak{}base.\allowbreak{}rs:\allowbreak{}L40-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L42}; \texttt{g/\allowbreak{}agents/\allowbreak{}state\_\allowbreak{}machine\_\allowbreak{}builder.\allowbreak{}rs:\allowbreak{}L66}, \texttt{L89-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L96}
\end{itemize}

\subsubsection*{Summary}
\begin{longtable}{@{}p{0.28\linewidth}p{0.66\linewidth}@{}}
\toprule \textbf{Primitive} & \textbf{Design decision} \\ \midrule \endhead
Input & User message persisted first; steer queue and elicitation answers; tool results batched into one message \\
Context & Templated system prompt plus hints, per-turn context event, 0.8 threshold summarization, tool-pair summaries, prompt caching \\
Model interface & Provider trait with required stream; reqwest SSE; 3 retries exponential; empty-response retries; no backup model \\
Output & All tools are MCP extensions; concurrent select\_all execution; optional Docker container; 50k/200k output spill \\
Control flow & Ordered operation pipeline with one inference step; 1000-turn cap; approval wait and rerun; stop hook; foreground sub-agents \\
\bottomrule \end{longtable}


\subsection{OpenAI Agents SDK (Python)}

\textbf{Source:} https://github.com/openai/openai-agents-python $\cdot$ \textbf{Version:} commit 17d994bd3045dae71acda676a4ef4762ed8ecd94 (2026-10-15) $\cdot$ \textbf{Language:} Python
\textbf{Path conventions:} \texttt{a/\allowbreak{}} = \texttt{src/\allowbreak{}agents/\allowbreak{}}; \texttt{ri/\allowbreak{}} = \texttt{src/\allowbreak{}agents/\allowbreak{}run\_\allowbreak{}internal/\allowbreak{}}; \texttt{m/\allowbreak{}} = \texttt{src/\allowbreak{}agents/\allowbreak{}models/\allowbreak{}}. All paths relative to the repo root.
\textbf{What it is:} The Agents SDK is OpenAI's library for building agents as \texttt{Agent} objects (instructions, tools, handoffs, guardrails) run by a \texttt{Runner} that loops model call, tool execution and handoff until a final output. The canonical shipped agent is an \texttt{Agent} with \texttt{@function\_\allowbreak{}tool} tools run via \texttt{Runner.\allowbreak{}run}/\texttt{run\_\allowbreak{}streamed}. The model is reached through the OpenAI Python SDK, by default the Responses API over HTTPS (optionally a Responses websocket), with Chat Completions, LiteLLM and any-llm providers selectable by model-name prefix.

\subsubsection*{Control flow}
\begin{itemize}
\item \textbf{Run loop:} \texttt{AgentRunner.\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}run\_\allowbreak{}impl} runs \texttt{while True}, calls \texttt{run\_\allowbreak{}single\_\allowbreak{}turn} once per iteration, then branches on \texttt{turn\_\allowbreak{}result.\allowbreak{}next\_\allowbreak{}step}: \texttt{NextStepRunAgain} continues, \texttt{NextStepFinalOutput} runs output guardrails and returns, \texttt{NextStepHandoff} swaps \texttt{current\_\allowbreak{}agent} and loops, \texttt{NextStepInterruption} returns a result holding pending approvals. \texttt{a/\allowbreak{}run.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1032-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1062}, \texttt{a/\allowbreak{}run.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1331-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1372}, \texttt{a/\allowbreak{}run.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1543-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1552}; \texttt{ri/\allowbreak{}run\_\allowbreak{}steps.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L164-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L190}
\item \textbf{Stop rule and turn budget:} a response with no tool calls or handoffs is the final output; after tools ran, \texttt{check\_\allowbreak{}for\_\allowbreak{}final\_\allowbreak{}output\_\allowbreak{}from\_\allowbreak{}tools} applies \texttt{Agent.\allowbreak{}tool\_\allowbreak{}use\_\allowbreak{}behavior} (\texttt{"run\_\allowbreak{}llm\_\allowbreak{}again"} default, \texttt{"stop\_\allowbreak{}on\_\allowbreak{}first\_\allowbreak{}tool"}, a StopAtTools name list, or a callable); \texttt{current\_\allowbreak{}turn += 1} and \texttt{MaxTurnsExceeded} once \texttt{current\_\allowbreak{}turn > max\_\allowbreak{}turns} (\texttt{DEFAULT\_\allowbreak{}MAX\_\allowbreak{}TURNS = 10}). \texttt{ri/\allowbreak{}turn\_\allowbreak{}resolution.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L776-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L809}; \texttt{a/\allowbreak{}agent.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L396-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L418}; \texttt{a/\allowbreak{}run.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1597-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1606}; \texttt{a/\allowbreak{}run\_\allowbreak{}config.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L45}; \texttt{a/\allowbreak{}exceptions.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L354-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L363}
\item \textbf{Guardrails the harness consumes:} input guardrails run on turn 0 only (sequential ones before the model call, parallel ones gathered with the model task), output guardrails run on the final output; a tripwire raises \texttt{InputGuardrailTripwireTriggered} / \texttt{OutputGuardrailTripwireTriggered} and ends the run. \texttt{a/\allowbreak{}run.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1046-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1062}, \texttt{a/\allowbreak{}run.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1833-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1845}; \texttt{ri/\allowbreak{}guardrails.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L116-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L170}, \texttt{ri/\allowbreak{}guardrails.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L172-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L225}; \texttt{a/\allowbreak{}guardrail.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L72-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L100}
\item \textbf{Approval gate:} \texttt{function\_\allowbreak{}needs\_\allowbreak{}approval} evaluates \texttt{FunctionTool.\allowbreak{}needs\_\allowbreak{}approval} before execution; unapproved calls become \texttt{ToolApprovalItem}s inside \texttt{NextStepInterruption}, the caller calls \texttt{RunState.\allowbreak{}approve}/\texttt{reject} and resumes by passing the \texttt{RunState} back as \texttt{input} to \texttt{Runner.\allowbreak{}run}. \texttt{ri/\allowbreak{}tool\_\allowbreak{}execution.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1306-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1315}; \texttt{ri/\allowbreak{}run\_\allowbreak{}steps.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L179-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L190}; \texttt{a/\allowbreak{}run\_\allowbreak{}state.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1380-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1410}; \texttt{a/\allowbreak{}run.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L279-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L300}
\item \textbf{Handoffs and sub-agents:} a \texttt{Handoff} is a function tool whose invocation returns the target \texttt{Agent}; \texttt{execute\_\allowbreak{}handoffs} calls \texttt{on\_\allowbreak{}invoke\_\allowbreak{}handoff}, applies \texttt{input\_\allowbreak{}filter} or \texttt{nest\_\allowbreak{}handoff\_\allowbreak{}history}, and returns \texttt{NextStepHandoff}, so the same loop continues with the new agent (one hop per turn, same run, same session); \texttt{Agent.\allowbreak{}as\_\allowbreak{}tool} instead runs a nested \texttt{Runner.\allowbreak{}run} with its own \texttt{max\_\allowbreak{}turns} and returns its output as a tool result. \texttt{a/\allowbreak{}handoffs/\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}init\_\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L126-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L182}, \texttt{a/\allowbreak{}handoffs/\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}init\_\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L320-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L350}; \texttt{ri/\allowbreak{}turn\_\allowbreak{}resolution.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L544-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L600}, \texttt{ri/\allowbreak{}turn\_\allowbreak{}resolution.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L605-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L690}; \texttt{a/\allowbreak{}agent.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L606-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L640}
\end{itemize}

\subsubsection*{Input}
\begin{itemize}
\item \textbf{Caller input:} \texttt{Runner.\allowbreak{}run(\allowbreak{}starting\_\allowbreak{}agent, input, .\allowbreak{}.\allowbreak{}.\allowbreak{})} accepts a string, a list of Responses input items, or a \texttt{RunState} to resume; a string is normalized to one user message by \texttt{input\_\allowbreak{}to\_\allowbreak{}new\_\allowbreak{}input\_\allowbreak{}list}. \texttt{a/\allowbreak{}run.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L279-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L300}; \texttt{a/\allowbreak{}items.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L813-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L826}
\item \textbf{Tool results re-enter:} each executed call becomes a \texttt{ToolCallOutputItem} (\texttt{function\_\allowbreak{}call\_\allowbreak{}output} keyed by \texttt{call\_\allowbreak{}id}) in \texttt{generated\_\allowbreak{}items}; \texttt{\_\allowbreak{}prepare\_\allowbreak{}turn\_\allowbreak{}input\_\allowbreak{}items} converts caller input plus generated items into the next turn's input list. \texttt{a/\allowbreak{}items.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L431}, \texttt{a/\allowbreak{}items.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L845-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L860}; \texttt{ri/\allowbreak{}run\_\allowbreak{}loop.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L349-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L358}
\item \textbf{Session input:} \texttt{prepare\_\allowbreak{}input\_\allowbreak{}with\_\allowbreak{}session} loads \texttt{session.\allowbreak{}get\_\allowbreak{}items(\allowbreak{})} history and concatenates the new input (or lets \texttt{session\_\allowbreak{}input\_\allowbreak{}callback} merge them); with a server-managed conversation only the new items are prepared. \texttt{ri/\allowbreak{}session\_\allowbreak{}persistence.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L424-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L510}; \texttt{a/\allowbreak{}run.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L716-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L745}
\item \textbf{Streamed input events:} \texttt{run\_\allowbreak{}streamed} puts each raw model event on an event queue as \texttt{RawResponsesStreamEvent} (receiving the stream is model interface; the queue is the harness's input side of rendering). \texttt{ri/\allowbreak{}run\_\allowbreak{}loop.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L2621-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L2652}; \texttt{a/\allowbreak{}stream\_\allowbreak{}events.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L11-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L60}
\end{itemize}

\subsubsection*{Context}
\begin{itemize}
\item \textbf{Instructions:} \texttt{Agent.\allowbreak{}get\_\allowbreak{}system\_\allowbreak{}prompt} returns the static \texttt{instructions} string or calls \texttt{instructions(\allowbreak{}context, agent)} (sync or async); \texttt{get\_\allowbreak{}prompt} resolves a server-side prompt object; both are gathered at the start of \texttt{run\_\allowbreak{}single\_\allowbreak{}turn}. \texttt{a/\allowbreak{}agent.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1129-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1170}; \texttt{ri/\allowbreak{}run\_\allowbreak{}loop.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L2838-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L2841}
\item \textbf{Tool and handoff descriptions:} \texttt{get\_\allowbreak{}all\_\allowbreak{}tools} merges MCP server tools with function tools filtered by \texttt{is\_\allowbreak{}enabled}; handoffs are listed as function tools; name collisions resolved per \texttt{tool\_\allowbreak{}name\_\allowbreak{}collision\_\allowbreak{}policy}. \texttt{a/\allowbreak{}agent.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L286-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L316}; \texttt{ri/\allowbreak{}run\_\allowbreak{}loop.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L2836-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L2850}
\item \textbf{Per-call assembly and filter:} the request is instructions plus caller input plus all run items so far; \texttt{call\_\allowbreak{}model\_\allowbreak{}input\_\allowbreak{}filter} may rewrite instructions and input, then items are de-duplicated before \texttt{get\_\allowbreak{}model}. \texttt{ri/\allowbreak{}run\_\allowbreak{}loop.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L2946-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L2956}; \texttt{ri/\allowbreak{}turn\_\allowbreak{}preparation.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L51-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L95}
\item \textbf{Session history and server-held conversations:} the \texttt{Session} protocol (\texttt{get\_\allowbreak{}items}/\texttt{add\_\allowbreak{}items}/\texttt{pop\_\allowbreak{}item}) is implemented by \texttt{SQLiteSession}, SQLAlchemy/Redis/Mongo/Dapr/encrypted extensions, and \texttt{OpenAIConversationsSession}; with \texttt{conversation\_\allowbreak{}id} or \texttt{previous\_\allowbreak{}response\_\allowbreak{}id}, \texttt{OpenAIServerConversationTracker.\allowbreak{}prepare\_\allowbreak{}input} sends only new deltas and records the last \texttt{response\_\allowbreak{}id}. \texttt{a/\allowbreak{}memory/\allowbreak{}session.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L53-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L95}; \texttt{a/\allowbreak{}memory/\allowbreak{}sqlite\_\allowbreak{}session.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L18-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L60}; \texttt{a/\allowbreak{}memory/\allowbreak{}openai\_\allowbreak{}conversations\_\allowbreak{}session.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L29-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L60}; \texttt{ri/\allowbreak{}oai\_\allowbreak{}conversation.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L124-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L140}, \texttt{ri/\allowbreak{}oai\_\allowbreak{}conversation.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L350-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L393}
\item \textbf{Window fitting:} \texttt{OpenAIResponsesCompactionSession} calls \texttt{responses.\allowbreak{}compact} after a write when at least 10 candidate items exist (default trigger); \texttt{ToolOutputTrimmer} replaces tool outputs over 500 chars from older turns with a preview; \texttt{model\_\allowbreak{}settings.\allowbreak{}truncation="auto"} delegates truncation to the server. \texttt{a/\allowbreak{}memory/\allowbreak{}openai\_\allowbreak{}responses\_\allowbreak{}compaction\_\allowbreak{}session.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L68-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L71}, \texttt{a/\allowbreak{}memory/\allowbreak{}openai\_\allowbreak{}responses\_\allowbreak{}compaction\_\allowbreak{}session.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L92-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L170}; \texttt{ri/\allowbreak{}session\_\allowbreak{}persistence.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L713-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L735}; \texttt{a/\allowbreak{}extensions/\allowbreak{}tool\_\allowbreak{}output\_\allowbreak{}trimmer.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L88-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L115}; \texttt{a/\allowbreak{}model\_\allowbreak{}settings.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L123}
\end{itemize}

\subsubsection*{Model interface}
\begin{itemize}
\item \textbf{Client:} the \texttt{Model} ABC exposes \texttt{get\_\allowbreak{}response} and \texttt{stream\_\allowbreak{}response}; \texttt{OpenAIResponsesModel} builds kwargs and calls \texttt{client.\allowbreak{}responses.\allowbreak{}create(\allowbreak{}**kwargs)} (or a Responses websocket), \texttt{OpenAIChatCompletionsModel} calls \texttt{chat.\allowbreak{}completions.\allowbreak{}create}; \texttt{MultiProvider} maps the model-name prefix (\texttt{openai/\allowbreak{}} or none, \texttt{litellm/\allowbreak{}}, \texttt{any-\allowbreak{}llm/\allowbreak{}}) to a \texttt{ModelProvider}. \texttt{m/\allowbreak{}interface.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L37-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L136}; \texttt{m/\allowbreak{}openai\_\allowbreak{}responses.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L513}, \texttt{m/\allowbreak{}openai\_\allowbreak{}responses.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L590-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L640}, \texttt{m/\allowbreak{}openai\_\allowbreak{}responses.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L860-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L900}; \texttt{m/\allowbreak{}openai\_\allowbreak{}chatcompletions.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L55}, \texttt{m/\allowbreak{}openai\_\allowbreak{}chatcompletions.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L742}; \texttt{m/\allowbreak{}multi\_\allowbreak{}provider.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L62-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L75}, \texttt{m/\allowbreak{}multi\_\allowbreak{}provider.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L155-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L200}; \texttt{ri/\allowbreak{}turn\_\allowbreak{}preparation.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L134-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L145}
\item \textbf{Retries and timeout:} \texttt{get\_\allowbreak{}response\_\allowbreak{}with\_\allowbreak{}retry} wraps the call with \texttt{model\_\allowbreak{}settings.\allowbreak{}retry} (\texttt{ModelRetrySettings} max\_retries, backoff), provider advice from \texttt{get\_\allowbreak{}openai\_\allowbreak{}retry\_\allowbreak{}advice} (honours Retry-After), exponential backoff with jitter, and \texttt{model\_\allowbreak{}settings.\allowbreak{}timeout} raising \texttt{ModelTimeoutError}; a rewind hook restores session and tracker state before replay. \texttt{ri/\allowbreak{}run\_\allowbreak{}loop.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L3024-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L3047}; \texttt{ri/\allowbreak{}model\_\allowbreak{}retry.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L175-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L205}, \texttt{ri/\allowbreak{}model\_\allowbreak{}retry.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L389-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L420}, \texttt{ri/\allowbreak{}model\_\allowbreak{}retry.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L574}; \texttt{a/\allowbreak{}retry.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L210-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L231}; \texttt{m/\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}openai\_\allowbreak{}retry.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L41-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L112}; \texttt{a/\allowbreak{}model\_\allowbreak{}settings.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L188}, \texttt{a/\allowbreak{}model\_\allowbreak{}settings.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L215}
\item \textbf{Streaming:} \texttt{stream\_\allowbreak{}response\_\allowbreak{}with\_\allowbreak{}retry} consumes the provider's event stream and yields raw \texttt{TResponseStreamEvent}s, with usage added to \texttt{context\_\allowbreak{}wrapper.\allowbreak{}usage} at the end. \texttt{ri/\allowbreak{}run\_\allowbreak{}loop.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L2621-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L2652}; \texttt{m/\allowbreak{}openai\_\allowbreak{}responses.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L683-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L720}; \texttt{ri/\allowbreak{}run\_\allowbreak{}loop.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L3065}
\item \textbf{Tool-call format:} \texttt{Converter.\allowbreak{}convert\_\allowbreak{}tools} emits Responses \texttt{\{"type":\allowbreak{} "function", "strict":\allowbreak{} .\allowbreak{}.\allowbreak{}.\allowbreak{}\}} entries for function tools and handoffs, plus hosted tool configs; output items are Responses \texttt{function\_\allowbreak{}call} items. \texttt{m/\allowbreak{}openai\_\allowbreak{}responses.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L2115-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L2251}, \texttt{m/\allowbreak{}openai\_\allowbreak{}responses.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L2360-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L2370}
\end{itemize}

\subsubsection*{Output}
\begin{itemize}
\item \textbf{Tool set:} \texttt{FunctionTool} (name, description, JSON schema, \texttt{on\_\allowbreak{}invoke\_\allowbreak{}tool}, \texttt{strict\_\allowbreak{}json\_\allowbreak{}schema}, \texttt{needs\_\allowbreak{}approval}, \texttt{timeout}, tool guardrails) built by \texttt{@function\_\allowbreak{}tool}; hosted tools (\texttt{WebSearchTool}, \texttt{FileSearchTool}, \texttt{CodeInterpreterTool}, \texttt{HostedMCPTool}, \texttt{ImageGenerationTool}) run inside the provider; \texttt{ComputerTool}, \texttt{ShellTool}, \texttt{ApplyPatchTool}, \texttt{LocalShellTool} run locally. \texttt{a/\allowbreak{}tool.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L455-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L530}, \texttt{a/\allowbreak{}tool.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L2621}, \texttt{a/\allowbreak{}tool.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L815-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1300}
\item \textbf{Response processing:} \texttt{process\_\allowbreak{}model\_\allowbreak{}response} sorts output items into function runs, handoffs, computer actions, shell and apply-patch calls, custom tools and MCP approval requests, then \texttt{execute\_\allowbreak{}tools\_\allowbreak{}and\_\allowbreak{}side\_\allowbreak{}effects} runs the plan. \texttt{ri/\allowbreak{}turn\_\allowbreak{}resolution.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L3133-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L3175}, \texttt{ri/\allowbreak{}turn\_\allowbreak{}resolution.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L941-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L990}
\item \textbf{Executor and parallelism:} \texttt{\_\allowbreak{}FunctionToolBatchExecutor} creates one \texttt{asyncio.\allowbreak{}Task} per function call, bounded by \texttt{tool\_\allowbreak{}execution.\allowbreak{}max\_\allowbreak{}function\_\allowbreak{}tool\_\allowbreak{}concurrency}, isolating sibling failures and applying per-tool timeouts. \texttt{ri/\allowbreak{}tool\_\allowbreak{}execution.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1548-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1600}, \texttt{ri/\allowbreak{}tool\_\allowbreak{}execution.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1648-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1670}, \texttt{ri/\allowbreak{}tool\_\allowbreak{}execution.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L2341-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L2371}; \texttt{a/\allowbreak{}run\_\allowbreak{}config.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L143-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L163}
\item \textbf{Sandbox:} \texttt{SandboxAgent} plus \texttt{RunConfig(\allowbreak{}sandbox=.\allowbreak{}.\allowbreak{}.\allowbreak{})} run shell, file and patch tools inside a \texttt{DockerSandboxSession} or a \texttt{UnixLocalSandboxSession} (asyncio subprocess with PTY). \texttt{a/\allowbreak{}sandbox/\allowbreak{}sandbox\_\allowbreak{}agent.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L31-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L60}; \texttt{a/\allowbreak{}sandbox/\allowbreak{}sandboxes/\allowbreak{}docker.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L287}; \texttt{a/\allowbreak{}sandbox/\allowbreak{}sandboxes/\allowbreak{}unix\_\allowbreak{}local.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L261}
\item \textbf{Result presentation:} \texttt{RunResult.\allowbreak{}final\_\allowbreak{}output} and \texttt{RunResultStreaming.\allowbreak{}stream\_\allowbreak{}events(\allowbreak{})} (\texttt{RawResponsesStreamEvent}, \texttt{RunItemStreamEvent}, \texttt{AgentUpdatedStreamEvent}); shell outputs are truncated to \texttt{max\_\allowbreak{}output\_\allowbreak{}length} before being shown to the model. \texttt{a/\allowbreak{}result.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L547}, \texttt{a/\allowbreak{}result.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L660}, \texttt{a/\allowbreak{}result.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1005}; \texttt{a/\allowbreak{}stream\_\allowbreak{}events.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L11-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L60}; \texttt{ri/\allowbreak{}tool\_\allowbreak{}execution.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1008-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1047}
\end{itemize}

\subsubsection*{Production concerns}
\begin{itemize}
\item \textbf{Persistence:} context: session history written by \texttt{save\_\allowbreak{}result\_\allowbreak{}to\_\allowbreak{}session} to any \texttt{Session} store (SQLite, SQLAlchemy, Redis, MongoDB, Dapr, encrypted wrapper, OpenAI Conversations); control flow: \texttt{RunState.\allowbreak{}to\_\allowbreak{}json}/\texttt{from\_\allowbreak{}json} serialize an interrupted run for resume after approvals. \texttt{ri/\allowbreak{}session\_\allowbreak{}persistence.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L778-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L800}; \texttt{a/\allowbreak{}extensions/\allowbreak{}memory/\allowbreak{}sqlalchemy\_\allowbreak{}session.\allowbreak{}py}; \texttt{a/\allowbreak{}run\_\allowbreak{}state.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L842}, \texttt{a/\allowbreak{}run\_\allowbreak{}state.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1899}, \texttt{a/\allowbreak{}run\_\allowbreak{}state.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L2423}
\item \textbf{Safety \& security:} control flow: \texttt{needs\_\allowbreak{}approval} gate and input/output guardrails; output: tool input/output guardrails with \texttt{allow}/\texttt{reject\_\allowbreak{}content}/\texttt{raise\_\allowbreak{}exception} behaviours, Docker sandbox for shell and patches, mount-credential redaction in errors; no prompt-injection fencing of tool output found. \texttt{a/\allowbreak{}tool\_\allowbreak{}guardrails.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L60-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L118}; \texttt{a/\allowbreak{}guardrail.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L72-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L100}; \texttt{a/\allowbreak{}sandbox/\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}mount\_\allowbreak{}security.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L487-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L511}
\item \textbf{Observability:} tracing spans (\texttt{agent\_\allowbreak{}span}, \texttt{generation\_\allowbreak{}span}, \texttt{function\_\allowbreak{}span}, \texttt{handoff\_\allowbreak{}span}, \texttt{guardrail\_\allowbreak{}span}) batched by \texttt{BatchTraceProcessor} and posted by \texttt{BackendSpanExporter} to \texttt{api.\allowbreak{}openai.\allowbreak{}com/\allowbreak{}v1/\allowbreak{}traces/\allowbreak{}ingest} with 3 retries; \texttt{Usage} sums requests and input/output tokens per run; \texttt{RunHooks}/\texttt{AgentHooks} expose lifecycle callbacks. \texttt{a/\allowbreak{}tracing/\allowbreak{}create.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L89-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L123}, \texttt{a/\allowbreak{}tracing/\allowbreak{}create.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L155-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L235}; \texttt{a/\allowbreak{}tracing/\allowbreak{}processors.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L45-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L90}, \texttt{a/\allowbreak{}tracing/\allowbreak{}processors.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L698-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L771}; \texttt{a/\allowbreak{}usage.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L111-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L141}; \texttt{a/\allowbreak{}lifecycle.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L13-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L118}
\item \textbf{Resilience:} model interface: retry policy, timeout, request rewind; output: \texttt{ToolTimeoutError} per tool; control flow: \texttt{MaxTurnsExceeded} handler via run error handlers, resumable \texttt{RunState}, session item rewind on failed writes. \texttt{ri/\allowbreak{}model\_\allowbreak{}retry.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L574}; \texttt{a/\allowbreak{}exceptions.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L387}, \texttt{a/\allowbreak{}exceptions.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L417}; \texttt{a/\allowbreak{}run.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1628}; \texttt{ri/\allowbreak{}session\_\allowbreak{}persistence.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1622}
\item \textbf{Performance:} streaming, concurrent tool tasks, parallel guardrails gathered with the model call, \texttt{PromptCacheKeyResolver} deriving \texttt{prompt\_\allowbreak{}cache\_\allowbreak{}key} from session or group id, \texttt{prompt\_\allowbreak{}cache\_\allowbreak{}retention}, \texttt{ToolOutputTrimmer}. \texttt{ri/\allowbreak{}prompt\_\allowbreak{}cache\_\allowbreak{}key.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L17-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L90}; \texttt{a/\allowbreak{}model\_\allowbreak{}settings.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L150}; \texttt{a/\allowbreak{}run.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1841}
\item \textbf{Evaluation:} 172 pytest files under \texttt{tests/\allowbreak{}} with a \texttt{ScriptedModel} double that scripts model turns; no agent-eval harness (datasets, graders) found in the SDK. \texttt{src/\allowbreak{}agents/\allowbreak{}testing/\allowbreak{}model.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L249}; \texttt{pyproject.\allowbreak{}toml:\allowbreak{}L256-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L259}
\end{itemize}

\subsubsection*{Hard to place}
\begin{itemize}
\item \textbf{Server-managed conversation:} with \texttt{conversation\_\allowbreak{}id}/\texttt{previous\_\allowbreak{}response\_\allowbreak{}id} the provider keeps the transcript; the harness's context primitive only sends deltas and the provider does assembly, so part of Context lives outside the harness. \texttt{ri/\allowbreak{}oai\_\allowbreak{}conversation.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L518-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L630}
\item \textbf{Hosted tools:} \texttt{WebSearchTool}, \texttt{CodeInterpreterTool}, \texttt{HostedMCPTool} execute inside the provider during the model call; the harness declares them (context) and reads results from the response (model interface), with no local Output step. \texttt{a/\allowbreak{}tool.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L853}, \texttt{a/\allowbreak{}tool.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1158}, \texttt{a/\allowbreak{}tool.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1189}
\item \textbf{Server-side compaction:} \texttt{OpenAIResponsesCompactionSession} fits the window (context) by calling \texttt{responses.\allowbreak{}compact}, an extra provider request issued from a session write rather than from the loop. \texttt{a/\allowbreak{}memory/\allowbreak{}openai\_\allowbreak{}responses\_\allowbreak{}compaction\_\allowbreak{}session.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L92-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L170}
\item \textbf{\texttt{tool\_\allowbreak{}use\_\allowbreak{}behavior} callable:} lives on \texttt{Agent} and receives tool results (output) but decides whether to go again (control flow); assigned by function. \texttt{ri/\allowbreak{}turn\_\allowbreak{}resolution.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L776-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L809}
\item \textbf{Sandbox memory generation:} \texttt{SandboxMemoryGenerationManager} turns rollouts into memory for the model (context) by issuing its own model calls (control flow) outside the main loop. \texttt{a/\allowbreak{}sandbox/\allowbreak{}memory/\allowbreak{}manager.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L43}
\end{itemize}

\subsubsection*{Summary}
\begin{longtable}{@{}p{0.28\linewidth}p{0.66\linewidth}@{}}
\toprule \textbf{Primitive} & \textbf{Design decision} \\ \midrule \endhead
Input & String or Responses items plus Session history; tool outputs re-enter as run items; RunState resumes \\
Context & Instructions callable, tools and handoffs as functions, full item list; server compaction and trimmer \\
Model interface & OpenAI Responses API via SDK; prefix-routed providers; configurable retry, timeout, streaming \\
Output & Function tools as concurrent asyncio tasks; hosted tools; Docker or local sandbox for shell and patches \\
Control flow & NextStep loop with max\_turns=10; guardrail tripwires; approval interruptions; handoffs swap agent in place \\
\bottomrule \end{longtable}


\subsection{Google Agent Development Kit (Python)}

\textbf{Source:} https://github.com/google/adk-python $\cdot$ \textbf{Version:} commit f78149a97e109e9c947ade06e3a9ab8a45385e0e (2026-10-15) $\cdot$ \textbf{Language:} Python
\textbf{Path conventions:} \texttt{adk/\allowbreak{}} = \texttt{src/\allowbreak{}google/\allowbreak{}adk/\allowbreak{}}; \texttt{fl/\allowbreak{}} = \texttt{src/\allowbreak{}google/\allowbreak{}adk/\allowbreak{}flows/\allowbreak{}llm\_\allowbreak{}flows/\allowbreak{}}. All paths relative to the repo root.
\textbf{What it is:} ADK is Google's framework for building agents as a tree of \texttt{BaseAgent}s (an \texttt{LlmAgent} with tools and sub-agents, plus \texttt{SequentialAgent}, \texttt{ParallelAgent}, \texttt{LoopAgent} workflow agents) executed by a \texttt{Runner} over a \texttt{Session} of \texttt{Event}s held by a session service. The canonical agent is an \texttt{LlmAgent} with \texttt{FunctionTool}s run by \texttt{Runner.\allowbreak{}run\_\allowbreak{}async}. The model is reached through \texttt{BaseLlm} adapters: \texttt{Gemini} over the \texttt{google-\allowbreak{}genai} client (Gemini API or Vertex AI), with Claude (\texttt{AnthropicLlm}) and LiteLLM providers resolved by model name.

\subsubsection*{Control flow}
\begin{itemize}
\item \textbf{Runner loop:} \texttt{Runner.\allowbreak{}run\_\allowbreak{}async} gets or creates the session, appends the user event, picks \texttt{agent\_\allowbreak{}to\_\allowbreak{}run} (\texttt{\_\allowbreak{}find\_\allowbreak{}agent\_\allowbreak{}to\_\allowbreak{}run}: the agent whose pending function call the message answers, else the last-replying transferable \texttt{LlmAgent}, else root), then iterates \texttt{active\_\allowbreak{}agent.\allowbreak{}run\_\allowbreak{}async(\allowbreak{}ctx)} inside \texttt{\_\allowbreak{}exec\_\allowbreak{}with\_\allowbreak{}plugin}, appending every non-partial event to the session, and runs post-invocation compaction. \texttt{adk/\allowbreak{}runners.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1124-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1136}, \texttt{adk/\allowbreak{}runners.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1190-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1215}, \texttt{adk/\allowbreak{}runners.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1336-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1370}, \texttt{adk/\allowbreak{}runners.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1815-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1846}, \texttt{adk/\allowbreak{}runners.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1531-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1557}
\item \textbf{Flow loop and stop rule:} \texttt{BaseLlmFlow.\allowbreak{}run\_\allowbreak{}async} runs \texttt{while True:\allowbreak{} \_\allowbreak{}run\_\allowbreak{}one\_\allowbreak{}step\_\allowbreak{}async} (request processors, one model call, response processors, function calls) and breaks when the step's last event \texttt{is\_\allowbreak{}final\_\allowbreak{}response(\allowbreak{})} (no function calls or responses, not partial, or a long-running tool id) or the context is aborted or \texttt{end\_\allowbreak{}invocation} is set. \texttt{fl/\allowbreak{}base\_\allowbreak{}llm\_\allowbreak{}flow.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L332-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L353}, \texttt{fl/\allowbreak{}base\_\allowbreak{}llm\_\allowbreak{}flow.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L377-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L450}; \texttt{adk/\allowbreak{}events/\allowbreak{}event.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L286-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L309}
\item \textbf{Budgets:} \texttt{increment\_\allowbreak{}llm\_\allowbreak{}call\_\allowbreak{}count} runs before every model call and raises \texttt{LlmCallsLimitExceededError} once calls exceed \texttt{RunConfig.\allowbreak{}max\_\allowbreak{}llm\_\allowbreak{}calls} (default read from \texttt{ADK\_\allowbreak{}MAX\_\allowbreak{}LLM\_\allowbreak{}CALLS}); \texttt{LoopAgent.\allowbreak{}max\_\allowbreak{}iterations} bounds workflow loops, and an \texttt{escalate} action exits them. \texttt{fl/\allowbreak{}core/\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}model\_\allowbreak{}call.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L227}; \texttt{adk/\allowbreak{}agents/\allowbreak{}invocation\_\allowbreak{}context.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L63-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L96}, \texttt{adk/\allowbreak{}agents/\allowbreak{}invocation\_\allowbreak{}context.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L596-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L609}; \texttt{adk/\allowbreak{}agents/\allowbreak{}run\_\allowbreak{}config.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L41-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L45}, \texttt{adk/\allowbreak{}agents/\allowbreak{}run\_\allowbreak{}config.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L264-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L282}; \texttt{adk/\allowbreak{}agents/\allowbreak{}loop\_\allowbreak{}agent.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L92-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L130}
\item \textbf{Gates the harness consumes:} plugin then agent \texttt{before\_\allowbreak{}model\_\allowbreak{}callback}/\texttt{before\_\allowbreak{}tool\_\allowbreak{}callback} may return a replacement and short-circuit (first non-None wins); a tool whose \texttt{check\_\allowbreak{}require\_\allowbreak{}confirmation} is true emits an \texttt{adk\_\allowbreak{}request\_\allowbreak{}confirmation} long-running call that pauses the invocation until a \texttt{ToolConfirmation} function response arrives, which the confirmation request processor replays. \texttt{fl/\allowbreak{}core/\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}finalizer.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L150-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L195}; \texttt{adk/\allowbreak{}plugins/\allowbreak{}plugin\_\allowbreak{}manager.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L277-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L325}; \texttt{adk/\allowbreak{}tools/\allowbreak{}function\_\allowbreak{}tool.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L404-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L430}; \texttt{fl/\allowbreak{}tools/\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}confirmation.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L259-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L300}; \texttt{adk/\allowbreak{}agents/\allowbreak{}invocation\_\allowbreak{}context.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L745-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L770}
\item \textbf{Sub-agents:} \texttt{AutoFlow} adds a \texttt{transfer\_\allowbreak{}to\_\allowbreak{}agent} tool and instructions listing peers and parent; the tool sets \texttt{actions.\allowbreak{}transfer\_\allowbreak{}to\_\allowbreak{}agent} and the postprocessor runs the target's \texttt{run\_\allowbreak{}async} in the same invocation and session; \texttt{AgentTool} instead spawns a nested \texttt{Runner} with in-memory services and returns its last text as a tool result; \texttt{SequentialAgent}, \texttt{ParallelAgent} (one \texttt{TaskGroup} task and branch per child) and \texttt{LoopAgent} sequence children without a model. \texttt{fl/\allowbreak{}extensions/\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}agent\_\allowbreak{}transfer.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L44-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L98}; \texttt{fl/\allowbreak{}core/\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}function\_\allowbreak{}call\_\allowbreak{}postprocessor.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L72-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L140}; \texttt{adk/\allowbreak{}tools/\allowbreak{}agent\_\allowbreak{}tool.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L224-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L300}; \texttt{adk/\allowbreak{}agents/\allowbreak{}parallel\_\allowbreak{}agent.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L134-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L136}, \texttt{adk/\allowbreak{}agents/\allowbreak{}parallel\_\allowbreak{}agent.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L176-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L200}; \texttt{adk/\allowbreak{}agents/\allowbreak{}sequential\_\allowbreak{}agent.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L96-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L130}
\end{itemize}

\subsubsection*{Input}
\begin{itemize}
\item \textbf{User message:} \texttt{run\_\allowbreak{}async(\allowbreak{}user\_\allowbreak{}id, session\_\allowbreak{}id, new\_\allowbreak{}message:\allowbreak{} types.\allowbreak{}Content, state\_\allowbreak{}delta, run\_\allowbreak{}config)}; the message becomes an \texttt{Event(\allowbreak{}author='user')} appended through \texttt{session\_\allowbreak{}service.\allowbreak{}append\_\allowbreak{}event}, with input blobs optionally saved as artifacts. \texttt{adk/\allowbreak{}runners.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1124-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1136}, \texttt{adk/\allowbreak{}runners.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L705-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L735}, \texttt{adk/\allowbreak{}runners.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1667-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1700}
\item \textbf{Tool results re-enter:} \texttt{\_\allowbreak{}build\_\allowbreak{}response\_\allowbreak{}event} wraps each tool result as a function-response \texttt{Event}; parallel results are merged into one event by \texttt{merge\_\allowbreak{}parallel\_\allowbreak{}function\_\allowbreak{}response\_\allowbreak{}events}; the event is yielded, appended to the session, and rebuilt into \texttt{contents} by the next step's content processor. \texttt{fl/\allowbreak{}tools/\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}caller.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L321-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L360}; \texttt{fl/\allowbreak{}tools/\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}batch\_\allowbreak{}executor.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L61-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L118}, \texttt{fl/\allowbreak{}tools/\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}batch\_\allowbreak{}executor.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L146-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L170}
\item \textbf{Answers the model consumes:} \texttt{request\_\allowbreak{}input} and \texttt{get\_\allowbreak{}user\_\allowbreak{}choice} are long-running tools that pause the invocation; the user's function response arrives as the next \texttt{new\_\allowbreak{}message} and \texttt{\_\allowbreak{}find\_\allowbreak{}agent\_\allowbreak{}to\_\allowbreak{}run} routes it to the agent that asked. \texttt{adk/\allowbreak{}tools/\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}request\_\allowbreak{}input\_\allowbreak{}tool.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L27-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L56}; \texttt{adk/\allowbreak{}tools/\allowbreak{}get\_\allowbreak{}user\_\allowbreak{}choice\_\allowbreak{}tool.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L23}; \texttt{adk/\allowbreak{}runners.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1815-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1846}
\item \textbf{Resume input:} for a resumable app, \texttt{run\_\allowbreak{}async} with an \texttt{invocation\_\allowbreak{}id} and no message resumes the paused invocation from stored events. \texttt{adk/\allowbreak{}runners.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1254-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1300}; \texttt{adk/\allowbreak{}apps/\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}configs.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L29-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L48}
\end{itemize}

\subsubsection*{Context}
\begin{itemize}
\item \textbf{Ordered request processors:} \texttt{SingleFlow} assembles one \texttt{LlmRequest} (model, \texttt{contents}, \texttt{GenerateContentConfig}, \texttt{tools\_\allowbreak{}dict}) by running basic, auth, request\_confirmation, instructions, identity, compaction, interactions, contents, context\_cache, planning, code\_execution and output\_schema processors in that order. \texttt{fl/\allowbreak{}single\_\allowbreak{}flow.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L42-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L80}; \texttt{fl/\allowbreak{}base\_\allowbreak{}llm\_\allowbreak{}flow.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L451-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L485}; \texttt{adk/\allowbreak{}models/\allowbreak{}llm\_\allowbreak{}request.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L52-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L95}
\item \textbf{Instructions:} \texttt{\_\allowbreak{}build\_\allowbreak{}instructions} appends the root \texttt{global\_\allowbreak{}instruction}, the agent's \texttt{static\_\allowbreak{}instruction}, and \texttt{instruction} (string or \texttt{InstructionProvider}, with \texttt{\{state\_\allowbreak{}key\}} injection from session state) as system instruction, fencing dynamic text between begin/end markers. \texttt{fl/\allowbreak{}prompt/\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}instructions.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L69-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L160}; \texttt{adk/\allowbreak{}agents/\allowbreak{}llm\_\allowbreak{}agent.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L327-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L354}, \texttt{adk/\allowbreak{}agents/\allowbreak{}llm\_\allowbreak{}agent.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L824-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L860}
\item \textbf{Conversation:} \texttt{\_\allowbreak{}ContentLlmRequestProcessor} rebuilds \texttt{contents} from session events filtered by branch and visibility (\texttt{include\_\allowbreak{}contents='default'}) or only the current turn (\texttt{'none'}), rearranging function responses and dropping orphans; \texttt{\_\allowbreak{}maybe\_\allowbreak{}append\_\allowbreak{}user\_\allowbreak{}content} guarantees a trailing user turn. \texttt{fl/\allowbreak{}context/\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}contents.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L116-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L200}, \texttt{fl/\allowbreak{}context/\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}contents.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L473-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L640}; \texttt{adk/\allowbreak{}models/\allowbreak{}base\_\allowbreak{}llm.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L234-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L275}
\item \textbf{Memory:} \texttt{BaseMemoryService.\allowbreak{}search\_\allowbreak{}memory} (in-memory, Vertex AI Memory Bank, Vertex RAG) is reached by \texttt{PreloadMemoryTool.\allowbreak{}process\_\allowbreak{}llm\_\allowbreak{}request}, which injects past conversations into the instructions, or by the \texttt{load\_\allowbreak{}memory} function tool. \texttt{adk/\allowbreak{}memory/\allowbreak{}base\_\allowbreak{}memory\_\allowbreak{}service.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L44-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L141}; \texttt{adk/\allowbreak{}tools/\allowbreak{}preload\_\allowbreak{}memory\_\allowbreak{}tool.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L33-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L75}; \texttt{adk/\allowbreak{}tools/\allowbreak{}load\_\allowbreak{}memory\_\allowbreak{}tool.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L38-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L60}
\item \textbf{Window fitting:} \texttt{EventsCompactionConfig} (sliding-window \texttt{compaction\_\allowbreak{}interval}/\texttt{overlap\_\allowbreak{}size} after an invocation, \texttt{token\_\allowbreak{}threshold} at request time) summarizes events with \texttt{LlmEventSummarizer} into a compaction event that \texttt{\_\allowbreak{}process\_\allowbreak{}compaction\_\allowbreak{}events} substitutes for raw events; \texttt{ContextFilterPlugin} keeps the last N invocations; \texttt{ContextCacheConfig} enables Gemini context caching. \texttt{adk/\allowbreak{}apps/\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}configs.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L50-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L85}; \texttt{adk/\allowbreak{}apps/\allowbreak{}compaction.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L675-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L800}; \texttt{adk/\allowbreak{}apps/\allowbreak{}llm\_\allowbreak{}event\_\allowbreak{}summarizer.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L29-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L160}; \texttt{fl/\allowbreak{}context/\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}compaction.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L33-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L100}; \texttt{adk/\allowbreak{}plugins/\allowbreak{}context\_\allowbreak{}filter\_\allowbreak{}plugin.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L101-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L120}; \texttt{fl/\allowbreak{}context/\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}cache.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L36-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L80}
\end{itemize}

\subsubsection*{Model interface}
\begin{itemize}
\item \textbf{Client:} \texttt{BaseLlm.\allowbreak{}generate\_\allowbreak{}content\_\allowbreak{}async(\allowbreak{}llm\_\allowbreak{}request, stream)} is an async generator of \texttt{LlmResponse}; \texttt{Gemini} calls \texttt{genai.\allowbreak{}Client(\allowbreak{}).\allowbreak{}aio.\allowbreak{}models.\allowbreak{}generate\_\allowbreak{}content} or \texttt{generate\_\allowbreak{}content\_\allowbreak{}stream} (or the Interactions API); \texttt{LLMRegistry.\allowbreak{}resolve} picks the adapter class by model-name regex (Gemini, \texttt{AnthropicLlm} for \texttt{claude-\allowbreak{}*}, \texttt{LiteLlm} for other providers). \texttt{adk/\allowbreak{}models/\allowbreak{}base\_\allowbreak{}llm.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L132-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L160}; \texttt{adk/\allowbreak{}models/\allowbreak{}google\_\allowbreak{}llm.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L99}, \texttt{adk/\allowbreak{}models/\allowbreak{}google\_\allowbreak{}llm.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L228-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L383}; \texttt{adk/\allowbreak{}models/\allowbreak{}registry.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L62-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L84}, \texttt{adk/\allowbreak{}models/\allowbreak{}registry.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L147-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L200}; \texttt{adk/\allowbreak{}models/\allowbreak{}anthropic\_\allowbreak{}llm.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1099}; \texttt{adk/\allowbreak{}models/\allowbreak{}lite\_\allowbreak{}llm.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L3679}
\item \textbf{Streaming:} \texttt{call\_\allowbreak{}llm\_\allowbreak{}async} passes \texttt{stream=True} when \texttt{RunConfig.\allowbreak{}streaming\_\allowbreak{}mode} is \texttt{SSE} and yields partial \texttt{LlmResponse}s as partial events, running \texttt{after\_\allowbreak{}model\_\allowbreak{}callback} on the final one. \texttt{fl/\allowbreak{}core/\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}model\_\allowbreak{}call.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L170-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L310}; \texttt{adk/\allowbreak{}agents/\allowbreak{}run\_\allowbreak{}config.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L145}
\item \textbf{Retries and fallback:} \texttt{Gemini.\allowbreak{}retry\_\allowbreak{}options} (\texttt{types.\allowbreak{}HttpRetryOptions}) is handed to the SDK's \texttt{HttpOptions}; \texttt{FallbackModel} tries each listed model in turn on retriable status codes (429 and friends) unless a response was already yielded; \texttt{ReflectAndRetryModelPlugin} feeds model errors back as guidance. \texttt{adk/\allowbreak{}models/\allowbreak{}google\_\allowbreak{}llm.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L190-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L205}, \texttt{adk/\allowbreak{}models/\allowbreak{}google\_\allowbreak{}llm.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L427-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L462}; \texttt{adk/\allowbreak{}models/\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}fallback\_\allowbreak{}model.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L207-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L260}, \texttt{adk/\allowbreak{}models/\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}fallback\_\allowbreak{}model.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L352-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L410}; \texttt{adk/\allowbreak{}plugins/\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}reflect\_\allowbreak{}retry\_\allowbreak{}model\_\allowbreak{}plugin.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L36-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L70}
\item \textbf{Tool-call format:} each \texttt{BaseTool.\allowbreak{}process\_\allowbreak{}llm\_\allowbreak{}request} registers a \texttt{FunctionDeclaration} on \texttt{GenerateContentConfig.\allowbreak{}tools} and itself in \texttt{tools\_\allowbreak{}dict}; responses carry \texttt{types.\allowbreak{}FunctionCall} parts that the postprocessor dispatches by name. \texttt{adk/\allowbreak{}tools/\allowbreak{}base\_\allowbreak{}tool.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L144-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L200}; \texttt{adk/\allowbreak{}tools/\allowbreak{}function\_\allowbreak{}tool.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L118-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L150}
\end{itemize}

\subsubsection*{Output}
\begin{itemize}
\item \textbf{Tool set:} \texttt{BaseTool} subclasses: \texttt{FunctionTool} (declaration generated from a Python callable, argument validation, optional confirmation), \texttt{LongRunningFunctionTool}, \texttt{AgentTool}, MCP, OpenAPI and toolsets, plus Google-hosted search, code execution and data tools. \texttt{adk/\allowbreak{}tools/\allowbreak{}base\_\allowbreak{}tool.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L57-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L200}; \texttt{adk/\allowbreak{}tools/\allowbreak{}function\_\allowbreak{}tool.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L76-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L150}, \texttt{adk/\allowbreak{}tools/\allowbreak{}function\_\allowbreak{}tool.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L370-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L453}
\item \textbf{Executor and parallelism:} \texttt{handle\_\allowbreak{}function\_\allowbreak{}call\_\allowbreak{}list\_\allowbreak{}async} prepares one \texttt{asyncio.\allowbreak{}Task} per function call and executes them concurrently, wrapping \texttt{tool.\allowbreak{}run\_\allowbreak{}async(\allowbreak{}args, tool\_\allowbreak{}context)} with plugin and agent before/after tool callbacks and an \texttt{execute\_\allowbreak{}tool} span, then merges the responses into one event. \texttt{fl/\allowbreak{}tools/\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}batch\_\allowbreak{}executor.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L215-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L250}, \texttt{fl/\allowbreak{}tools/\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}batch\_\allowbreak{}executor.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L384-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L404}; \texttt{fl/\allowbreak{}tools/\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}caller.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L471-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L640}, \texttt{fl/\allowbreak{}tools/\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}caller.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L663-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L671}
\item \textbf{Code execution:} an agent's \texttt{code\_\allowbreak{}executor} runs model-written code via \texttt{BuiltInCodeExecutor} (inside Gemini), \texttt{UnsafeLocalCodeExecutor} (\texttt{exec} in process), \texttt{ContainerCodeExecutor} (Docker image), or GKE, Vertex and Agent Engine sandboxes; request and response processors extract code blocks and feed results back. \texttt{adk/\allowbreak{}code\_\allowbreak{}executors/\allowbreak{}built\_\allowbreak{}in\_\allowbreak{}code\_\allowbreak{}executor.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L31}; \texttt{adk/\allowbreak{}code\_\allowbreak{}executors/\allowbreak{}unsafe\_\allowbreak{}local\_\allowbreak{}code\_\allowbreak{}executor.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L120}; \texttt{adk/\allowbreak{}code\_\allowbreak{}executors/\allowbreak{}container\_\allowbreak{}code\_\allowbreak{}executor.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L126-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L240}; \texttt{fl/\allowbreak{}extensions/\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}code\_\allowbreak{}execution.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L146-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L190}
\item \textbf{Result presentation:} every step is yielded to the caller as an \texttt{Event} (an \texttt{LlmResponse} with author, \texttt{actions}, \texttt{partial}), and \texttt{is\_\allowbreak{}final\_\allowbreak{}response(\allowbreak{})} marks the user-facing answer; \texttt{output\_\allowbreak{}key} writes the final text into session state. \texttt{adk/\allowbreak{}events/\allowbreak{}event.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L87-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L160}, \texttt{adk/\allowbreak{}events/\allowbreak{}event.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L286-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L309}; \texttt{adk/\allowbreak{}agents/\allowbreak{}llm\_\allowbreak{}agent.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1093-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1120}
\item \textbf{Tool effects as actions:} \texttt{EventActions} carries \texttt{state\_\allowbreak{}delta}, \texttt{artifact\_\allowbreak{}delta}, \texttt{transfer\_\allowbreak{}to\_\allowbreak{}agent}, \texttt{escalate}, \texttt{requested\_\allowbreak{}tool\_\allowbreak{}confirmations} and \texttt{end\_\allowbreak{}of\_\allowbreak{}agent}, which the session service and loops apply. \texttt{adk/\allowbreak{}events/\allowbreak{}event\_\allowbreak{}actions.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L78-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L204}
\end{itemize}

\subsubsection*{Production concerns}
\begin{itemize}
\item \textbf{Persistence:} context: \texttt{Session} (state plus events) through \texttt{BaseSessionService.\allowbreak{}append\_\allowbreak{}event} in memory, SQLite (\texttt{aiosqlite}), SQLAlchemy \texttt{DatabaseSessionService}, or Vertex AI sessions; artifacts via \texttt{BaseArtifactService} (file, GCS, in-memory); memory via memory services. \texttt{adk/\allowbreak{}sessions/\allowbreak{}base\_\allowbreak{}session\_\allowbreak{}service.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L166-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L235}; \texttt{adk/\allowbreak{}sessions/\allowbreak{}sqlite\_\allowbreak{}session\_\allowbreak{}service.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L184-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L212}; \texttt{adk/\allowbreak{}sessions/\allowbreak{}database\_\allowbreak{}session\_\allowbreak{}service.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L310-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L340}; \texttt{adk/\allowbreak{}artifacts/\allowbreak{}base\_\allowbreak{}artifact\_\allowbreak{}service.\allowbreak{}py}
\item \textbf{Safety \& security:} control flow: tool confirmation gate and before-callback vetoes; context: \texttt{\_\allowbreak{}fencing.\allowbreak{}py} fences tool descriptions and quotes untrusted or other-agent text to blunt prompt injection, \texttt{\_\allowbreak{}restricted\_\allowbreak{}pickle} limits what session state can deserialize; output: container and remote code sandboxes; auth preprocessor and credential service for tool credentials. \texttt{fl/\allowbreak{}context/\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}fencing.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L98-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L140}, \texttt{fl/\allowbreak{}context/\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}fencing.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L206-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L223}; \texttt{adk/\allowbreak{}sessions/\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}restricted\_\allowbreak{}pickle.\allowbreak{}py}; \texttt{fl/\allowbreak{}single\_\allowbreak{}flow.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L51-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L54}
