\item \textbf{Observability:} OpenTelemetry spans \texttt{call\_\allowbreak{}llm} (request, response, token usage attributes) and \texttt{execute\_\allowbreak{}tool} with gen\_ai semantic conventions; exporters configured from \texttt{OTEL\_\allowbreak{}EXPORTER\_\allowbreak{}OTLP\_\allowbreak{}*} or Google Cloud; \texttt{LoggingPlugin}, \texttt{DebugLoggingPlugin} and \texttt{BigQueryAgentAnalyticsPlugin}; \texttt{TokenUsage} accounting. \texttt{adk/\allowbreak{}telemetry/\allowbreak{}tracing.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L251-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L369}, \texttt{adk/\allowbreak{}telemetry/\allowbreak{}tracing.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L623-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L717}; \texttt{adk/\allowbreak{}telemetry/\allowbreak{}setup.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L45-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L114}; \texttt{adk/\allowbreak{}telemetry/\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}token\_\allowbreak{}usage.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L96}; \texttt{adk/\allowbreak{}plugins/\allowbreak{}logging\_\allowbreak{}plugin.\allowbreak{}py}
\item \textbf{Resilience:} model interface: SDK HTTP retries and \texttt{FallbackModel}; output: \texttt{ReflectAndRetryToolPlugin} returns failures to the model up to \texttt{max\_\allowbreak{}retries}, \texttt{on\_\allowbreak{}tool\_\allowbreak{}error} callbacks; control flow: \texttt{on\_\allowbreak{}model\_\allowbreak{}error} callbacks, resumable invocations (\texttt{ResumabilityConfig}), \texttt{rewind\_\allowbreak{}async} to roll back state and artifacts, an abort signal checked each step. \texttt{adk/\allowbreak{}plugins/\allowbreak{}reflect\_\allowbreak{}retry\_\allowbreak{}tool\_\allowbreak{}plugin.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L47-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L131}; \texttt{adk/\allowbreak{}apps/\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}configs.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L29-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L48}; \texttt{adk/\allowbreak{}runners.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1394-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1432}; \texttt{adk/\allowbreak{}agents/\allowbreak{}invocation\_\allowbreak{}context.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L439-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L460}
\item \textbf{Performance:} SSE streaming, concurrent tool tasks, \texttt{ParallelAgent} branches, Gemini context caching (\texttt{ContextCacheConfig}, \texttt{GeminiContextCacheManager}), event compaction and \texttt{ContextFilterPlugin} trimming. \texttt{adk/\allowbreak{}agents/\allowbreak{}context\_\allowbreak{}cache\_\allowbreak{}config.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L27-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L84}; \texttt{adk/\allowbreak{}models/\allowbreak{}gemini\_\allowbreak{}context\_\allowbreak{}cache\_\allowbreak{}manager.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L84}; \texttt{adk/\allowbreak{}agents/\allowbreak{}parallel\_\allowbreak{}agent.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L134-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L136}
\item \textbf{Evaluation:} \texttt{AgentEvaluator.\allowbreak{}evaluate} runs eval sets through \texttt{LocalEvalService} with trajectory, final-response match, LLM-as-judge, rubric, hallucination and safety metrics; 682 test files under \texttt{tests/\allowbreak{}}. \texttt{adk/\allowbreak{}evaluation/\allowbreak{}agent\_\allowbreak{}evaluator.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L118-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L130}, \texttt{adk/\allowbreak{}evaluation/\allowbreak{}agent\_\allowbreak{}evaluator.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L302-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L390}; \texttt{adk/\allowbreak{}evaluation/\allowbreak{}local\_\allowbreak{}eval\_\allowbreak{}service.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L131}; \texttt{pyproject.\allowbreak{}toml:\allowbreak{}L470-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L473}
\end{itemize}

\subsubsection*{Hard to place}
\begin{itemize}
\item \textbf{\texttt{transfer\_\allowbreak{}to\_\allowbreak{}agent}:} a tool call (output) whose effect is control flow, but the target runs inside the same invocation, session and event stream, so the "inner agent" is not a separate harness; transfer depth is unbounded via peer and parent hops. \texttt{fl/\allowbreak{}core/\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}function\_\allowbreak{}call\_\allowbreak{}postprocessor.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L72-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L140}
\item \textbf{Agent routing by history:} \texttt{\_\allowbreak{}find\_\allowbreak{}agent\_\allowbreak{}to\_\allowbreak{}run} decides control flow by reading the session's events (context) rather than from the loop's own state. \texttt{adk/\allowbreak{}runners.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1815-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1846}
\item \textbf{Plugins and callbacks are bundles:} a \texttt{before\_\allowbreak{}model\_\allowbreak{}callback} returning content both skips the model call (control flow) and stands in for the response (model interface); a \texttt{before\_\allowbreak{}tool\_\allowbreak{}callback} can replace a tool result (output); each resolved by function. \texttt{adk/\allowbreak{}plugins/\allowbreak{}base\_\allowbreak{}plugin.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L41-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L105}; \texttt{fl/\allowbreak{}core/\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}finalizer.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L150-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L195}
\item \textbf{Hosted execution:} \texttt{BuiltInCodeExecutor} and Google-hosted tools run inside Gemini; the harness declares them (context) and never executes (output). \texttt{adk/\allowbreak{}code\_\allowbreak{}executors/\allowbreak{}built\_\allowbreak{}in\_\allowbreak{}code\_\allowbreak{}executor.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L31}
\item \textbf{\texttt{output\_\allowbreak{}key} into state:} model text is written to session state (persistence) and read back by \texttt{\{key\}} instruction injection, so an output effect feeds the next context. \texttt{adk/\allowbreak{}agents/\allowbreak{}llm\_\allowbreak{}agent.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1093-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1120}; \texttt{fl/\allowbreak{}prompt/\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}instructions.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L85-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L95}
\end{itemize}

\subsubsection*{Summary}
\begin{longtable}{@{}p{0.28\linewidth}p{0.66\linewidth}@{}}
\toprule \textbf{Primitive} & \textbf{Design decision} \\ \midrule \endhead
Input & types.Content user message appended as an Event; tool results and HITL answers re-enter as function-response events \\
Context & Ordered request processors rebuild LlmRequest from session events each step; instructions, memory tool, compaction, caching \\
Model interface & BaseLlm adapters resolved by model name; Gemini via google-genai; SSE streaming; SDK retries; FallbackModel \\
Output & BaseTool subclasses run concurrently as asyncio tasks with callbacks; code executors from in-process to Docker \\
Control flow & Runner picks agent, flow loops until final response; max\_llm\_calls; confirmation pauses; transfers run sub-agents in place \\
\bottomrule \end{longtable}


\subsection{AutoGen (agentchat AssistantAgent and its runtime)}

\textbf{Source:} https://github.com/microsoft/autogen $\cdot$ \textbf{Version:} commit 027ecf0a379bcc1d09956d46d12d44a3ad9cee14 (2026-04-06) $\cdot$ \textbf{Language:} Python
\textbf{Path conventions:} \texttt{ac/\allowbreak{}} = \texttt{python/\allowbreak{}packages/\allowbreak{}autogen-\allowbreak{}agentchat/\allowbreak{}src/\allowbreak{}autogen\_\allowbreak{}agentchat/\allowbreak{}}; \texttt{core/\allowbreak{}} = \texttt{python/\allowbreak{}packages/\allowbreak{}autogen-\allowbreak{}core/\allowbreak{}src/\allowbreak{}autogen\_\allowbreak{}core/\allowbreak{}}; \texttt{ext/\allowbreak{}} = \texttt{python/\allowbreak{}packages/\allowbreak{}autogen-\allowbreak{}ext/\allowbreak{}src/\allowbreak{}autogen\_\allowbreak{}ext/\allowbreak{}}; \texttt{t/\allowbreak{}} = \texttt{python/\allowbreak{}packages/\allowbreak{}autogen-\allowbreak{}agentchat/\allowbreak{}tests/\allowbreak{}}. All paths relative to the repo root.
\textbf{What it is:} AutoGen is Microsoft's layered agent framework: \texttt{autogen-\allowbreak{}core} is an actor-style message runtime, \texttt{autogen-\allowbreak{}agentchat} builds chat agents and teams on it, and \texttt{autogen-\allowbreak{}ext} supplies model clients, tools and code executors. \texttt{AssistantAgent} is the canonical tool-using agent; it reaches the model through a \texttt{ChatCompletionClient} (for example \texttt{OpenAIChatCompletionClient} over the \texttt{openai} SDK) and runs tools through a \texttt{Workbench}.

\subsubsection*{Control flow}
\begin{itemize}
\item \textbf{Per-turn tool loop:} \texttt{on\_\allowbreak{}messages\_\allowbreak{}stream} adds the incoming messages to the model context, queries memory, calls the model once, then \texttt{\_\allowbreak{}process\_\allowbreak{}model\_\allowbreak{}result} loops \texttt{for loop\_\allowbreak{}iteration in range(\allowbreak{}max\_\allowbreak{}tool\_\allowbreak{}iterations)}: a text response returns immediately; tool calls are executed, their results added to context, and another model call is made unless this is the last iteration. \texttt{ac/\allowbreak{}agents/\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}assistant\_\allowbreak{}agent.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L901-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1013}, \texttt{ac/\allowbreak{}agents/\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}assistant\_\allowbreak{}agent.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1149-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1270}
\item \textbf{Stop rule and budget:} \texttt{max\_\allowbreak{}tool\_\allowbreak{}iterations} (default 1) bounds the loop; after it the agent either reflects (one more model call without tools) or emits a \texttt{ToolCallSummaryMessage} built from a format string; \texttt{reflect\_\allowbreak{}on\_\allowbreak{}tool\_\allowbreak{}use} defaults to true only when a structured output type is set. \texttt{ac/\allowbreak{}agents/\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}assistant\_\allowbreak{}agent.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L738-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L739}, \texttt{ac/\allowbreak{}agents/\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}assistant\_\allowbreak{}agent.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L842-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L853}, \texttt{ac/\allowbreak{}agents/\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}assistant\_\allowbreak{}agent.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1302-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1325}
\item \textbf{Handoff as early exit:} if any executed call was a handoff tool, \texttt{\_\allowbreak{}check\_\allowbreak{}and\_\allowbreak{}handle\_\allowbreak{}handoff} returns a \texttt{HandoffMessage} carrying the tool-call context and the loop returns; multi-agent delegation then happens in the team. \texttt{ac/\allowbreak{}agents/\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}assistant\_\allowbreak{}agent.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1245-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1251}, \texttt{ac/\allowbreak{}agents/\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}assistant\_\allowbreak{}agent.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1328-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1408}
\item \textbf{Team sequencing:} \texttt{BaseGroupChat.\allowbreak{}run\_\allowbreak{}stream} starts a \texttt{SingleThreadedAgentRuntime}, publishes \texttt{GroupChatStart}, and reads an output queue until a \texttt{GroupChatTermination}; the manager's \texttt{handle\_\allowbreak{}agent\_\allowbreak{}response} applies the \texttt{TerminationCondition} and \texttt{max\_\allowbreak{}turns}, then \texttt{select\_\allowbreak{}speaker} (round-robin: next index modulo participants) and publishes \texttt{GroupChatRequestPublish} to the chosen \texttt{ChatAgentContainer}. \texttt{ac/\allowbreak{}teams/\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}group\_\allowbreak{}chat/\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}base\_\allowbreak{}group\_\allowbreak{}chat.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L489-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L558}, \texttt{ac/\allowbreak{}teams/\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}group\_\allowbreak{}chat/\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}base\_\allowbreak{}group\_\allowbreak{}chat\_\allowbreak{}manager.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L135-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L238}, \texttt{ac/\allowbreak{}teams/\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}group\_\allowbreak{}chat/\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}round\_\allowbreak{}robin\_\allowbreak{}group\_\allowbreak{}chat.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L72-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L82}
\item \textbf{Core runtime loop:} \texttt{SingleThreadedAgentRuntime.\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}process\_\allowbreak{}next} pops one envelope from an asyncio queue, runs intervention handlers (\texttt{on\_\allowbreak{}send}/\texttt{on\_\allowbreak{}publish}, which may return \texttt{DropMessage}), and dispatches to the agent's handler. \texttt{core/\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}single\_\allowbreak{}threaded\_\allowbreak{}agent\_\allowbreak{}runtime.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L332-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L385}, \texttt{core/\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}single\_\allowbreak{}threaded\_\allowbreak{}agent\_\allowbreak{}runtime.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L671-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L720}
\end{itemize}

\subsubsection*{Input}
\begin{itemize}
\item \textbf{Task entry:} \texttt{BaseChatAgent.\allowbreak{}run} wraps a string task as \texttt{TextMessage(\allowbreak{}source="user")}, accepts a message or message list, and calls \texttt{on\_\allowbreak{}messages}; in a team the container buffers messages from other agents and passes them to \texttt{on\_\allowbreak{}messages\_\allowbreak{}stream} when asked to speak. \texttt{ac/\allowbreak{}agents/\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}base\_\allowbreak{}chat\_\allowbreak{}agent.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L111-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L153}, \texttt{ac/\allowbreak{}teams/\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}group\_\allowbreak{}chat/\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}chat\_\allowbreak{}agent\_\allowbreak{}container.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L86-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L144}
\item \textbf{Tool results re-enter the context:} each executed call yields a \texttt{FunctionExecutionResult} (content, \texttt{is\_\allowbreak{}error}) and the batch is added as one \texttt{FunctionExecutionResultMessage}; \texttt{HandoffMessage.\allowbreak{}context} carries the prior tool calls into the receiving agent. \texttt{ac/\allowbreak{}agents/\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}assistant\_\allowbreak{}agent.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1236-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1240}, \texttt{ac/\allowbreak{}agents/\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}assistant\_\allowbreak{}agent.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1014-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1026}
\item \textbf{Human input:} \texttt{UserProxyAgent} calls \texttt{input\_\allowbreak{}func} (default \texttt{cancellable\_\allowbreak{}input} over \texttt{input(\allowbreak{})}) and returns the text as a \texttt{TextMessage}, emitting \texttt{UserInputRequestedEvent} first; the model consumes it as the next turn. \texttt{ac/\allowbreak{}agents/\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}user\_\allowbreak{}proxy\_\allowbreak{}agent.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L22-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L27}, \texttt{ac/\allowbreak{}agents/\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}user\_\allowbreak{}proxy\_\allowbreak{}agent.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L165-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L170}, \texttt{ac/\allowbreak{}agents/\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}user\_\allowbreak{}proxy\_\allowbreak{}agent.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L186-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L200}
\end{itemize}

\subsubsection*{Context}
\begin{itemize}
\item \textbf{System prompt:} a single \texttt{SystemMessage} from the \texttt{system\_\allowbreak{}message} argument (default tells the model to use tools and reply TERMINATE); it is prepended to the context's messages at call time. \texttt{ac/\allowbreak{}agents/\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}assistant\_\allowbreak{}agent.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L735-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L737}, \texttt{ac/\allowbreak{}agents/\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}assistant\_\allowbreak{}agent.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L770}, \texttt{ac/\allowbreak{}agents/\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}assistant\_\allowbreak{}agent.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1085-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1086}
\item \textbf{History as \texttt{ChatCompletionContext}:} the agent keeps an \texttt{UnboundedChatCompletionContext} by default; \texttt{get\_\allowbreak{}messages} returns what the model sees, and \texttt{on\_\allowbreak{}reset} clears it. \texttt{ac/\allowbreak{}agents/\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}assistant\_\allowbreak{}agent.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L840}, \texttt{core/\allowbreak{}model\_\allowbreak{}context/\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}chat\_\allowbreak{}completion\_\allowbreak{}context.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L10-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L60}, \texttt{ac/\allowbreak{}agents/\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}assistant\_\allowbreak{}agent.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1626-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1628}
\item \textbf{Window fitting variants:} \texttt{BufferedChatCompletionContext} keeps the last \texttt{buffer\_\allowbreak{}size} messages (dropping a leading function-result), \texttt{TokenLimitedChatCompletionContext} pops middle messages until \texttt{remaining\_\allowbreak{}tokens} or \texttt{token\_\allowbreak{}limit} is satisfied, \texttt{HeadAndTailChatCompletionContext} keeps the first and last N; no summarization is built in. \texttt{core/\allowbreak{}model\_\allowbreak{}context/\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}buffered\_\allowbreak{}chat\_\allowbreak{}completion\_\allowbreak{}context.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L34-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L41}, \texttt{core/\allowbreak{}model\_\allowbreak{}context/\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}token\_\allowbreak{}limited\_\allowbreak{}chat\_\allowbreak{}completion\_\allowbreak{}context.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L57-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L75}, \texttt{core/\allowbreak{}model\_\allowbreak{}context/\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}head\_\allowbreak{}and\_\allowbreak{}tail\_\allowbreak{}chat\_\allowbreak{}completion\_\allowbreak{}context.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L41-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L60}
\item \textbf{Memory injection:} each \texttt{Memory.\allowbreak{}update\_\allowbreak{}context} may rewrite the model context before the call, and the agent yields a \texttt{MemoryQueryEvent} with the retrieved items. \texttt{ac/\allowbreak{}agents/\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}assistant\_\allowbreak{}agent.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1028-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1055}, \texttt{core/\allowbreak{}memory/\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}base\_\allowbreak{}memory.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L60-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L94}
\item \textbf{Client compatibility pass:} \texttt{\_\allowbreak{}get\_\allowbreak{}compatible\_\allowbreak{}context} strips images when \texttt{model\_\allowbreak{}info["vision"]} is false. \texttt{ac/\allowbreak{}agents/\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}assistant\_\allowbreak{}agent.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1642-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1648}, \texttt{ac/\allowbreak{}utils/\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}utils.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L36}
\end{itemize}

\subsubsection*{Model interface}
\begin{itemize}
\item \textbf{\texttt{ChatCompletionClient} protocol:} \texttt{create}/\texttt{create\_\allowbreak{}stream} take messages, tool schemas, \texttt{json\_\allowbreak{}output} and a \texttt{CancellationToken}, and expose \texttt{count\_\allowbreak{}tokens}, \texttt{remaining\_\allowbreak{}tokens} and \texttt{model\_\allowbreak{}info}. \texttt{core/\allowbreak{}models/\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}model\_\allowbreak{}client.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L209-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L295}
\item \textbf{OpenAI client:} \texttt{BaseOpenAIChatCompletionClient.\allowbreak{}create} validates create args, awaits \texttt{chat.\allowbreak{}completions.\allowbreak{}create} linked to the cancellation token, records \texttt{RequestUsage}, and maps \texttt{tool\_\allowbreak{}calls} to \texttt{FunctionCall}s with the content kept as \texttt{thought}; retries are delegated to the SDK via \texttt{max\_\allowbreak{}retries}. \texttt{ext/\allowbreak{}models/\allowbreak{}openai/\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}openai\_\allowbreak{}client.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L663-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L815}, \texttt{ext/\allowbreak{}models/\allowbreak{}openai/\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}openai\_\allowbreak{}client.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L131-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L134}, \texttt{ext/\allowbreak{}models/\allowbreak{}openai/\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}openai\_\allowbreak{}client.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1200}
\item \textbf{Streaming:} \texttt{create\_\allowbreak{}stream} iterates chunks, accumulates \texttt{content\_\allowbreak{}deltas} and per-index tool-call fragments, and ends with a \texttt{CreateResult}; the agent re-emits text chunks as \texttt{ModelClientStreamingChunkEvent}. \texttt{ext/\allowbreak{}models/\allowbreak{}openai/\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}openai\_\allowbreak{}client.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L816-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1033}, \texttt{ac/\allowbreak{}agents/\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}assistant\_\allowbreak{}agent.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1093-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1107}
\item \textbf{Fallback:} no backup-model routing found in the agent or client; \texttt{ChatCompletionCache} wraps a client to serve repeated requests from a cache store. \texttt{ext/\allowbreak{}models/\allowbreak{}cache/\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}chat\_\allowbreak{}completion\_\allowbreak{}cache.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L29-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L70}
\end{itemize}

\subsubsection*{Output}
\begin{itemize}
\item \textbf{Tool set and workbench:} callables become \texttt{FunctionTool}s, and tools are wrapped in a \texttt{StaticWorkbench} (or the user passes workbenches); handoffs are separate tools; names must be unique. \texttt{ac/\allowbreak{}agents/\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}assistant\_\allowbreak{}agent.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L774-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L805}, \texttt{ac/\allowbreak{}agents/\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}assistant\_\allowbreak{}agent.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L827-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L838}, \texttt{core/\allowbreak{}tools/\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}function\_\allowbreak{}tool.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L105-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L125}
\item \textbf{Executor and concurrency:} all tool calls in one response run concurrently with \texttt{asyncio.\allowbreak{}gather}; \texttt{\_\allowbreak{}execute\_\allowbreak{}tool\_\allowbreak{}call} parses arguments, tries handoff tools, then \texttt{workbench.\allowbreak{}call\_\allowbreak{}tool\_\allowbreak{}stream}, returning an error result for bad JSON or an unknown tool. \texttt{ac/\allowbreak{}agents/\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}assistant\_\allowbreak{}agent.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1197-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1215}, \texttt{ac/\allowbreak{}agents/\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}assistant\_\allowbreak{}agent.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1536-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1624}, \texttt{core/\allowbreak{}tools/\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}static\_\allowbreak{}workbench.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L94-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L124}
\item \textbf{Sandbox (code tools):} \texttt{DockerCommandLineCodeExecutor} writes each block to a work dir and runs \texttt{timeout <s> <cmd> <file>} inside a container (default 60 s, auto-remove); \texttt{LocalCommandLineCodeExecutor} runs subprocesses on the host with the same timeout. \texttt{ext/\allowbreak{}code\_\allowbreak{}executors/\allowbreak{}docker/\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}docker\_\allowbreak{}code\_\allowbreak{}executor.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L85-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L222}, \texttt{ext/\allowbreak{}code\_\allowbreak{}executors/\allowbreak{}docker/\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}docker\_\allowbreak{}code\_\allowbreak{}executor.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L327-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L376}, \texttt{ext/\allowbreak{}code\_\allowbreak{}executors/\allowbreak{}local/\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}init\_\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L45-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L173}
\item \textbf{Result presentation:} \texttt{ToolCallRequestEvent} and \texttt{ToolCallExecutionEvent} are yielded as inner events, the final \texttt{Response} carries a \texttt{TextMessage}, \texttt{StructuredMessage}, \texttt{ToolCallSummaryMessage} or \texttt{HandoffMessage}, and \texttt{Console} prints the stream. \texttt{ac/\allowbreak{}messages.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L445-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L500}, \texttt{ac/\allowbreak{}agents/\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}assistant\_\allowbreak{}agent.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1498-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1535}, \texttt{ac/\allowbreak{}ui/\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}console.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L82-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L103}
\end{itemize}

\subsubsection*{Production concerns}
\begin{itemize}
\item \textbf{Persistence:} \texttt{save\_\allowbreak{}state}/\texttt{load\_\allowbreak{}state} serialize the model context (context primitive) as \texttt{AssistantAgentState}; teams and the runtime save state the same way; nothing is written to disk by default. \texttt{ac/\allowbreak{}agents/\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}assistant\_\allowbreak{}agent.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1630-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1640}, \texttt{core/\allowbreak{}model\_\allowbreak{}context/\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}chat\_\allowbreak{}completion\_\allowbreak{}context.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L73}
\item \textbf{Safety \& security:} code runs in Docker when that executor is chosen; runtime intervention handlers can drop or rewrite messages; \texttt{CancellationToken} cancels in-flight calls; no approval gate on tool calls or prompt-injection defense found. \texttt{ext/\allowbreak{}code\_\allowbreak{}executors/\allowbreak{}docker/\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}docker\_\allowbreak{}code\_\allowbreak{}executor.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L85-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L120}, \texttt{core/\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}single\_\allowbreak{}threaded\_\allowbreak{}agent\_\allowbreak{}runtime.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L691-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L711}
\item \textbf{Observability:} the OpenAI client logs \texttt{LLMCallEvent}/\texttt{LLMStreamStartEvent} with prompt and usage tokens to the event logger, tools log \texttt{ToolCallEvent}, agentchat emits typed events on \texttt{autogen\_\allowbreak{}agentchat.\allowbreak{}events}, and \texttt{\_\allowbreak{}telemetry} adds OpenTelemetry tracing around runtime messages. \texttt{ext/\allowbreak{}models/\allowbreak{}openai/\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}openai\_\allowbreak{}client.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L716-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L717}, \texttt{core/\allowbreak{}logging.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L10-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L160}, \texttt{ac/\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}init\_\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L8-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L11}, \texttt{core/\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}telemetry/\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}tracing.\allowbreak{}py}
\item \textbf{Resilience:} HTTP retries are the SDK's \texttt{max\_\allowbreak{}retries}; code execution has a timeout; a \texttt{TimeoutTermination} and \texttt{TokenUsageTermination} exist for teams; no overflow compaction, only the trimming contexts. \texttt{ext/\allowbreak{}models/\allowbreak{}openai/\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}openai\_\allowbreak{}client.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1200}, \texttt{ac/\allowbreak{}conditions/\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}terminations.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L229-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L354}
\item \textbf{Performance:} concurrent tool execution, streaming chunks, \texttt{ChatCompletionCache}, and token-limited contexts. \texttt{ac/\allowbreak{}agents/\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}assistant\_\allowbreak{}agent.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1200}, \texttt{ext/\allowbreak{}models/\allowbreak{}cache/\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}chat\_\allowbreak{}completion\_\allowbreak{}cache.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L29}, \texttt{core/\allowbreak{}model\_\allowbreak{}context/\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}token\_\allowbreak{}limited\_\allowbreak{}chat\_\allowbreak{}completion\_\allowbreak{}context.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L19}
\item \textbf{Evaluation:} pytest suites per package cover the assistant agent, group chats, termination conditions and executors with replay model clients. \texttt{t/\allowbreak{}test\_\allowbreak{}assistant\_\allowbreak{}agent.\allowbreak{}py}, \texttt{t/\allowbreak{}test\_\allowbreak{}group\_\allowbreak{}chat.\allowbreak{}py}, \texttt{t/\allowbreak{}test\_\allowbreak{}termination\_\allowbreak{}condition.\allowbreak{}py}
\end{itemize}

\subsubsection*{Hard to place}
\begin{itemize}
\item \textbf{Reflection call:} \texttt{\_\allowbreak{}reflect\_\allowbreak{}on\_\allowbreak{}tool\_\allowbreak{}use\_\allowbreak{}flow} is a second model call whose request is assembled from the same context but with no tools; its decision to run is control flow, its assembly context, its call model interface, and it lives in one method. \texttt{ac/\allowbreak{}agents/\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}assistant\_\allowbreak{}agent.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1409-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1497}
\item \textbf{Handoff tool:} by rule the tool call is output and the delegation is control flow of the team; here the agent itself returns a \texttt{HandoffMessage} and the team's \texttt{Swarm} manager routes on it, so the delegation decision is split across two harness layers. \texttt{ac/\allowbreak{}agents/\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}assistant\_\allowbreak{}agent.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1328-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1408}, \texttt{ac/\allowbreak{}teams/\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}group\_\allowbreak{}chat/\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}swarm\_\allowbreak{}group\_\allowbreak{}chat.\allowbreak{}py}
\item \textbf{Runtime intervention handlers:} \texttt{on\_\allowbreak{}send}/\texttt{on\_\allowbreak{}publish} can drop or rewrite any message between agents; they act on the transport, not the model request, so they sit between input and control flow rather than within one primitive. \texttt{core/\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}single\_\allowbreak{}threaded\_\allowbreak{}agent\_\allowbreak{}runtime.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L691-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L711}
\end{itemize}

\subsubsection*{Summary}
\begin{longtable}{@{}p{0.28\linewidth}p{0.66\linewidth}@{}}
\toprule \textbf{Primitive} & \textbf{Design decision} \\ \midrule \endhead
Input & Chat messages from user or peers; tool results batched as one function-result message \\
Context & One system message plus a pluggable ChatCompletionContext (unbounded, buffered, token-limited); memory hooks \\
Model interface & ChatCompletionClient protocol; OpenAI SDK client with usage logging and streaming; SDK retries \\
Output & Workbench tools run concurrently via asyncio.gather; Docker or local code executors \\
Control flow & Bounded max\_tool\_iterations loop, then reflect or summarize; teams via actor runtime, termination conditions \\
\bottomrule \end{longtable}


\subsection{CrewAI (one Agent executing a Task with tools)}

\textbf{Source:} https://github.com/crewAIInc/crewAI $\cdot$ \textbf{Version:} commit 6115839576d68d2e83b9f7f31e6c55d471e1fc64 (2026-10-15) $\cdot$ \textbf{Language:} Python
\textbf{Path conventions:} \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}} = \texttt{lib/\allowbreak{}crewai/\allowbreak{}src/\allowbreak{}crewai/\allowbreak{}}; \texttt{t/\allowbreak{}} = \texttt{lib/\allowbreak{}crewai/\allowbreak{}tests/\allowbreak{}}. All paths relative to the repo root.
\textbf{What it is:} CrewAI (crewAI Inc.) is a multi-agent orchestration framework in which a \texttt{Crew} runs \texttt{Task}s through role-playing \texttt{Agent}s. Each task execution hands a prompt to an \texttt{AgentExecutor}, a state-machine \texttt{Flow} that loops a ReAct-style LLM call and tool execution until a final answer. The model is reached through the \texttt{LLM} class, which routes to native provider clients (OpenAI, Anthropic, Azure, Gemini, Bedrock) or falls back to \texttt{litellm.\allowbreak{}completion}.

\subsubsection*{Control flow}
\begin{itemize}
\item \textbf{Crew task sequencing:} \texttt{kickoff} prepares inputs then dispatches to \texttt{\_\allowbreak{}run\_\allowbreak{}sequential\_\allowbreak{}process} or \texttt{\_\allowbreak{}run\_\allowbreak{}hierarchical\_\allowbreak{}process} (which first creates a manager agent); both call \texttt{\_\allowbreak{}execute\_\allowbreak{}tasks}, a \texttt{for} loop over tasks that runs each synchronously via \texttt{task.\allowbreak{}execute\_\allowbreak{}sync} or asynchronously via a future, feeding prior outputs forward. \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}crew.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1053-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1069}, \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}crew.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1522-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1529}, \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}crew.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1595-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1635}
\item \textbf{Agent step loop as a Flow:} \texttt{AgentExecutor} is a \texttt{Flow}; \texttt{check\_\allowbreak{}max\_\allowbreak{}iterations} routes to \texttt{continue\_\allowbreak{}reasoning} (text ReAct) or \texttt{continue\_\allowbreak{}reasoning\_\allowbreak{}native} (API tool calls), tool routers return to \texttt{initialized}, and \texttt{finalize} ends when \texttt{current\_\allowbreak{}answer} is an \texttt{AgentFinish}. \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}agents/\allowbreak{}agent\_\allowbreak{}executor.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L2404-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L2422}, \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}agents/\allowbreak{}agent\_\allowbreak{}executor.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1828-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1909}, \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}agents/\allowbreak{}agent\_\allowbreak{}executor.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L2544-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L2624}
\item \textbf{Iteration budget:} \texttt{max\_\allowbreak{}iter} (default 25) is checked by \texttt{has\_\allowbreak{}reached\_\allowbreak{}max\_\allowbreak{}iterations}; on exhaustion \texttt{ensure\_\allowbreak{}force\_\allowbreak{}final\_\allowbreak{}answer} appends a "give your final answer" instruction and makes one more LLM call. \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}agents/\allowbreak{}agent\_\allowbreak{}builder/\allowbreak{}base\_\allowbreak{}agent.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L285-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L287}, \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}utilities/\allowbreak{}agent\_\allowbreak{}utils.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L364-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L375}, \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}agents/\allowbreak{}agent\_\allowbreak{}executor.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1600-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1621}, \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}utilities/\allowbreak{}agent\_\allowbreak{}utils.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L377-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L432}
\item \textbf{Task-level retries, timeouts and guardrails:} \texttt{Agent.\allowbreak{}execute\_\allowbreak{}task} re-enters itself on exception up to \texttt{max\_\allowbreak{}retry\_\allowbreak{}limit}, optionally under a thread-pool \texttt{max\_\allowbreak{}execution\_\allowbreak{}time}; \texttt{Task.\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}execute\_\allowbreak{}core} then runs guardrails up to \texttt{guardrail\_\allowbreak{}max\_\allowbreak{}retries} times, re-executing the agent when one fails. \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}agent/\allowbreak{}core.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L862-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L908}, \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}agent/\allowbreak{}core.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1029-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1068}, \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}task.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L893-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L910}, \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}task.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1327-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1395}
\item \textbf{Human-in-the-loop gate:} when \texttt{human\_\allowbreak{}input} is set, \texttt{invoke} calls \texttt{\_\allowbreak{}handle\_\allowbreak{}human\_\allowbreak{}feedback}, whose sync provider loops \texttt{input(\allowbreak{})} -> append feedback message -> re-run the flow until an empty reply. \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}task.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L233}, \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}agents/\allowbreak{}agent\_\allowbreak{}executor.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L3079-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L3098}, \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}core/\allowbreak{}providers/\allowbreak{}human\_\allowbreak{}input.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L255-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L265}, \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}core/\allowbreak{}providers/\allowbreak{}human\_\allowbreak{}input.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L319-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L364}
\end{itemize}

\subsubsection*{Input}
\begin{itemize}
\item \textbf{Crew inputs:} \texttt{kickoff(\allowbreak{}inputs)} normalizes a dict, runs \texttt{before\_\allowbreak{}kickoff} callbacks, interpolates it into task and agent templates, and optionally runs crew-level planning. \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}crews/\allowbreak{}utils.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L243-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L379}
\item \textbf{Tool results re-enter as text:} in text mode the observation is appended to the action text and pushed as an assistant message; in native mode each result becomes a \texttt{role:\allowbreak{} "tool"} message; tool errors are injected as "Observation: Error executing tool". \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}agents/\allowbreak{}agent\_\allowbreak{}executor.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1845-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1871}, \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}agents/\allowbreak{}agent\_\allowbreak{}executor.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L2014-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L2046}
\item \textbf{Human feedback:} the feedback string is formatted with the \texttt{human\_\allowbreak{}feedback} slice and appended to the executor's messages before re-running. \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}core/\allowbreak{}providers/\allowbreak{}human\_\allowbreak{}input.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L230-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L235}, \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}agents/\allowbreak{}agent\_\allowbreak{}executor.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L3537-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L3549}
\end{itemize}

\subsubsection*{Context}
\begin{itemize}
\item \textbf{System and user prompt assembly:} \texttt{Prompts.\allowbreak{}task\_\allowbreak{}execution} concatenates i18n slices (\texttt{role\_\allowbreak{}playing}, \texttt{tools} or \texttt{no\_\allowbreak{}tools}, a skill block, a date block) into a system prompt and a \texttt{task} slice into the user prompt, substituting \texttt{\{role\}}, \texttt{\{goal\}}, \texttt{\{backstory\}}. \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}utilities/\allowbreak{}prompts.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L93-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L142}, \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}utilities/\allowbreak{}prompts.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L211-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L260}, \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}translations/\allowbreak{}en.\allowbreak{}json:\allowbreak{}L8-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L19}
\item \textbf{Task prompt enrichment:} \texttt{\_\allowbreak{}prepare\_\allowbreak{}task\_\allowbreak{}execution} takes \texttt{task.\allowbreak{}prompt(\allowbreak{})}, adds an output schema and the prior tasks' context, then appends up to five recalled memories under a "Memories from past conversations" header; knowledge retrieval and training data are added next. \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}agent/\allowbreak{}core.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L678-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L705}, \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}agent/\allowbreak{}core.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L757-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L823}, \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}agent/\allowbreak{}core.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L957-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L993}
\item \textbf{Message list per call:} \texttt{\_\allowbreak{}setup\_\allowbreak{}messages} builds system + history + user (+ trailing) messages with cache breakpoints; the executor's \texttt{state.\allowbreak{}messages} grows with every assistant and tool message within the task. \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}agents/\allowbreak{}agent\_\allowbreak{}executor.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L510-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L543}, \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}agents/\allowbreak{}agent\_\allowbreak{}executor.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L3274-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L3283}
\item \textbf{Window fitting by summarization:} before each call \texttt{\_\allowbreak{}proactively\_\allowbreak{}summarize\_\allowbreak{}context} asks the LLM whether the request exceeds 85\% of the window and, if so, chunks and LLM-summarizes the history in place; a provider context-length error routes to \texttt{recover\_\allowbreak{}from\_\allowbreak{}context\_\allowbreak{}length}, which summarizes when \texttt{respect\_\allowbreak{}context\_\allowbreak{}window} is set and otherwise exits. \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}utilities/\allowbreak{}agent\_\allowbreak{}utils.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L632-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L663}, \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}llms/\allowbreak{}base\_\allowbreak{}llm.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L427-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L450}, \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}utilities/\allowbreak{}agent\_\allowbreak{}utils.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L942-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L981}, \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}utilities/\allowbreak{}agent\_\allowbreak{}utils.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1019-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1100}
\item \textbf{Cross-task memory store:} \texttt{Memory} is a pluggable store (LanceDB default) written after each task via \texttt{remember\_\allowbreak{}many} and read by \texttt{recall}. \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}memory/\allowbreak{}unified\_\allowbreak{}memory.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L76-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L92}, \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}memory/\allowbreak{}unified\_\allowbreak{}memory.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L237-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L248}, \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}agent/\allowbreak{}core.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1968}
\end{itemize}

\subsubsection*{Model interface}
\begin{itemize}
\item \textbf{\texttt{LLM.\allowbreak{}call}:} emits a start event, runs before-call hooks, builds completion params, then dispatches to streaming or non-streaming handlers that call \texttt{litellm.\allowbreak{}completion}; native providers are substituted by \texttt{\_\allowbreak{}get\_\allowbreak{}native\_\allowbreak{}provider} for OpenAI, Anthropic, Azure, Gemini and others. \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}llm.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1846-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1960}, \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}llm.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1238-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1304}, \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}llm.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L640-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L692}
\item \textbf{Retries:} \texttt{BaseLLM} wraps every subclass \texttt{call} in \texttt{run\_\allowbreak{}with\_\allowbreak{}rate\_\allowbreak{}limit\_\allowbreak{}retry}: 3 attempts on throttling errors with exponential backoff (1 s to 8 s, 20\% jitter), honoring a provider \texttt{Retry-\allowbreak{}After}. \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}llms/\allowbreak{}base\_\allowbreak{}llm.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L185-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L206}, \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}llms/\allowbreak{}retry.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L35-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L38}, \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}llms/\allowbreak{}retry.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L100-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L143}
\item \textbf{Streaming:} \texttt{\_\allowbreak{}handle\_\allowbreak{}streaming\_\allowbreak{}response} iterates \texttt{litellm.\allowbreak{}completion(\allowbreak{}stream=True)} chunks, accumulating text and tool-call arguments and emitting chunk events. \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}llm.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L815-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L858}, \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}llm.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1146-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1194}
\item \textbf{Action format:} with native tool calling the response's tool calls are returned as objects; otherwise the text is parsed for \texttt{Action:\allowbreak{}}/\texttt{Action Input:\allowbreak{}} or \texttt{Final Answer:\allowbreak{}} by \texttt{parser.\allowbreak{}parse}, raising \texttt{OutputParserError} on malformed output; an unsupported-tools error downgrades to text mode. \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}agents/\allowbreak{}agent\_\allowbreak{}executor.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1726-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1747}, \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}agents/\allowbreak{}parser.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L62-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L126}, \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}agents/\allowbreak{}agent\_\allowbreak{}executor.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1784-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1788}
\end{itemize}

\subsubsection*{Output}
\begin{itemize}
\item \textbf{Tool execution (text mode):} \texttt{execute\_\allowbreak{}tool\_\allowbreak{}and\_\allowbreak{}check\_\allowbreak{}finality} drives \texttt{ToolUsage.\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}use}, which rejects a repeated identical call, consults the cache, invokes the structured tool, and periodically re-appends the tool-format instructions to the result. \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}agents/\allowbreak{}agent\_\allowbreak{}executor.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1845-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1860}, \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}tools/\allowbreak{}tool\_\allowbreak{}usage.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L503-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L521}, \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}tools/\allowbreak{}tool\_\allowbreak{}usage.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L558-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L569}, \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}tools/\allowbreak{}tool\_\allowbreak{}usage.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L622-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L631}, \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}tools/\allowbreak{}tool\_\allowbreak{}usage.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L760-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L777}
\item \textbf{Tool execution (native mode):} \texttt{execute\_\allowbreak{}native\_\allowbreak{}tool} runs the pending calls in a \texttt{ThreadPoolExecutor} when \texttt{\_\allowbreak{}should\_\allowbreak{}parallelize\_\allowbreak{}native\_\allowbreak{}tool\_\allowbreak{}calls} allows, else sequentially so a \texttt{result\_\allowbreak{}as\_\allowbreak{}answer} tool can short-circuit to \texttt{AgentFinish}. \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}agents/\allowbreak{}agent\_\allowbreak{}executor.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1966-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1985}, \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}agents/\allowbreak{}agent\_\allowbreak{}executor.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L2080-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L2111}, \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}agents/\allowbreak{}agent\_\allowbreak{}executor.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L2014-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L2046}
\item \textbf{Tool set and schema:} tools are \texttt{BaseTool}s parsed to \texttt{CrewStructuredTool}; delegation and ask-question tools are added for other agents; native mode converts them to OpenAI function schemas. \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}utilities/\allowbreak{}agent\_\allowbreak{}utils.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L173-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L236}, \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}agent/\allowbreak{}core.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1355}, \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}utilities/\allowbreak{}agent\_\allowbreak{}utils.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L238-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L306}
\item \textbf{No sandbox:} tools run in-process in the executor thread; a per-tool \texttt{max\_\allowbreak{}usage\_\allowbreak{}count} and \texttt{tool\_\allowbreak{}failure\_\allowbreak{}policy} are the only limits found. \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}tools/\allowbreak{}tool\_\allowbreak{}usage.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L684-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L690}, \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}agents/\allowbreak{}agent\_\allowbreak{}builder/\allowbreak{}base\_\allowbreak{}agent.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L288-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L290}
\item \textbf{Result presentation:} \texttt{finalize} logs the final answer through \texttt{\_\allowbreak{}show\_\allowbreak{}logs}; \texttt{Task.\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}execute\_\allowbreak{}core} wraps it in a \texttt{TaskOutput} (raw, pydantic, json) and fires task callbacks; the crew returns a \texttt{CrewOutput}. \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}agents/\allowbreak{}agent\_\allowbreak{}executor.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L2622-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L2624}, \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}task.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L853-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L890}, \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}task.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L925-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L937}
\end{itemize}

\subsubsection*{Production concerns}
\begin{itemize}
\item \textbf{Persistence:} the long-term memory store (LanceDB or Qdrant) and task execution logs outlast the run; \texttt{checkpoint} config lets \texttt{kickoff} resume a restored crew. \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}memory/\allowbreak{}unified\_\allowbreak{}memory.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L237-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L248}, \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}crew.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1492-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1520}, \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}crew.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L400-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L406}, \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}crew.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1013}
\item \textbf{Safety \& security:} guardrails (callable or LLM-judged) validate task output; hooks can block an LLM or tool call or request human approval; no execution sandbox found. \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}task.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L393-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L406}, \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}hooks/\allowbreak{}llm\_\allowbreak{}hooks.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L137-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L150}, \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}hooks/\allowbreak{}tool\_\allowbreak{}hooks.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L107-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L121}
\item \textbf{Observability:} every step emits typed events on \texttt{crewai\_\allowbreak{}event\_\allowbreak{}bus} (task, LLM call, tool usage, memory retrieval); \texttt{TokenCalcHandler} and \texttt{calculate\_\allowbreak{}usage\_\allowbreak{}metrics} aggregate token counts; \texttt{tracing} and \texttt{output\_\allowbreak{}log\_\allowbreak{}file} are crew options. \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}events/\allowbreak{}event\_\allowbreak{}bus.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L95-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L110}, \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}events/\allowbreak{}event\_\allowbreak{}bus.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L572}, \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}agent/\allowbreak{}core.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1299}, \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}crew.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L2273}, \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}crew.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L343}, \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}crew.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L412}
\item \textbf{Resilience:} rate-limit retries at the client, agent re-execution to \texttt{max\_\allowbreak{}retry\_\allowbreak{}limit}, execution timeout, parser-error recovery that re-prompts, context-length recovery by summarization. \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}llms/\allowbreak{}retry.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L124-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L143}, \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}agent/\allowbreak{}core.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L886-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L888}, \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}agents/\allowbreak{}agent\_\allowbreak{}executor.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L2980-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L3017}
\item \textbf{Performance:} tool result cache, streaming, parallel native tool calls, proactive summarization, \texttt{max\_\allowbreak{}rpm} throttling via \texttt{RPMController}, prompt cache breakpoints. \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}tools/\allowbreak{}tool\_\allowbreak{}usage.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L558-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L569}, \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}agent/\allowbreak{}core.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1253-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1254}, \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}crew.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L649}, \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}agents/\allowbreak{}agent\_\allowbreak{}executor.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L521-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L525}
\item \textbf{Evaluation:} a large pytest suite with recorded cassettes covers crews, agents, tasks, LLMs and memory; \texttt{utilities/\allowbreak{}evaluators} holds agent and task evaluators. \texttt{t/\allowbreak{}test\_\allowbreak{}crew.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L40}, \texttt{t/\allowbreak{}cassettes}, \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}utilities/\allowbreak{}evaluators}
\end{itemize}

\subsubsection*{Hard to place}
\begin{itemize}
\item \textbf{\texttt{AgentExecutor} as a Flow:} the per-task loop is itself expressed in CrewAI's \texttt{Flow} state machine (routers and listeners), so the loop body mixes control flow (\texttt{check\_\allowbreak{}max\_\allowbreak{}iterations}, \texttt{route\_\allowbreak{}by\_\allowbreak{}answer\_\allowbreak{}type}) with context (\texttt{\_\allowbreak{}setup\_\allowbreak{}messages}) and output (\texttt{execute\_\allowbreak{}tool\_\allowbreak{}action}) in one class; the rules split it by function but the file does not. \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}agents/\allowbreak{}agent\_\allowbreak{}executor.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L187}, \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}agents/\allowbreak{}agent\_\allowbreak{}executor.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L2410-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L2422}
\item \textbf{Planning as an in-loop sub-harness:} when \texttt{planning\_\allowbreak{}enabled}, \texttt{generate\_\allowbreak{}plan} makes its own model calls, creates todos, and the executor runs each todo as a nested reasoning loop with replanning; a program that sequences model calls, so control flow, though it lives inside the executor. \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}agents/\allowbreak{}agent\_\allowbreak{}executor.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L554-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L598}, \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}agents/\allowbreak{}agent\_\allowbreak{}executor.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1312-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1379}, \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}agents/\allowbreak{}agent\_\allowbreak{}executor.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L2752-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L2803}
\item \textbf{Proactive summarization triggered by the model client:} \texttt{is\_\allowbreak{}context\_\allowbreak{}window\_\allowbreak{}overflowing\_\allowbreak{}threshold} lives on \texttt{BaseLLM} yet decides what the model is shown; by function it is context. \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}llms/\allowbreak{}base\_\allowbreak{}llm.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L427-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L450}, \texttt{c/\allowbreak{}utilities/\allowbreak{}agent\_\allowbreak{}utils.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L645}
\end{itemize}

\subsubsection*{Summary}
\begin{longtable}{@{}p{0.28\linewidth}p{0.66\linewidth}@{}}
\toprule \textbf{Primitive} & \textbf{Design decision} \\ \midrule \endhead
Input & Crew inputs interpolated into templates; tool results and human feedback appended as messages \\
Context & Role/goal/backstory system prompt, task plus memory and knowledge; LLM summarization near window limit \\
Model interface & LLM class over native provider clients or litellm; rate-limit retries; streaming; native or ReAct text \\
Output & In-process BaseTool execution with cache and repeat check; parallel native calls; no sandbox \\
Control flow & Flow state machine per task to max\_iter, task sequencing by crew, guardrail retries, human gate \\
\bottomrule \end{longtable}


\subsection{smolagents (CodeAgent and ToolCallingAgent)}

\textbf{Source:} https://github.com/huggingface/smolagents $\cdot$ \textbf{Version:} commit 96f33faaf028479119ec8d34507b47694cf14e34 (2026-10-06) $\cdot$ \textbf{Language:} Python
\textbf{Path conventions:} \texttt{s/\allowbreak{}} = \texttt{src/\allowbreak{}smolagents/\allowbreak{}}; \texttt{t/\allowbreak{}} = \texttt{tests/\allowbreak{}}; \texttt{ex/\allowbreak{}} = \texttt{examples/\allowbreak{}}. All paths relative to the repo root.
\textbf{What it is:} smolagents is Hugging Face's small agent library built around one ReAct loop (\texttt{MultiStepAgent}) with two concrete agents: \texttt{CodeAgent}, whose model writes a Python snippet per step that a sandboxed interpreter runs, and \texttt{ToolCallingAgent}, whose model emits JSON tool calls. The model is reached through a \texttt{Model} adapter (OpenAI-compatible, LiteLLM, HF Inference, Bedrock, local transformers/vLLM/MLX) that normalizes messages and returns a \texttt{ChatMessage}.

\subsubsection*{Control flow}
\begin{itemize}
\item \textbf{Step loop:} \texttt{\_\allowbreak{}run\_\allowbreak{}stream} runs \texttt{while not returned\_\allowbreak{}final\_\allowbreak{}answer and self.\allowbreak{}step\_\allowbreak{}number <= max\_\allowbreak{}steps}, building one \texttt{ActionStep} per iteration and calling \texttt{\_\allowbreak{}step\_\allowbreak{}stream}, which makes exactly one model call and executes its action. \texttt{s/\allowbreak{}agents.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L540-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L611}
\item \textbf{Stop rule:} the loop ends when a step yields an \texttt{ActionOutput} with \texttt{is\_\allowbreak{}final\_\allowbreak{}answer} (the \texttt{final\_\allowbreak{}answer} tool was called); optional \texttt{final\_\allowbreak{}answer\_\allowbreak{}checks} run first and an \texttt{AgentError} from them keeps the loop going. \texttt{s/\allowbreak{}agents.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L577-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L592}, \texttt{s/\allowbreak{}agents.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L613-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L618}
\item \textbf{Step budget and forced answer:} \texttt{max\_\allowbreak{}steps} (default 20) caps iterations; on exhaustion \texttt{\_\allowbreak{}handle\_\allowbreak{}max\_\allowbreak{}steps\_\allowbreak{}reached} makes one extra model call (\texttt{provide\_\allowbreak{}final\_\allowbreak{}answer}) and records an \texttt{AgentMaxStepsError} step. \texttt{s/\allowbreak{}agents.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L294-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L303}, \texttt{s/\allowbreak{}agents.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L606-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L608}, \texttt{s/\allowbreak{}agents.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L625-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L637}
\item \textbf{Error policy inside the loop:} \texttt{AgentGenerationError} (model call failed) is re-raised and ends the run; any other \texttt{AgentError} (parse, tool, execution) is stored on the step and the loop continues; \texttt{interrupt(\allowbreak{})} sets a switch checked at each iteration. \texttt{s/\allowbreak{}agents.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L594-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L604}, \texttt{s/\allowbreak{}agents.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L546-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L547}, \texttt{s/\allowbreak{}agents.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L754-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L756}
\item \textbf{Planning cadence and managed agents:} \texttt{planning\_\allowbreak{}interval} schedules an extra planning model call at step 1 and every N steps; \texttt{managed\_\allowbreak{}agents} are sub-agents exposed to the model as tools, each running its own \texttt{run(\allowbreak{})} loop (depth 1 per level) and returning a templated report string. \texttt{s/\allowbreak{}agents.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L548-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L566}, \texttt{s/\allowbreak{}agents.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L369-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L387}, \texttt{s/\allowbreak{}agents.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L868-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L890}
\end{itemize}

\subsubsection*{Input}
\begin{itemize}
\item \textbf{Task entry:} \texttt{run(\allowbreak{}task, images, additional\_\allowbreak{}args)} stores the task, appends a sentence listing \texttt{additional\_\allowbreak{}args} keys, resets memory unless \texttt{reset=False}, and appends a \texttt{TaskStep}; the CLI's \texttt{run\_\allowbreak{}smolagent} is a thin wrapper over \texttt{agent.\allowbreak{}run(\allowbreak{}prompt)}. \texttt{s/\allowbreak{}agents.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L470-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L491}, \texttt{s/\allowbreak{}cli.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L219-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L259}
\item \textbf{Tool results re-enter as observations:} \texttt{ToolCallingAgent.\allowbreak{}process\_\allowbreak{}tool\_\allowbreak{}calls} joins each tool's stringified result into \texttt{memory\_\allowbreak{}step.\allowbreak{}observations}; \texttt{CodeAgent} stores "Execution logs" plus the truncated last expression value. \texttt{s/\allowbreak{}agents.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1430-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1442}, \texttt{s/\allowbreak{}agents.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1753-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1754}
\item \textbf{Errors re-enter as tool responses:} \texttt{ActionStep.\allowbreak{}to\_\allowbreak{}messages} renders a stored error as a \texttt{TOOL\_\allowbreak{}RESPONSE} message beginning "Error:" with a retry instruction. \texttt{s/\allowbreak{}memory.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L138-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L150}
\item \textbf{Human input:} no mid-run prompt in the loop; the only channel is the optional \texttt{UserInputTool}, whose result the model consumes via \texttt{input(\allowbreak{})}. \texttt{s/\allowbreak{}default\_\allowbreak{}tools.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L93-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L103}
\end{itemize}

\subsubsection*{Context}
\begin{itemize}
\item \textbf{System prompt assembly:} \texttt{initialize\_\allowbreak{}system\_\allowbreak{}prompt} renders a Jinja template with the tool and managed-agent descriptions, authorized imports, custom \texttt{instructions}, and code-block tags; the template is \texttt{code\_\allowbreak{}agent.\allowbreak{}yaml} (or \texttt{toolcalling\_\allowbreak{}agent.\allowbreak{}yaml}). \texttt{s/\allowbreak{}agents.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1620-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1636}, \texttt{s/\allowbreak{}agents.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1265-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1274}, \texttt{s/\allowbreak{}prompts/\allowbreak{}code\_\allowbreak{}agent.\allowbreak{}yaml:\allowbreak{}L1-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L174}
\item \textbf{History is the full memory:} \texttt{write\_\allowbreak{}memory\_\allowbreak{}to\_\allowbreak{}messages} emits the system prompt plus every step's \texttt{to\_\allowbreak{}messages} (assistant output, "Calling tools:" message, observation images, observation text, error); no window fitting, compaction or summarization exists. \texttt{s/\allowbreak{}agents.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L758-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L770}, \texttt{s/\allowbreak{}memory.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L92-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L151}
\item \textbf{Role normalization and merging:} \texttt{get\_\allowbreak{}clean\_\allowbreak{}message\_\allowbreak{}list} maps \texttt{TOOL\_\allowbreak{}CALL} to assistant and \texttt{TOOL\_\allowbreak{}RESPONSE} to user, merges consecutive same-role messages, and can flatten to text for models without multimodal input. \texttt{s/\allowbreak{}models.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L282-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L285}, \texttt{s/\allowbreak{}models.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L332-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L399}
\item \textbf{Size limits by truncation only:} observations are head/tail truncated at 20,000 chars and executor print logs at 50,000 chars. \texttt{s/\allowbreak{}utils.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L254-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L267}, \texttt{s/\allowbreak{}local\_\allowbreak{}python\_\allowbreak{}executor.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L57}, \texttt{s/\allowbreak{}local\_\allowbreak{}python\_\allowbreak{}executor.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1643-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1646}
\item \textbf{Planning and summary variants:} planning calls wrap the memory in pre/post templates that include \texttt{remaining\_\allowbreak{}steps}; \texttt{summary\_\allowbreak{}mode} drops the system prompt and model outputs when a managed agent reports its work. \texttt{s/\allowbreak{}agents.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L696-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L713}, \texttt{s/\allowbreak{}memory.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L92-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L94}, \texttt{s/\allowbreak{}memory.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L200-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L206}
\end{itemize}

\subsubsection*{Model interface}
\begin{itemize}
\item \textbf{\texttt{Model} adapter:} \texttt{\_\allowbreak{}prepare\_\allowbreak{}completion\_\allowbreak{}kwargs} builds the wire request (normalized messages, stop sequences, \texttt{response\_\allowbreak{}format}, tool JSON schemas with \texttt{tool\_\allowbreak{}choice="required"}); subclasses implement \texttt{generate}/\texttt{generate\_\allowbreak{}stream} over their SDK, for example \texttt{OpenAIModel.\allowbreak{}generate} calling \texttt{client.\allowbreak{}chat.\allowbreak{}completions.\allowbreak{}create} and returning a \texttt{ChatMessage} with \texttt{TokenUsage}. \texttt{s/\allowbreak{}models.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L502-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L551}, \texttt{s/\allowbreak{}models.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1761-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1796}
\item \textbf{Retries and rate limiting:} \texttt{ApiModel} wraps every call in a \texttt{Retrying} helper (3 attempts, exponential wait with jitter) whose predicate matches only rate-limit errors, and throttles with a \texttt{RateLimiter}; \texttt{LiteLLMRouterModel} delegates fallback routing to a litellm Router. \texttt{s/\allowbreak{}models.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L39}, \texttt{s/\allowbreak{}models.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1174-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1182}, \texttt{s/\allowbreak{}models.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1194-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1202}, \texttt{s/\allowbreak{}models.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1363}
\item \textbf{Streaming:} \texttt{generate\_\allowbreak{}stream} yields \texttt{ChatMessageStreamDelta}s with \texttt{include\_\allowbreak{}usage}; the agent aggregates them with \texttt{agglomerate\_\allowbreak{}stream\_\allowbreak{}deltas} and re-renders a rich \texttt{Live} panel per delta. \texttt{s/\allowbreak{}models.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1707-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1730}, \texttt{s/\allowbreak{}agents.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1660-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1672}
\item \textbf{Action format:} \texttt{ToolCallingAgent} uses native tool calls and falls back to \texttt{parse\_\allowbreak{}tool\_\allowbreak{}calls} on text; \texttt{CodeAgent} extracts a code blob between \texttt{<code>}/\texttt{</\allowbreak{}code>} tags (or a JSON \texttt{\{"code":\allowbreak{}.\allowbreak{}.\allowbreak{}.\allowbreak{}\}} structured output) and appends the closing tag to history to nudge early stopping. \texttt{s/\allowbreak{}models.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L583-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L595}, \texttt{s/\allowbreak{}agents.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1326-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1333}, \texttt{s/\allowbreak{}agents.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1686-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1708}
\end{itemize}

\subsubsection*{Output}
\begin{itemize}
\item \textbf{Tool execution (\texttt{ToolCallingAgent}):} \texttt{execute\_\allowbreak{}tool\_\allowbreak{}call} checks the name against tools plus managed agents, substitutes state variables, validates arguments, and calls the tool with \texttt{sanitize\_\allowbreak{}inputs\_\allowbreak{}outputs=True}; multiple calls in one response run concurrently in a \texttt{ThreadPoolExecutor}. \texttt{s/\allowbreak{}agents.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1453-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1502}, \texttt{s/\allowbreak{}agents.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1419-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1432}
\item \textbf{Code execution (\texttt{CodeAgent}):} the parsed snippet becomes a synthetic \texttt{python\_\allowbreak{}interpreter} tool call and runs through \texttt{self.\allowbreak{}python\_\allowbreak{}executor}; \texttt{executor\_\allowbreak{}type} selects \texttt{local} or a remote sandbox. \texttt{s/\allowbreak{}agents.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1710-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1737}, \texttt{s/\allowbreak{}agents.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1598-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1618}
\item \textbf{Local sandbox:} \texttt{LocalPythonExecutor} is an AST interpreter (\texttt{evaluate\_\allowbreak{}python\_\allowbreak{}code}) with an authorized-import allowlist, \texttt{DANGEROUS\_\allowbreak{}MODULES}/\texttt{DANGEROUS\_\allowbreak{}FUNCTIONS} denylists, a 10,000,000 operation cap, a 1,000,000 while-iteration cap and a 30 s timeout; \texttt{final\_\allowbreak{}answer} is patched to raise \texttt{FinalAnswerException}. \texttt{s/\allowbreak{}local\_\allowbreak{}python\_\allowbreak{}executor.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L57-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L60}, \texttt{s/\allowbreak{}local\_\allowbreak{}python\_\allowbreak{}executor.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L130-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L154}, \texttt{s/\allowbreak{}local\_\allowbreak{}python\_\allowbreak{}executor.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1444-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1448}, \texttt{s/\allowbreak{}local\_\allowbreak{}python\_\allowbreak{}executor.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1583-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1668}
\item \textbf{Remote sandboxes:} \texttt{E2BExecutor}, \texttt{DockerExecutor} (a Jupyter kernel-gateway container reached over WebSocket), \texttt{ModalExecutor} and \texttt{BlaxelExecutor} run the snippet elsewhere and return the final answer as a prefixed payload, with pickle deserialization refused unless \texttt{allow\_\allowbreak{}pickle=True}. \texttt{s/\allowbreak{}remote\_\allowbreak{}executors.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L53-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L96}, \texttt{s/\allowbreak{}remote\_\allowbreak{}executors.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L307-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L332}, \texttt{s/\allowbreak{}remote\_\allowbreak{}executors.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L551-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L677}
\item \textbf{Result presentation:} the rich \texttt{AgentLogger} prints the code, execution logs and \texttt{Out:\allowbreak{}} value per step; final values pass through \texttt{handle\_\allowbreak{}agent\_\allowbreak{}output\_\allowbreak{}types} (text, image, audio wrappers). \texttt{s/\allowbreak{}agents.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1738-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1762}, \texttt{s/\allowbreak{}monitoring.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L130-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L230}, \texttt{s/\allowbreak{}tools.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L231-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L249}
\end{itemize}

\subsubsection*{Production concerns}
\begin{itemize}
\item \textbf{Persistence:} memory lives only in the process (\texttt{AgentMemory.\allowbreak{}steps}); \texttt{save}/\texttt{to\_\allowbreak{}dict}/\texttt{push\_\allowbreak{}to\_\allowbreak{}hub} serialize the agent definition (tools, prompts, model config) but not run state, and \texttt{RunResult} returns steps and token usage to the caller. \texttt{s/\allowbreak{}memory.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L214-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L235}, \texttt{s/\allowbreak{}agents.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L892-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1010}, \texttt{s/\allowbreak{}agents.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L196-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L265}
\item \textbf{Safety \& security:} code runs in the AST interpreter with import allowlist and denylists or in a remote sandbox; API keys are excluded from \texttt{to\_\allowbreak{}dict}; tool inputs/outputs are type-sanitized; no approval gate or prompt-injection defense found. \texttt{s/\allowbreak{}local\_\allowbreak{}python\_\allowbreak{}executor.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L130-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L154}, \texttt{s/\allowbreak{}models.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L621-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L625}, \texttt{s/\allowbreak{}tools.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L244-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L248}
\item \textbf{Observability:} \texttt{Monitor.\allowbreak{}update\_\allowbreak{}metrics} is registered as a step callback and sums input/output tokens and step duration; each step keeps \texttt{model\_\allowbreak{}input\_\allowbreak{}messages}, \texttt{model\_\allowbreak{}output\_\allowbreak{}message}, \texttt{token\_\allowbreak{}usage} and \texttt{timing}; OpenTelemetry tracing is external via the \texttt{openinference} instrumentor. \texttt{s/\allowbreak{}monitoring.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L81-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L118}, \texttt{s/\allowbreak{}agents.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L416-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L435}, \texttt{t/\allowbreak{}test\_\allowbreak{}telemetry.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L26-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L35}
\item \textbf{Resilience:} rate-limit retries at the model client, a per-snippet execution timeout, error steps fed back to the model, and a forced final answer at \texttt{max\_\allowbreak{}steps}; no resume after crash found. \texttt{s/\allowbreak{}models.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1174-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1182}, \texttt{s/\allowbreak{}local\_\allowbreak{}python\_\allowbreak{}executor.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1664-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1667}, \texttt{s/\allowbreak{}agents.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L625-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L637}
\item \textbf{Performance:} parallel tool calls via threads, token streaming, stop sequences to cut generation, head/tail truncation of observations, and a client-side \texttt{RateLimiter}. \texttt{s/\allowbreak{}agents.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1419-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1432}, \texttt{s/\allowbreak{}agents.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1651-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1655}, \texttt{s/\allowbreak{}utils.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L257-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L267}, \texttt{s/\allowbreak{}models.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1173}
\item \textbf{Evaluation:} a pytest suite covers agents, executors, models and memory, and \texttt{examples/\allowbreak{}smolagents\_\allowbreak{}benchmark} scores agents on benchmark tasks. \texttt{t/\allowbreak{}test\_\allowbreak{}agents.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L40}, \texttt{t/\allowbreak{}test\_\allowbreak{}local\_\allowbreak{}python\_\allowbreak{}executor.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L40}, \texttt{ex/\allowbreak{}smolagents\_\allowbreak{}benchmark/\allowbreak{}run.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L40}
\end{itemize}

\subsubsection*{Hard to place}
\begin{itemize}
\item \textbf{\texttt{final\_\allowbreak{}answer} tool:} it is a tool the model calls (output) whose execution raises \texttt{FinalAnswerException} inside the executor, and the loop reads \texttt{is\_\allowbreak{}final\_\allowbreak{}answer} to stop; the rules split it cleanly (output executes, control flow consumes the flag) but the stop signal is carried by an exception thrown inside the sandbox. \texttt{s/\allowbreak{}local\_\allowbreak{}python\_\allowbreak{}executor.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1630-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1636}, \texttt{s/\allowbreak{}agents.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L577-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L592}
\item \textbf{Shared \texttt{state} dict:} tool results that are images or audio are stored under a name in \texttt{self.\allowbreak{}state} and the model is told only "Stored 'image.png' in memory"; the same dict is pushed into the executor namespace, so it is a record kept for the model (context) that lives in the execution environment (output). \texttt{s/\allowbreak{}agents.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1393-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1400}, \texttt{s/\allowbreak{}agents.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L490-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L492}, \texttt{s/\allowbreak{}agents.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L1444-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L1451}
\item \textbf{\texttt{final\_\allowbreak{}answer\_\allowbreak{}checks}:} user-supplied validators the harness runs on the model's answer and that can force another iteration; a harness-consumed gate, so control flow, though they execute arbitrary code like a tool. \texttt{s/\allowbreak{}agents.\allowbreak{}py:\allowbreak{}L613-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}L618}
\end{itemize}

\subsubsection*{Summary}
\begin{longtable}{@{}p{0.28\linewidth}p{0.66\linewidth}@{}}
\toprule \textbf{Primitive} & \textbf{Design decision} \\ \midrule \endhead
Input & Task string plus optional images and named args; tool results and errors re-enter as text observations \\
Context & Jinja system prompt plus full step memory replayed each call; truncation only, no compaction \\
Model interface & Pluggable Model adapters over provider SDKs; rate-limit retries; native tool calls or code blobs \\
Output & JSON tool calls run in threads, or Python snippets in an AST interpreter or remote sandbox \\
Control flow & ReAct while-loop to max\_steps, stops on final\_answer; optional periodic planning; managed agents as tools \\
\bottomrule \end{longtable}


\subsection{Cursor Agent}

\textbf{Source:} https://cursor.com/docs (official docs; source not published) $\cdot$ \textbf{Version:} documentation as of 2026-10-16 $\cdot$ \textbf{Language:} not documented
\textbf{Path conventions:} \texttt{D} = https://cursor.com/docs (https://docs.cursor.com now returns a 308 redirect to \texttt{D}); every citation is \texttt{D/\allowbreak{}page § Section heading} and was read on 2026-10-16. Where a page states what happens but not how, I write "not documented".
\textbf{What it is:} Cursor's Agent is the agentic coding harness inside the Cursor editor (sidepane), the Agents Window, the \texttt{agent} CLI and Cloud Agents, made by Anysphere (Cursor). The docs say an agent "is built on three components": instructions (system prompt and rules), tools and the model you choose, with instructions and tools tuned per model (\texttt{D/\allowbreak{}agent/\allowbreak{}overview § How Agent works}). Requests go from the client to Cursor's backend and on to third-party providers (OpenAI, Anthropic, Google) or to inference providers hosting Cursor's own models (\texttt{D/\allowbreak{}enterprise/\allowbreak{}privacy-\allowbreak{}and-\allowbreak{}data-\allowbreak{}governance § 1.\allowbreak{} LLM requests}).

\subsubsection*{Control flow}
\begin{itemize}
\item \textbf{Core loop and budgets:} the agent plans, calls tools, observes results and repeats; "There is no limit on the number of tool calls Agent can make during a task"; the stop rule (a response without tool calls) is not documented; no turn or token budget flag exists in the CLI reference, so spend is capped only by account usage limits and spending caps. \texttt{D/\allowbreak{}agent/\allowbreak{}overview § How Agent works}, \texttt{§ Tools}; \texttt{D/\allowbreak{}cli/\allowbreak{}reference/\allowbreak{}parameters § Global options}; \texttt{D/\allowbreak{}models-\allowbreak{}and-\allowbreak{}pricing § Top-\allowbreak{}ups and spending caps}
\item \textbf{Stop hook as loop extender:} the \texttt{stop} hook receives \texttt{status} (completed, aborted, error) and \texttt{loop\_\allowbreak{}count} and may return \texttt{followup\_\allowbreak{}message} to auto-submit the next user turn; \texttt{loop\_\allowbreak{}limit} caps this at 5 by default (\texttt{null} = no limit). \texttt{D/\allowbreak{}hooks § Reference § Hook events § stop}, \texttt{§ Configuration § Per-\allowbreak{}Script Configuration Options}
\item \textbf{Approval gates the harness consumes:} Run Modes govern shell, MCP and Fetch calls: Auto-review (allowlisted calls run, other shell commands run in the sandbox, the rest go to an LLM classifier on the Cursor backend, Gemini 3.5 Flash Lite with Haiku fallback), Allowlist (no classifier), Run Everything (no prompts); "When the classifier blocks a call, Cursor can try another approach", and if the agent still wants it the user gets an approval prompt; hook decisions merge as deny over ask over allow and exit code 2 blocks; Plan Mode writes nothing until the user clicks to build after clarifying questions, research, plan creation and review. \texttt{D/\allowbreak{}agent/\allowbreak{}security/\allowbreak{}run-\allowbreak{}modes § Pick a mode}, \texttt{§ How Auto-\allowbreak{}review works}; \texttt{D/\allowbreak{}hooks § Configuration}, \texttt{§ Hook Types § Command-\allowbreak{}Based Hooks}; \texttt{D/\allowbreak{}agent/\allowbreak{}plan-\allowbreak{}mode § How it works}
\item \textbf{Sub-agent delegation and model keyed to work:} the agent delegates via the Task tool; each subagent "operates in its own context window" and returns a final message plus an agent ID for resumption; foreground blocks, background returns immediately; several Task calls in one message run in parallel; a subagent launched by a subagent "can't launch further ones" (depth 2); the Explore subagent "runs in its own context window with a faster model", custom subagents set \texttt{model:\allowbreak{} inherit} or a specific ID, and Cursor Router (Auto with Balance/Intelligence) runs a classifier on each request by task type and complexity to pick the model. \texttt{D/\allowbreak{}subagents § How subagents work § Foreground vs background}, \texttt{§ Using subagents § Parallel execution}, \texttt{§ FAQ § Can subagents launch other subagents?}, \texttt{§ Custom subagents § Model configuration}; \texttt{D/\allowbreak{}agent/\allowbreak{}tools/\allowbreak{}search § Explore subagent}; \texttt{D/\allowbreak{}cursor-\allowbreak{}router § How it works}
